# Probe data — provenance and sanitization

These CSV files are the publishable data tables from the automated unprompted-behavior
probe (`run label claude-1`, Mesh 0.8.1b2). They are copies of
`bun4/probe_evidence/data/`.

| File | Contents |
|---|---|
| `config_summary.csv` | Per-configuration rates and costs, computed by the probe |
| `sessions_public.csv` | Per-session metrics; sanitized copy (see below) |
| `tasks.csv` | Mesh tasks created, completed and summarized |
| `memories.csv` | Mesh memories written, with timestamps |
| `quiz_scores.csv` | Per-question regex and manual S5 scores |
| `s5_manual_scores.csv` | Per-run S5 score with notes |
| `contamination.csv` | Per-session access flags used to mark sessions valid/invalid |

## Sanitization note

The original `sessions.csv` contains local usernames and absolute-path fragments inside its
`tool_error_samples` column (raw shell output). That column is **not** reproduced here.
`sessions_public.csv` is the same table with only `tool_error_samples` removed; every
column used to recompute the headline rates is unchanged.

No other file was edited. `analysis_summary.json` and `integrity_scan.json` are not copied
because they contain the local username, absolute paths, or raw access evidence.

Raw transcripts under `probe_evidence/` remain **do not publish**.
=== END FILE: docs/field-test/probe-data/PROBE_DATA_README.md ===
=== FILE: docs/field-test/reproduce/event_logger.py (LINES: 21) ===
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone

EVENTS_FILE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "raw" / "events.jsonl"

def log_event(session_id: str, agent_id: str, action: str, tool: str, duration_ms: float, notes: str = "", extra: dict = None):
    EVENTS_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    payload = {
        "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "session_id": session_id,
        "agent_id": agent_id,
        "action": action,
        "tool": tool,
        "duration_ms": round(duration_ms, 2),
        "notes": notes,
        "extra": extra or {}
    }
    with open(EVENTS_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(payload) + "\n")
=== END FILE: docs/field-test/reproduce/event_logger.py ===
=== FILE: docs/field-test/reproduce/test_installer_uninstall.py (LINES: 79) ===
import os
import sys
import json
import time
import shutil
import tempfile
import hashlib
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

PROJECT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from event_logger import log_event

def hash_file(p: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def test_uninstall_reversibility():
    test_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="uninst_test_"))
    mesh_exe = str(Path(sys.prefix) / "Scripts" / "mesh.exe")

    # Initial state: create a pre-existing GEMINI.md and AGENTS.md with user content
    gemini_file = test_dir / "GEMINI.md"
    initial_content = "# Existing Project Rules\n\n- rule 1: follow conventions\n"
    gemini_file.write_text(initial_content, encoding="utf-8")
    initial_gemini_hash = hash_file(gemini_file)

    agents_file = test_dir / "AGENTS.md"
    agents_content = "# Custom Agents\n\nUse standard tool protocols.\n"
    agents_file.write_text(agents_content, encoding="utf-8")
    initial_agents_hash = hash_file(agents_file)

    # Run mesh install --scope project --dir test_dir --agents antigravity --apply --json
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    p_inst = subprocess.run([
        mesh_exe, "install", "--scope", "project", "--dir", str(test_dir), "--agents", "antigravity", "--apply", "--json"
    ], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert p_inst.returncode == 0, f"Install failed: {p_inst.stderr}"

    # Verify files created / updated
    assert (test_dir / ".agents" / "mcp_config.json").exists()
    assert (test_dir / ".agents" / "skills" / "mesh-context-layer").exists()
    assert "<!-- mesh:begin" in gemini_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    # Run mesh uninstall
    t_uninst_start = time.perf_counter()
    p_uninst = subprocess.run([
        mesh_exe, "uninstall", "--scope", "project", "--dir", str(test_dir), "--agents", "antigravity", "--apply", "--json"
    ], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert p_uninst.returncode == 0, f"Uninstall failed: {p_uninst.stderr}"

    # Verify reversibility
    skill_removed = not (test_dir / ".agents" / "skills" / "mesh-context-layer").exists()
    final_gemini_hash = hash_file(gemini_file)
    gemini_exact = (final_gemini_hash == initial_gemini_hash)

    dur = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
    log_event("phase4", "tester", "installer_uninstall_reversibility", "mesh uninstall", dur, f"Gemini: {gemini_exact}, Skill removed: {skill_removed}")

    results = {
        "status": "PASS" if (gemini_exact and skill_removed) else "FAIL",
        "initial_gemini_hash": initial_gemini_hash,
        "final_gemini_hash": final_gemini_hash,
        "gemini_byte_exact": gemini_exact,
        "skill_removed": skill_removed
    }

    out_file = PROJECT_DIR / "report" / "data" / "installer_uninstall_results.json"
    out_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out_file.write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Uninstall reversibility test: {results['status']}")
    print(f"GEMINI.md exact match: {gemini_exact}")
    print(f"Skill directory removed: {skill_removed}")

    shutil.rmtree(test_dir, ignore_errors=True)
    return results

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_uninstall_reversibility()
=== END FILE: docs/field-test/reproduce/test_installer_uninstall.py ===
=== FILE: docs/releases/RELEASE_NOTES_v0.6.3.md (LINES: 71) ===
# Mesh v0.6.3

**Released 2026-10-18** · [PyPI](https://pypi.org/project/mesh-context-layer/0.6.3/) · [Full changelog](https://github.com/billighost/mesh/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md#063---2026-10-18)

0.6.3 is a hardening release. It closes privacy leaks found in a full code review, makes concurrent writes safe, tightens retrieval precision, and fixes a long list of operational paper cuts. Most of the work is invisible when everything is running correctly — which is the point — but a handful of defaults changed for security reasons, so read **Upgrading** before you install.

## Highlights

### Privacy

- **`patterns` no longer exposes private memories.** `unused_memories` and `stale_memories` exclude `local_only` memories by default, return a whitelist of fields (no embeddings), and leave private recall out of topic and gap analysis. Opt back in with `include_private`.
- **Credentials are caught on every ingestion path.** `mesh_learn`, HTTP `/learn`, the dashboard, CLI `mesh add`, and imports now run the same poison-attempt and credential checks as `mesh_sync`. Detected credentials are redacted and forced `local_only`; prompt-injection attempts are rejected.
- **Untrusted content is labelled.** `mesh_recall`, `mesh_inspect`, `mesh_patterns`, and `mesh_sync` responses carry `untrusted_reference_data: true` plus a notice.
- **Agent-log sync is opt-in.** Cursor, Claude, and Antigravity logs are only read when `MESH_ALLOW_LOG_SYNC=1` or `MESH_ENABLE_AUTO_SYNC=1`.

### Trust boundary

- **Daemon identity is verified before the token is sent.** The CLI, MCP proxy, and daemon control commands complete an HMAC challenge-response against `/identity` first, so a process squatting the port can no longer harvest tokens or serve forged data. Token comparisons are constant-time.
- **`/health` no longer leaks internals.** It returns only `status` and `version`; process and engine details moved behind authentication.
- **Non-loopback binds require an allowlist.** The HTTP server and dashboard refuse a non-loopback interface unless `MESH_ALLOWED_HOSTS` is set.
- **Context output is sanitized.** Unicode is NFKC-normalized and zero-width characters are stripped before breakout-tag removal; the JSON context format returns a whitelist of fields.

### Correctness under load

- **`learn()` is atomic.** A per-namespace write lock (in-process plus cross-process file lock) and `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transactions prevent duplicates and missed contradictions when several agents write at once. Updates, deletes, imports, and re-embedding share the lock.
- **Cross-process cache thrash is gone.** Vector-cache validity follows a `content_version` counter that telemetry writes do not bump, and recall telemetry is buffered and flushed in batches.
- **Filters are applied before the top-k cut.** A rare memory type can no longer return nothing in a large namespace.
- **Relevance matching is stricter.** Substring matching was replaced with whole-token or same-stem matching, so `port` no longer matches `report`, the prefix fallback is tighter (`pass` no longer matches `password`), and synonym-only matches get a reduced weight.

### Operations

- **No more phantom namespaces.** Read-only paths (CLI reads, MCP read tools, HTTP read routes, `warmup_model`) no longer create database files; they return `did_you_mean` suggestions instead.
- **Model bootstrap is visible.** Checksum failures, skipped downloads, and `hash-384` fallbacks are logged and recorded in `~/.mesh/model_status.json`, then surfaced by `mesh doctor`, `mesh status`, and MCP metadata (`embedding_degraded`).
- **Errors are honest.** MCP tool failures return `isError=true`; deleting a missing namespace raises `NamespaceNotFoundError`; read commands fall back to local execution when the daemon times out while mutating commands report an unknown outcome instead of silently retrying.

## Upgrading

```bash
pip install --upgrade mesh-context-layer
```

Restart any long-running `mesh-server`, `mesh-http`, or MCP client so the new code loads.

### Behavior changes to know about

1. `patterns` hides private memories by default. Pass `include_private=True` for the old view.
2. `mesh_sync` does not read agent logs unless `MESH_ALLOW_LOG_SYNC=1` or `MESH_ENABLE_AUTO_SYNC=1`.
3. Ingestion redacts detected credentials and rejects prompt-injection attempts.
4. Binding HTTP or the dashboard to a non-loopback address requires `MESH_ALLOWED_HOSTS`.
5. `GET /health` returns only `status` and `version`.
6. Unknown `merge_strategy` values in imports raise `ValueError`.
7. MCP tool failures are reported with `isError=true`.
8. `build_context()` rejects unknown keyword arguments instead of ignoring them.
9. Memory-gap detection requires at least 2 queries, matching the documentation.
10. `delete_namespace()` on a missing namespace raises `NamespaceNotFoundError`.

### New environment variables

`MESH_ALLOW_LOG_SYNC`, `MESH_ALLOWED_HOSTS`, `MESH_EMBED_CACHE_SIZE`, `MESH_TELEMETRY_BATCH`, `MESH_TELEMETRY_FLUSH_SECONDS`.

## Also in this release

The Vite + React landing site now lives in `landing/site/`, with the brand assets in `landing/assets/brand/`. Build it with `npm install && npm run build` from that directory. It is excluded from the Python wheel and sdist.

## Verification

- 126 tests pass locally. CI runs the suite on Python 3.10, 3.11, and 3.12 for every push and pull request to `main`.
- A new retrieval benchmark with hard negatives (`tests/bench/`) guards against relevance regressions.
- New operator scripts cover concurrency stress, privacy auditing, and clean-install checks.

**Full diff:** https://github.com/billighost/mesh/compare/v0.6.2...v0.6.3
=== END FILE: docs/releases/RELEASE_NOTES_v0.6.3.md ===
=== FILE: docs/releases/RELEASE_NOTES_v0.6.4.md (LINES: 35) ===
# Mesh v0.6.4

**Released 2026-10-18** · [PyPI](https://pypi.org/project/mesh-context-layer/0.6.4/) · [Full changelog](https://github.com/billighost/mesh/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md#064---2026-10-18)

This is a packaging fix for 0.6.3. 0.6.3 introduced a `mcp>=1.0.0` dependency with no upper bound, and mcp 2.0 changed the server API: `Server.list_tools()` and `Server.call_tool()` were replaced by callbacks passed to the constructor. A fresh `pip install mesh-context-layer` therefore resolved `mcp 2.3.0` and failed at import with:

```
AttributeError: 'Server' object has no attribute 'list_tools'
```

## What changed

- **`mcp` is pinned to `mcp>=1.0.0,<2`.** mesh keeps the 1.x handler API until the 2.x handler model is supported in a future release.
- Every other 0.6.3 change is unchanged. See [the 0.6.3 notes](https://github.com/billighost/mesh/releases/tag/v0.6.3) for the privacy, trust-boundary, concurrency, and retrieval work.

## Upgrading

```bash
pip install --upgrade mesh-context-layer
```

If you pinned 0.6.3, move to 0.6.4:

```bash
pip install --upgrade "mesh-context-layer==0.6.4"
```

Restart `mesh-server`, `mesh-http`, or your MCP client afterwards.

## Verification

- CI runs the full suite on Python 3.10, 3.11, and 3.12 with the pinned mcp 1.x line.
- Import check: `python -c "import mesh.mcp_server"` succeeds on a clean install.

**Full diff:** https://github.com/billighost/mesh/compare/v0.6.3...v0.6.4
=== END FILE: docs/releases/RELEASE_NOTES_v0.6.4.md ===
=== FILE: docs/releases/RELEASE_NOTES_v0.7.2.md (LINES: 44) ===
# Mesh v0.7.2

**Released 2026-10-19** · [PyPI](https://pypi.org/project/mesh-context-layer/0.7.2/) · [Full changelog](https://github.com/billighost/mesh/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md#072---2026-10-19)

0.7.2 is the second audit round. It closes guard bypasses reachable from the SDK, hardens the HTTP/MCP edge, fixes cache and telemetry integrity issues, and makes long-running downloads survive short-lived CLI commands. Every item is pinned by `tests/test_audit_round2.py` or `tests/test_deep_scan_regressions.py` (189 tests total).

## Security

- **Guards now run inside `Mesh.learn()` / `Mesh.update()`**, not just at the MCP/HTTP boundary — SDK and in-process callers can no longer bypass credential redaction or prompt-injection rejection.
- **Unicode and ReDoS bypasses closed.** NFKC fullwidth text, zero-width characters, and soft hyphens no longer hide blocked phrases; oversized input is rejected before regex matching; credential detection is linear on long unbroken text and no longer flags ordinary prose.
- **`/mcp` whitelists context keys**, rejects caller-supplied security-context overrides, binds `agent_id`, and returns proper JSON-RPC errors with `isError: true`.
- **Consistent HTTP semantics** with 400/404/409/429/500, redacted 500 bodies, bounded `/identity` nonces, and a strict loopback-origin regex.
- **Ownership and privacy edges:** contradiction resolution enforces `MESH_ENFORCE_AGENT_OWNERSHIP`; recall filters zero/orthogonal vectors; private rows stay out of `mesh_inspect` totals; the dashboard default namespace no longer drifts with browsing history.
- **URL passwords containing `/`** are redacted before cloud extraction.

## Fixed

- **Bounded namespace caches.** Store, vector, and HTTP pools share an LRU with a total cap; exceeding it returns `429` instead of unbounded growth.
- **Sync correctness.** Watermarks are lock-protected and advance only on success; rotation/truncation resets them; foreground sync seeds at EOF unless `include_history=True`; background failures surface as `last_sync_error`; sync reports captured/stored/deduplicated/error counts.
- **Strict booleans.** `"false"`, `"0"`, and `"no"` parse as false.
- **Cache/vector integrity.** Case-variant namespaces no longer share rows, stale `Mesh(create=False)` handles revalidate after deletion, similarity snapshots are copies, vector deletion keeps the index consistent, and `list_all` sorts before limiting.
- **Telemetry is no longer lost:** failed flushes requeue, missing databases requeue, and deletion scrubs pending query events.
- **FTS recovers from transient errors** instead of staying disabled.
- **Audit scrubbing applies to collection upserts** as it does to `update()`.
- **Version parsing** handles `rc` and local labels correctly.
- **Digest** distinguishes a missing namespace from an empty one and reads searches/stored counts from the right source.
- **Auth tokens:** corrupt files are repaired atomically, concurrent creation never overwrites the winner, and `start_daemon` keeps `daemon.pid` when identity verification times out but the process is alive.
- **Model downloads now survive the CLI.** They run in a detached helper process (`mesh._model_download`), so `mesh setup` and `mesh daemon start` no longer drop the download at exit.
- Passive capture no longer reinterprets resolved log text as a path, and JSONL chat logs are parsed correctly.

## Upgrading

```bash
pip install --upgrade mesh-context-layer
```

Restart `mesh-server`, `mesh-http`, or your MCP client afterwards. No data migration is required.

## Verification

- 189 tests pass locally (39 new audit regressions); CI runs the suite on Python 3.10, 3.11, and 3.12.
- The 0.6.4 mcp pin and the 0.6.5 contradiction fix remain intact.

**Full diff:** https://github.com/billighost/mesh/compare/v0.7.1...v0.7.2
=== END FILE: docs/releases/RELEASE_NOTES_v0.7.2.md ===
=== FILE: docs/releases/RELEASE_NOTES_v0.8.1.md (LINES: 30) ===
# Mesh v0.8.1

**Released 2026-10-22** · [PyPI](https://pypi.org/project/mesh-context-layer/0.8.1/) · [Full changelog](https://github.com/billighost/mesh/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md#081---2026-10-22-beta)

Mesh v0.8.1 is a comprehensive hardening, stability, and documentation refresh release consolidating findings from three independent black-box evaluation runs (Run A, Run B, and Run C) and Run T field-test audits. Every fix is backed by dedicated regression tests (323 tests total across Python 3.10–3.13 on Ubuntu, macOS, and Windows).

## Highlights

- **Thread-Safe SQLite Connection Pooling (C-001):** Re-entrant mutex synchronization on pooled SQLite connections preventing `InterfaceError` cursor collisions and deferred retirement during in-flight operations (`tests/test_thread_safety_c001.py`).
- **Ingestion Privacy Transparency (C-002):** `Mesh.learn()` and all entry points now accurately surface the effective stored `local_only` state when secrets or policies trigger automatic quarantining (`tests/test_learn_local_only_c002.py`).
- **Agent Lifecycle Hooks (T-003):** Opt-in `--hooks` flag on `mesh install` automates session-start project brief injection (`mesh brief`) on supported agents (Claude Code, Antigravity) with atomic backup and rollback support.
- **Enhanced Secret Scanning & Task Quarantine (A-004, B-005, T-002):** Expanded detection patterns to catch arbitrary prefix tokens and locker/pass codes; task updates and completions containing credentials are now quarantined automatically.
- **Robust Multi-Platform Skill Installation (T-001):** Implemented exponential backoff and garbage collection retry loops in `_safe_replace_dir` to eliminate transient Windows file lock contention during skill updates.
- **Complete End-User Documentation:** Comprehensive overhaul of `DOCS.md`, `ARCHITECTURE.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, `DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md`, and `README.md`, including an isolated public field-test archive under `docs/field-test/`.

## Upgrading

```bash
pip install --upgrade mesh-context-layer
mesh install --force
```

Restart your MCP clients (Claude Desktop, Cursor, Antigravity, etc.) or daemons afterwards. Existing databases and configurations remain fully backward-compatible.

## Verification

- **323 automated tests pass** on Ubuntu, macOS, and Windows across Python 3.10, 3.11, 3.12, and 3.13.
- Full documentation consistency and skill limit verification pass (`scripts/check_docs.py` and `scripts/check_skill.py`).

**Full diff:** https://github.com/billighost/mesh/compare/v0.8.0...v0.8.1
=== END FILE: docs/releases/RELEASE_NOTES_v0.8.1.md ===
=== FILE: examples/two_agents_demo.py (LINES: 117) ===
"""
two_agents_demo.py

The canonical Mesh demo. Two agents, one shared memory, zero direct coordination.
Agent A learns things. Agent B recalls them. They never communicate directly.

Run: python examples/two_agents_demo.py
"""

import time
from mesh import Mesh

print("=" * 60)
print("MESH — Two-Agent Memory Demo")
print("=" * 60)
print()

# ── Agent A: a coding agent discovers things during its work ──

print("[ Agent A: coding-agent ] starting work...")
print()

agent_a = Mesh(namespace="demo-project", agent_id="coding-agent")
agent_a.clear()  # Fresh start for the demo

mid1 = agent_a.learn(
    "The production API rate limit is 100 requests per minute per IP",
    memory_type="fact",
    confidence=1.0,
    tags=["api", "limits", "production"]
)
print(f"  ✓ Stored: API rate limit (id: {mid1[:8]}...)")

mid2 = agent_a.learn(
    "The user strongly prefers TypeScript over plain JavaScript for all new files",
    memory_type="preference",
    confidence=1.0,
    tags=["language", "typescript", "user-preference"]
)
print(f"  ✓ Stored: TypeScript preference (id: {mid2[:8]}...)")

mid3 = agent_a.learn(
    "Authentication uses JWT tokens stored in HttpOnly cookies, NOT localStorage",
    memory_type="instruction",
    confidence=1.0,
    tags=["auth", "security", "jwt"]
)
print(f"  ✓ Stored: Auth pattern (id: {mid3[:8]}...)")

mid4 = agent_a.learn(
    "The database migration script failed on 2024-01-15 due to a missing index on user_id",
    memory_type="result",
    confidence=0.95,
    tags=["database", "migration", "incident"]
)
print(f"  ✓ Stored: Migration incident (id: {mid4[:8]}...)")

print()
print(f"  Agent A finished. Total memories: {agent_a.count()}")
print()
print("-" * 60)
print()

# Small pause for dramatic effect
time.sleep(0.5)

# ── Agent B: a completely separate agent, different process, different purpose ──

print("[ Agent B: review-agent ] starting — has never spoken to Agent A")
print()

agent_b = Mesh(namespace="demo-project", agent_id="review-agent")

# Query 1: asks about API constraints
query1 = "what are the API constraints I need to know about?"
print(f'  → Asking: "{query1}"')
results1 = agent_b.recall(query1, n=2)
for r in results1:
    print(f"    [{r['similarity']:.2f}] {r['content']}")
    print(f"           from: {r['source_agent']} | type: {r['memory_type']}")
print()

# Query 2: asks about language preferences
query2 = "what language should I use for new code?"
print(f'  → Asking: "{query2}"')
results2 = agent_b.recall(query2, n=2)
for r in results2:
    print(f"    [{r['similarity']:.2f}] {r['content']}")
    print(f"           from: {r['source_agent']} | type: {r['memory_type']}")
print()

# Query 3: asks about auth — using completely different words
query3 = "how does login and session management work?"
print(f'  → Asking: "{query3}"')
results3 = agent_b.recall(query3, n=2)
for r in results3:
    print(f"    [{r['similarity']:.2f}] {r['content']}")
    print(f"           from: {r['source_agent']} | type: {r['memory_type']}")
print()

# Query 4: asks about past problems
query4 = "have there been any database issues?"
print(f'  → Asking: "{query4}"')
results4 = agent_b.recall(query4, n=2)
for r in results4:
    print(f"    [{r['similarity']:.2f}] {r['content']}")
    print(f"           from: {r['source_agent']} | type: {r['memory_type']}")
print()

print("-" * 60)
print()
print("Agent B recalled all of Agent A's memories")
print("without any direct communication.")
print()
print("That's Mesh.")
print()
print("=" * 60)

=== FILE: landing/site/HANDOFF.md (LINES: 107) ===
# Mesh landing site — handoff

A single-page, story-driven landing site for **Mesh** (`mesh-context-layer`), built from
`landing/BUILD_PROMPT.md` and the approved **Kernel** direction in
`landing/direction-sampler.html`.

## Run it

```bash
cd landing/site
npm install
npm run dev       # http://localhost:5173
npm run build     # production build → dist/
npm run preview   # serve the production build → http://localhost:4173
```

Requires Node 18+.

## Deploy

`dist/` is a fully static site. Upload it to any static host (Vercel, Netlify, Cloudflare
Pages, GitHub Pages, S3 + CDN). No server-side runtime is needed.

Before going live:

1. Replace the placeholder `SITE_URL` in `src/data/content.js` with the production URL.
2. Replace the canonical URL and OG image URLs in `index.html` (`https://mesh.example/`).
3. Point the GitHub / PyPI / Agensi links in `src/data/content.js` at the real destinations.
4. If the site is served from a subpath, set `base` in `vite.config.js` (e.g. `"/mesh/"`).

## What is in here

```
landing/site/
  index.html                 document shell, SEO meta, JSON-LD, inline theme boot
  public/assets/brand/       the 30 approved brand assets (SVG, PNG, ICO, manifest)
  src/
    App.jsx                  page composition
    data/content.js          all copy, nav, demo data, links
    components/              one component per page section + UI primitives
    hooks/                   preferences, in-view, clipboard
    lib/highlight.js         highlight.js core + python / json / bash only
    styles/                  tokens, base, components, sections, motion
  dist/                      production output (git-ignored)
```

## Design and behavior

- **Kernel direction**: white-first palette, acid-lime accent `#B9F24A`, ink `#0B0D08`,
  hard offset shadows, tight radii, Martian Mono display with Instrument Sans body.
- **Theme**: light is the default on a first visit. Light / System / Dark are selectable in
  the top bar and the mobile menu; the choice persists in `localStorage` under `mesh-theme`
  and is applied before first paint by the inline boot script.
- **Motion**: a Motion switch (persisted under `mesh-motion`) soft-disables animation.
  `prefers-reduced-motion` is also honored.
- **Content**: edit `src/data/content.js`; all section copy, demo facts, scores, links and
  navigation live there.
- **Demos**: How It Works, Search Explorer, and Memory (dedupe / contradictions / TTL /
  privacy) are deterministic and illustrative. Search and memory demo rows are labeled
  "example data" in the UI.

## Motion system

The preview's motion vocabulary is used throughout:

- magnetic pull on every `Button` (`components/ui/Magnetic.jsx`)
- spring press/bounce on buttons (`btnBounce` in `components.css`)
- cursor spotlight (`components/ui/CursorSpotlight.jsx`)
- 3D tilt on the fused result card (`components/ui/Tilt.jsx`)
- text scramble on the final headline (`components/ui/Scramble.jsx`)
- five-stage boot loader (`components/BootLoader.jsx`)
- staggered mobile menu entrance/exit (`menuIn` / `menuOut` in `sections.css`)
- scroll reveals, marquee, terminal line stagger, how-it-works packets, TTL/merge
  animations, and search-stage fills

## Code highlighting

`highlight.js` core is used with only three grammars (Python, JSON, Bash) registered, so
the full language pack is never shipped. Token colors are theme-independent inside the
dark terminal panels and defined in `components.css` under `hljs-*`.

## Accessibility

- Skip link, semantic landmarks, one `h1`, labeled sections.
- Keyboard support: tabs (arrows/Home/End), segmented controls (arrows), accordion,
  mobile menu focus trap + Escape + scroll lock, `aria-live` status lines.
- Motion off removes non-essential animation; theme and motion controls are real
  `radiogroup` / `switch` widgets.
- Demo copy is illustrative, not presented as a live benchmark.

## QA performed

- Production build passes (`vite build`).
- Desktop at 1440×900 and mobile at 390×844 / 320×720: no horizontal page scroll.
- Light and dark palettes checked on hero, search explorer, and interfaces.
- Theme default, boot loader, magnetic transform, press bounce, spotlight variables,
  menu open/close animation, tilt and scramble verified in the running build.
- No console errors in the production preview.

Final bundle: ~76.7 kB gzip JS, ~11.2 kB gzip CSS, ~1.6 kB gzip HTML.

## Do not change

- Do not use the Agensi logo. The mark in `public/assets/brand/` and the preview is the
  approved logo.
- `landing/direction-sampler.html` and `landing/assets/brand/` are source references; the
  site consumes copies under `public/assets/brand/`.

=== FILE: landing/site/public/assets/brand/README.md (LINES: 135) ===
# Mesh brand assets — Kernel

This folder is the approved brand asset set for the Mesh landing page. The mark is
the three-node glyph from `../direction-sampler.html` (the Kernel direction), not the
Agensi listing logo. The wordmark is **Martian Mono SemiExpanded Bold**, converted to
outlines so the lockups do not depend on a webfont being loaded.

## Mark

- Three nodes, two upper diagonals, one lower baseline. The lower baseline is a link,
  not a container. Do not close the triangle or add a fourth node.
- Source geometry lives in a `64 × 64` viewBox: nodes at `(32,13)`, `(12,50)`, `(52,50)`,
  radius `7`, stroke `4.5`, round caps.
- The app icon is an ink (`#0B0D08`) rounded square (`rx 14/64`) with a lime
  (`#B9F24A`) mark. The maskable variant is full-bleed ink with the mark scaled to
  `0.72` so it stays inside the safe zone.

## Color

| Token | Light | Dark | Use |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Ground | `#F7F8F3` | `#0B0D08` | Page background |
| Surface | `#FFFFFF` | `#11140C` | Cards, panels |
| Surface 2 | `#EEF0E7` | `#171B11` | Inset panels, code chips |
| Ink | `#0B0D08` | `#F4F7EC` | Text, borders, shadows |
| Muted | `#5D6455` | `#A2AA96` | Secondary text |
| Line | `#D7DBCD` | `#2A2F22` | Hairlines, dividers |
| Accent | `#B9F24A` | `#C8F04A` | Signal fills, highlights |
| Accent text | `#2F6B00` | `#C8F04A` | Accent-colored *text* |
| Accent soft | `#E8F9C7` | `#1E2A0E` | Tinted backgrounds |

The accent is a signal color. Do not use `#B9F24A` as body text on white — use the
accent-text token instead. The ink-on-lime pairing is the primary button treatment.

## Assets

### SVG (preferred for web)

| File | Use |
| --- | --- |
| `mesh-mark.svg` | Ink mark on light surfaces |
| `mesh-mark-light.svg` | Off-white mark on ink surfaces |
| `mesh-mark-accent.svg` | Lime mark on ink surfaces, or as an accent |
| `mesh-mark-currentColor.svg` | Inline mark that inherits `currentColor` |
| `mesh-logo.svg` | Ink mark + ink wordmark on light surfaces |
| `mesh-logo-light.svg` | Lime mark + off-white wordmark on ink surfaces |
| `mesh-app-icon.svg` | Rounded app icon / favicon / PWA icon |
| `mesh-app-icon-maskable.svg` | Full-bleed maskable icon |
| `favicon.svg` | SVG favicon (same artwork as the app icon) |
| `safari-pinned-tab.svg` | Monochrome Safari pinned-tab mask |

### PNG

| File | Size | Use |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `favicon-16.png` | 16 | Legacy favicon |
| `favicon-32.png` | 32 | Legacy favicon |
| `favicon-48.png` | 48 | Legacy favicon |
| `apple-touch-icon-180.png` | 180 | iOS home screen |
| `icon-192.png` | 192 | PWA any |
| `icon-512.png` | 512 | PWA any / social avatar |
| `icon-1024.png` | 1024 | App stores, press |
| `icon-maskable-192.png` | 192 | PWA maskable |
| `icon-maskable-512.png` | 512 | PWA maskable |
| `mstile-150x150.png` | 150 | Windows tile |
| `mesh-mark-512.png` | 512 | Transparent ink mark |
| `mesh-mark-light-512.png` | 512 | Transparent off-white mark |
| `mesh-mark-accent-512.png` | 512 | Transparent lime mark |
| `mesh-logo.png` | 1200×320 | Transparent ink lockup |
| `mesh-logo-light.png` | 1200×320 | Transparent light lockup; **use on dark backgrounds only** |
| `og-image.png` | 1200×630 | Open Graph / X / LinkedIn preview |
| `favicon.ico` | 16/32/48 | Browser tab fallback |

### Metadata

| File | Use |
| --- | --- |
| `site.webmanifest` | PWA manifest; assumes the folder is served at `/assets/brand/` |
| `browserconfig.xml` | Windows tile config |

## HTML head

These paths assume the folder is copied/served at **`/assets/brand/`**. If the site
lives under a subpath, adjust the absolute paths (and `start_url` / `scope` in the
manifest).

```html
<link rel="icon" href="/assets/brand/favicon.ico" sizes="any">
<link rel="icon" type="image/svg+xml" href="/assets/brand/favicon.svg">
<link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="/assets/brand/apple-touch-icon-180.png">
<link rel="mask-icon" href="/assets/brand/safari-pinned-tab.svg" color="#0B0D08">
<link rel="manifest" href="/assets/brand/site.webmanifest">
<meta name="msapplication-config" content="/assets/brand/browserconfig.xml">
<meta name="msapplication-TileColor" content="#0B0D08">
<meta name="theme-color" content="#0B0D08">

<meta property="og:image" content="https://mesh.example/assets/brand/og-image.png">
<meta property="og:image:width" content="1200">
<meta property="og:image:height" content="630">
<meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image">
<meta name="twitter:image" content="https://mesh.example/assets/brand/og-image.png">
```

Replace `mesh.example` with the production origin. The OG image already contains the
headline, install command, and the Agensi availability line; it is a finished asset,
not a template.

## Using the mark inline

Prefer `<img src="/assets/brand/mesh-mark.svg" alt="" width="16" height="16">` next to
text, so the SVG stays cacheable and has no duplicate IDs. If you need the mark to
inherit the surrounding text color, inline `mesh-mark-currentColor.svg` and give its
`<title>` a unique `id` (the file uses `id="t"`; duplicate IDs across many inline
copies are invalid).

The mark is decorative when it sits next to the word "mesh" or the text "Mesh".
Give it `alt=""` / `aria-hidden="true"` in that case. When it is the only brand
element in a link, label the link "Mesh home".

## Usage rules

- **Clear space:** at least the outer diameter of one node (≈ `18/64` of the mark's
  width) on every side. For the lockup, keep at least `16px` clear space per `64px`
  of lockup height.
- **Minimum sizes:** mark `16px` digital / `8mm` print; horizontal lockup `96px` wide
  digital / `30mm` print.
- **Dark surfaces:** use the `-light` variants. The light lockup's wordmark is off-white
  and is intentionally invisible on white; that is not a bug.
- **Do not:** rotate, stretch, outline, add gradients, add glows, change the node count,
  close the triangle, replace the wordmark with a different typeface, or recolor the
  mark outside the provided variants.
- **Never use the Agensi listing logo.** The Agensi listing is a distribution channel;
  it is not the Mesh mark. The user explicitly rejected that artwork.
- Do not put the mark inside another shape except the supplied app-icon and maskable
  variants.

=== FILE: marketing/scripts/generate_sfx.py (LINES: 312) ===
import math
import struct
import wave
import random
import os

SAMPLE_RATE = 48000

def write_wav(filename, samples, sample_rate=SAMPLE_RATE):
    """Write mono or stereo samples (float between -1.0 and 1.0) to 16-bit WAV."""
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(filename), exist_ok=True)
    is_stereo = isinstance(samples[0], (tuple, list))
    channels = 2 if is_stereo else 1
    
    with wave.open(filename, 'w') as wf:
        wf.setnchannels(channels)
        wf.setsampwidth(2)
        wf.setframerate(sample_rate)
        
        raw_data = bytearray()
        for s in samples:
            if is_stereo:
                l, r = s
                # Soft clip
                l = max(-0.99, min(0.99, l))
                r = max(-0.99, min(0.99, r))
                il = int(l * 32767.0)
                ir = int(r * 32767.0)
                raw_data.extend(struct.pack('<hh', il, ir))
            else:
                v = max(-0.99, min(0.99, s))
                iv = int(v * 32767.0)
                raw_data.extend(struct.pack('<h', iv))
        wf.writeframes(raw_data)
    print(f"Generated: {filename} ({len(samples)/sample_rate:.2f}s)")

def make_ambient_drone(duration=94.0):
    """Deep, cinematic analog pad drone (A1 chord: 55Hz, 82.4Hz, 110Hz, 164.8Hz)."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        # Envelope: 4s fade in, 4s fade out
        env = min(1.0, t / 4.0) * min(1.0, (duration - t) / 4.0)
        
        # Subtle slow LFOs for organic movement
        lfo1 = math.sin(2 * math.pi * 0.15 * t)
        lfo2 = math.cos(2 * math.pi * 0.08 * t)
        
        # Fundamental sub-bass + harmonics
        f0 = 55.0  # A1
        sub = 0.35 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * f0 * t)
        fifth = 0.22 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * (f0 * 1.5) * t + lfo1 * 0.2)
        octave = 0.18 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * (f0 * 2.0) * t + lfo2 * 0.3)
        third = 0.12 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * (f0 * 2.5) * t)
        high = 0.06 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * (f0 * 4.0) * t)
        
        # Subtle warm noise bed
        noise = (random.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.015
        
        mono = (sub + fifth + octave + third + high + noise) * env * 0.45
        # Slight stereo separation
        left = mono * (0.9 + 0.1 * lfo1)
        right = mono * (0.9 - 0.1 * lfo1)
        samples.append((left, right))
    return samples

def make_ping(freq=1760.0, duration=0.25):
    """High-frequency crystal electronic snap/ping."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 24.0)
        sig = 0.7 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * freq * t) + 0.3 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * (freq * 2.0) * t)
        samples.append(sig * env * 0.4)
    return samples

def make_glitch(duration=0.45):
    """Electronic glitch / error connection break."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 7.0)
        # Frequency modulation
        mod = math.sin(2 * math.pi * 32.0 * t)
        carrier = math.sin(2 * math.pi * (180.0 + mod * 220.0) * t)
        # Noise bursts
        noise = (random.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.4 if (int(t * 80) % 2 == 0) else 0
        sig = (carrier * 0.6 + noise * 0.4) * env * 0.5
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def make_impact_boom(duration=1.8):
    """Cinematic sub-bass boom impact."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 3.5)
        # Pitch drop from 120Hz down to 38Hz
        freq = 38.0 + (120.0 - 38.0) * math.exp(-t * 12.0)
        phase = 2 * math.pi * freq * t
        sub = math.sin(phase)
        # Transient punch at the start
        punch = math.exp(-t * 40.0) * math.sin(2 * math.pi * 80.0 * t) * 0.6
        sig = (sub * 0.8 + punch) * env * 0.75
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def make_whoosh(duration=0.65):
    """Airy cinematic stereo whoosh."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        # Hann window
        env = 0.5 * (1 - math.cos(2 * math.pi * t / duration))
        # Swept noise
        f_center = 200.0 + 2200.0 * math.sin(math.pi * t / duration)
        noise = random.random() * 2 - 1
        sig = noise * math.sin(2 * math.pi * f_center * t) * env * 0.5
        # Pan left to right
        pan = t / duration
        samples.append((sig * (1 - pan), sig * pan))
    return samples

def make_key_click(duration=0.07):
    """Subtle tactile mechanical keyboard click."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        click_env = math.exp(-t * 90.0)
        thock_env = math.exp(-t * 45.0)
        click = math.sin(2 * math.pi * 2400.0 * t) * click_env * 0.3
        thock = math.sin(2 * math.pi * 420.0 * t) * thock_env * 0.4
        noise = (random.random() * 2 - 1) * math.exp(-t * 120.0) * 0.25
        sig = (click + thock + noise) * 0.5
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def make_key_return(duration=0.12):
    """Deeper enter/return keystroke."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 30.0)
        thock = math.sin(2 * math.pi * 280.0 * t) * env * 0.55
        snap = math.sin(2 * math.pi * 3200.0 * t) * math.exp(-t * 80.0) * 0.25
        sig = (thock + snap) * 0.55
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def make_crystallize(duration=0.85):
    """Modern UI crystal pop / glass resonance."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    f0 = 1180.0
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 5.0)
        h1 = 0.5 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * f0 * t) * math.exp(-t * 4.5)
        h2 = 0.3 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * (f0 * 2.76) * t) * math.exp(-t * 7.0)
        h3 = 0.2 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * (f0 * 5.4) * t) * math.exp(-t * 10.0)
        sig = (h1 + h2 + h3) * env * 0.55
        samples.append((sig * 0.9, sig * 1.1))
    return samples

def make_sonar_pulse(duration=1.2):
    """Ethereal radar / sonar wave pulse."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    f0 = 840.0
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 3.2)
        sine = math.sin(2 * math.pi * f0 * t)
        oct = math.sin(2 * math.pi * (f0 * 2.0) * t) * 0.15
        sig = (sine + oct) * env * 0.45
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def make_stage_chime(note_freq=523.25, duration=0.7):
    """Ascending harmonic bell chime for search stages."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 6.5)
        h1 = 0.6 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * note_freq * t)
        h2 = 0.25 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * (note_freq * 2.0) * t)
        h3 = 0.15 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * (note_freq * 3.0) * t)
        sig = (h1 + h2 + h3) * env * 0.5
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def make_packet_transmit(duration=0.25):
    """Digital telemetry blip / packet transmission."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 15.0)
        # Fast upward chirp
        freq = 1200.0 + 1600.0 * (t / duration)
        sig = math.sin(2 * math.pi * freq * t) * env * 0.4
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def make_warn_buzz(duration=0.55):
    """Amber conflict warning buzz."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 5.0)
        # Dual detuned saw waves
        saw1 = (2.0 * ((180.0 * t) % 1.0) - 1.0) * 0.5
        saw2 = (2.0 * ((186.0 * t) % 1.0) - 1.0) * 0.5
        sig = (saw1 + saw2) * env * 0.4
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def make_resolved_bell(duration=0.9):
    """Warm major triad resolution chime."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    # D major triad: D5 (587.33), F#5 (739.99), A5 (880.0)
    chord = [587.33, 739.99, 880.0]
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 4.0)
        sig = sum(0.33 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * f * t) for f in chord) * env * 0.5
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def make_decay_sweep(duration=0.8):
    """Downward filter sweep for TTL decay."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 3.5)
        freq = 600.0 * math.exp(-t * 3.0) + 80.0
        sig = math.sin(2 * math.pi * freq * t) * env * 0.35
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def make_shield_deflect(duration=0.4):
    """High-Q metallic shield bounce ping."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        env = math.exp(-t * 16.0)
        metallic = (
            0.5 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * 2200.0 * t) +
            0.3 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * 3850.0 * t) +
            0.2 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * 5400.0 * t)
        )
        samples.append((metallic * env * 0.45, metallic * env * 0.45))
    return samples

def make_riser_swell(duration=3.2):
    """Cinematic riser building tension into finale."""
    n_samples = int(duration * SAMPLE_RATE)
    samples = []
    for i in range(n_samples):
        t = i / SAMPLE_RATE
        # Exponential curve up
        progress = t / duration
        env = math.pow(progress, 2.0)
        freq = 80.0 + 380.0 * math.pow(progress, 2.2)
        sine = math.sin(2 * math.pi * freq * t) * 0.4
        noise = (random.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.15 * progress
        sig = (sine + noise) * env * 0.55
        samples.append((sig, sig))
    return samples

def generate_all(out_dir):
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "ambient_drone.wav"), make_ambient_drone(94.0))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "mark_node_ping.wav"), make_ping(1760.0))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "connection_glitch.wav"), make_glitch(0.45))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "impact_boom.wav"), make_impact_boom(1.8))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "whoosh_fast.wav"), make_whoosh(0.65))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "key_click.wav"), make_key_click(0.07))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "key_return.wav"), make_key_return(0.12))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "crystallize.wav"), make_crystallize(0.85))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "sonar_pulse.wav"), make_sonar_pulse(1.2))
    
    # 5 pentatonic chimes for 5-stage search
    pentatonic = [523.25, 587.33, 659.25, 783.99, 880.00]
    for idx, f in enumerate(pentatonic, 1):
        write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, f"stage_chime_{idx}.wav"), make_stage_chime(f, 0.7))
        
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "packet_transmit.wav"), make_packet_transmit(0.25))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "warn_buzz.wav"), make_warn_buzz(0.55))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "resolved_bell.wav"), make_resolved_bell(0.9))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "decay_filter.wav"), make_decay_sweep(0.8))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "shield_deflect.wav"), make_shield_deflect(0.4))
    write_wav(os.path.join(out_dir, "riser_swell.wav"), make_riser_swell(3.2))
    print("All sound effects generated successfully!")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    dest = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "./sfx"
    generate_all(dest)

=== FILE: mesh/c_bridge.py (LINES: 248) ===
"""
mesh/c_bridge.py - ctypes bridge to the native core (mesh/_c_core.dll / .so / .dylib).

Public API (every function has a pure-Python/NumPy fallback, so the package works
without the native library):

  c_guard_prescan(text) -> bool            True  = provably clean ASCII, skip the regex guards
  c_hash_embed_384(text) -> ndarray | None 384-d hash embedding, None = "use the Python path"
  c_cosine_batch_384(matrix, query)        n dot products (unit vectors => cosine)
  c_compute_decay_batch(...)               vectorised TTL decay / staleness
  is_native_available() / native_info()

Build (see build_and_verify.py, which does this for you):
    Windows (MinGW/MSYS2):  gcc -O3 -std=c11 -shared -static-libgcc -o mesh/_c_core.dll c_core/mesh_core.c -lm
    Linux / macOS:          gcc -O3 -std=c11 -shared -fPIC -o mesh/_c_core.so c_core/mesh_core.c -lm

A library older than ABI version 2 is rejected on purpose: earlier builds had a
prescan that could wave through secrets such as "the admin key is ..." unredacted.
Set MESH_DISABLE_NATIVE=1 to force the Python fallbacks, MESH_C_CORE_PATH to point
at a specific library file.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ctypes
import os
import sys
import threading
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

import numpy as np

_REQUIRED_ABI = 2
_LIB: Optional[ctypes.CDLL] = None
_LIB_LOAD_LOCK = threading.Lock()
_LIB_LOAD_ATTEMPTED = False
_LIB_PATH: Optional[Path] = None
_HAS_AVX2 = False


def _candidate_paths() -> list[Path]:
    override = (os.environ.get("MESH_C_CORE_PATH") or "").strip()
    if override:
        return [Path(override)]
    mesh_dir = Path(__file__).parent
    if sys.platform == "win32":
        names = ["_c_core.dll"]
    elif sys.platform == "darwin":
        names = ["_c_core.dylib", "_c_core.so"]
    else:
        names = ["_c_core.so"]
    return [mesh_dir / n for n in names]


def _load_lib() -> Optional[ctypes.CDLL]:
    global _LIB, _LIB_LOAD_ATTEMPTED, _LIB_PATH, _HAS_AVX2
    with _LIB_LOAD_LOCK:
        if _LIB_LOAD_ATTEMPTED:
            return _LIB
        _LIB_LOAD_ATTEMPTED = True
        if os.environ.get("MESH_DISABLE_NATIVE", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
            return None
        for path in _candidate_paths():
            if not path.exists():
                continue
            try:
                lib = ctypes.CDLL(str(path))
                lib.mesh_c_version.argtypes = []
                lib.mesh_c_version.restype = ctypes.c_int
                if int(lib.mesh_c_version()) < _REQUIRED_ABI:
                    continue

                lib.mesh_c_cpu_has_avx2.argtypes = []
                lib.mesh_c_cpu_has_avx2.restype = ctypes.c_int

                lib.mesh_c_guard_prescan.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_size_t]
                lib.mesh_c_guard_prescan.restype = ctypes.c_int

                lib.mesh_c_hash_embed_384.argtypes = [ctypes.c_char_p, ctypes.c_size_t, ctypes.c_void_p]
                lib.mesh_c_hash_embed_384.restype = ctypes.c_int

                lib.mesh_c_cosine_batch_384.argtypes = [
                    ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_size_t, ctypes.c_void_p,
                ]
                lib.mesh_c_cosine_batch_384.restype = None

                lib.mesh_c_compute_decay_batch.argtypes = [
                    ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_size_t,
                    ctypes.c_double, ctypes.c_float, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p,
                ]
                lib.mesh_c_compute_decay_batch.restype = None

                _HAS_AVX2 = bool(lib.mesh_c_cpu_has_avx2())
                _LIB = lib
                _LIB_PATH = path
                return lib
            except Exception:
                continue
        return None


_load_lib()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Guard prescan
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Must stay identical to PRESCAN_TRIGGERS in c_core/mesh_core.c (build_and_verify.py
# fuzzes both implementations against each other and against the real regexes).
_PRESCAN_TRIGGERS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "sk-", "ghp", "gho", "ghu", "ghs", "ghr", "github", "glpat", "akia", "asia", "aiza", "xox",
    "eyj", "pypi", "sg.", "npm", "hf_", "rk_", "demo_fake", "demo", "password", "passwd",
    "passphrase", "passcode", "pwd", "pin", "secret", "token", "api_key", "apikey", "api key",
    "api-key", "key", "phrase", "mnemonic", "seed", "credential", "private", "code", "auth",
    "bearer", "basic", "hunter2", "vault", "locker", "login", "recovery", "aws", "ignor",
    "disregard", "forget", "override", "bypass", "system", "inst", "instruct", "prompt",
    "directive", "guideline", "constraint", "guardrail", "policies", "follow", "obey",
    "adhere", "mode", "jailbreak", "unrestricted", "developer", "mesh", "context", "important",
    "critical", "urgent", "anweisung", "instrucc", "regeln", "reglas", "regles", "instruc",
    "dan",
)

_PRESCAN_STRUCTURAL = ("<", "=", "_", "*", "`", "~", "[", "]", "--", "://")


def _py_guard_prescan(text: str) -> bool:
    """Pure-Python prescan, semantically identical to mesh_c_guard_prescan."""
    if not text or len(text) > 16384:
        return False
    for ch in text:
        c = ord(ch)
        if (c < 32 and c != 10 and c != 9) or c >= 127:
            return False
    for s in _PRESCAN_STRUCTURAL:
        if s in text:
            return False
    low = text.lower()
    for trigger in _PRESCAN_TRIGGERS:
        if trigger in low:
            return False
    return True


def c_guard_prescan(text: str) -> bool:
    """True = text is provably clean ASCII (skip the regex guards); False = run the full pipeline."""
    lib = _LIB
    if lib is not None:
        try:
            if not text.isascii():
                return False
            return lib.mesh_c_guard_prescan(text.encode("ascii"), len(text)) == 0
        except Exception:
            pass
    return _py_guard_prescan(text)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Hash embedding
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def c_hash_embed_384(text: str) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """Native memory._hash_embed_384; returns None when the caller must use the Python path."""
    lib = _LIB
    if lib is None:
        return None
    try:
        if not text.isascii() or len(text) > 8192:
            return None
        out = np.empty(384, dtype=np.float32)
        rc = lib.mesh_c_hash_embed_384(text.encode("ascii"), len(text), out.ctypes.data)
        if rc != 0:
            return None
        return out
    except Exception:
        return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Cosine / decay
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def c_cosine_batch_384(matrix: np.ndarray, query: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Dot product of every row of `matrix` (N x 384 float32) with `query` (384 float32)."""
    n = int(matrix.shape[0])
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float32)
    mat_c = matrix if (matrix.dtype == np.float32 and matrix.flags.c_contiguous) else np.ascontiguousarray(matrix, dtype=np.float32)
    q_flat = query.reshape(-1)
    q_c = q_flat if (q_flat.dtype == np.float32 and q_flat.flags.c_contiguous and q_flat.shape[0] == 384) else np.ascontiguousarray(q_flat[:384], dtype=np.float32)
    lib = _LIB
    if lib is not None and mat_c.shape[1] == 384 and q_c.shape[0] == 384:
        out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float32)
        try:
            lib.mesh_c_cosine_batch_384(mat_c.ctypes.data, q_c.ctypes.data, n, out.ctypes.data)
            return out
        except Exception:
            pass
    return mat_c @ q_c


def c_compute_decay_batch(
    confidences: np.ndarray,
    ttl_days: np.ndarray,
    decay_starts: np.ndarray,
    now_epoch: float,
    stale_threshold: float,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """
    Vectorised TTL decay. `ttl_days` / `decay_starts` use NaN for "no TTL" / "unknown start".
    Returns (effective_confidence float32[n], is_stale bool[n]).
    """
    conf = np.ascontiguousarray(confidences, dtype=np.float32)
    ttl = np.ascontiguousarray(ttl_days, dtype=np.float32)
    start = np.ascontiguousarray(decay_starts, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(conf.shape[0])
    lib = _LIB
    if lib is not None and n > 0:
        eff = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float32)
        stale = np.empty(n, dtype=np.uint8)
        try:
            lib.mesh_c_compute_decay_batch(
                conf.ctypes.data, ttl.ctypes.data, start.ctypes.data, n,
                float(now_epoch), float(stale_threshold), eff.ctypes.data, stale.ctypes.data,
            )
            return eff, stale.astype(bool)
        except Exception:
            pass
    c64 = conf.astype(np.float64)
    t64 = ttl.astype(np.float64)
    no_decay = ~(t64 > 0.0) | np.isnan(start)
    elapsed = np.maximum(0.0, (now_epoch - np.where(np.isnan(start), now_epoch, start)) / 86400.0)
    factor = np.clip(1.0 - elapsed / np.where(t64 > 0.0, t64, 1.0), 0.0, 1.0)
    eff64 = np.round(np.where(no_decay, c64, c64 * factor), 4)
    thr32 = np.float32(stale_threshold)
    stale_arr = np.where(no_decay, c64.astype(np.float32) < thr32, eff64.astype(np.float32) < thr32)
    return eff64.astype(np.float32), stale_arr.astype(bool)


def is_native_available() -> bool:
    """True if the native library was loaded."""
    return _LIB is not None


def native_info() -> dict:
    return {
        "available": _LIB is not None,
        "path": str(_LIB_PATH) if _LIB_PATH else None,
        "avx2_kernel": _HAS_AVX2,
        "abi": _REQUIRED_ABI,
    }
=== END FILE: mesh/c_bridge.py ===
=== FILE: mesh/cli.py (LINES: 3310) ===
"""
mesh/cli.py — Command-Line Interface for Mesh.

Everything a human sees is rendered through `mesh.ui` so the whole CLI shares one
visual language (see ui.py). Behaviour, security rules and daemon semantics:

  - Zero-overhead lazy command routing (never imports FastAPI, Uvicorn, or MCP on CLI operations)
  - Interactive permission prompts before starting background processes, modifying OS startup,
    deleting namespaces, or installing shell completions
  - Cryptographically verified (`verify_daemon_identity`) local daemon proxy with safe read fallback
    and non-retrying mutating command protection on timeout/502/503/504
  - Read-only commands use `create=False` so typo'd namespaces never create empty `.db` files
  - Multi-shell autocompletion (`bash`, `zsh`, `fish`, `powershell`) with idempotent `--install`
  - `mesh doctor` diagnostics (SQLite WAL/FTS5, permissions, ONNX SHA-256, degraded state, daemon, MCP)
  - Non-recursive interactive REPL (`mesh shell` / `mesh i`) with namespace switching
  - First-class secret handling (`mesh add --vault`, `mesh add --secret-ref`, `mesh run`),
    at-rest encryption (`mesh encrypt-db`, `mesh export --encrypt`), and quarantine review (`mesh approve`)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import difflib
import json
import os
import platform
import re
import shlex
import shutil
import socket
import sqlite3
import stat
import sys
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

from . import ui
from .audit import validate_namespace
from .envutil import env_bool, env_int
from .version import VERSION, check_for_updates

# Ensure Windows cp1252 / legacy consoles never crash on Unicode output
for _stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
    if hasattr(_stream, "reconfigure"):
        try:
            getattr(_stream, "reconfigure")(errors="replace")
        except Exception:
            pass

_FALLBACK_HTTP_CODES = {502, 503, 504}

# Memory type -> colour (purely cosmetic, unknown types fall back to muted)
_TYPE_COLORS = {
    "fact": "info",
    "preference": "accent",
    "decision": "brand",
    "instruction": "warn",
    "error": "error",
    "result": "brand",
    "process": "info",
    "context": "muted",
    "secret_ref": "warn",
    "general": "muted",
}

_ACTION_COLORS = {
    "recall": "info", "read": "info",
    "learn": "brand", "write": "brand",
    "update": "warn",
    "forget": "error", "delete": "error",
}
_ACTION_LABELS = {"recall": "read", "learn": "write"}

_SEP = f"  {ui.sym('dot')}  "


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Small shared helpers
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


def _type_text(mtype: str) -> str:
    return ui.style(mtype, _TYPE_COLORS.get(mtype, "muted"))


def _tilde(text: str) -> str:
    try:
        home = str(Path.home())
        return text.replace(home, "~") if home and home != "/" else text
    except Exception:
        return text


def _muted_join(parts: list[str]) -> str:
    sep = ui.style(_SEP, "muted")
    return sep.join(p for p in parts if p)


def _resolve_namespace(ns_arg: Optional[str]) -> str:
    raw = (ns_arg or os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE", "shared")).strip() or "shared"
    try:
        return validate_namespace(raw)
    except ValueError as e:
        ui.fail(str(e), "Namespaces may contain letters, numbers, '-', '_' and '.'.")


def _split_tags(raw: Optional[str]) -> list[str]:
    return [t.strip() for t in (raw or "").split(",") if t.strip()]


def _handle_missing_namespace_cli(ns: str, as_json: bool = False) -> None:
    """Explain a missing namespace for read commands, with did-you-mean suggestions."""
    from .memory import suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces

    suggestions = suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(ns)
    if as_json:
        ui.json_out({"namespace": ns, "namespace_exists": False, "did_you_mean": suggestions})
        return
    if suggestions:
        hint = "Did you mean: " + ", ".join(suggestions) + "?"
    else:
        hint = f'It will be created the first time you store a memory: mesh add "..." --namespace {ns}'
    ui.warn(f"Namespace '{ns}' doesn't exist", hint)


import http.client


class _FastHTTPConnection(http.client.HTTPConnection):
    def connect(self) -> None:
        super().connect()
        if getattr(self, "sock", None) is not None:
            try:
                self.sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_TCP, socket.TCP_NODELAY, 1)
            except Exception:
                pass


_CLI_HTTP_POOL: dict[tuple[str, int], http.client.HTTPConnection] = {}
_CLI_HTTP_POOL_LOCK = threading.Lock()

_CLI_MESH_CACHE: dict[tuple[str, str, bool], Any] = {}
_CLI_MESH_CACHE_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def _get_cli_mesh(
    namespace: str,
    agent_id: str = "cli",
    create: bool = False,
    trusted_local: bool = True,
) -> Any:
    key = (namespace, agent_id, trusted_local)
    with _CLI_MESH_CACHE_LOCK:
        m = _CLI_MESH_CACHE.get(key)
        if m is not None:
            return m
        from .memory import Mesh

        m = Mesh(namespace=namespace, agent_id=agent_id, create=create, trusted_local=trusted_local)
        _CLI_MESH_CACHE[key] = m
        return m


def evict_cli_mesh_cache(namespace: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    with _CLI_MESH_CACHE_LOCK:
        if namespace is None:
            _CLI_MESH_CACHE.clear()
        else:
            to_del = [k for k in _CLI_MESH_CACHE if k[0] == namespace]
            for k in to_del:
                _CLI_MESH_CACHE.pop(k, None)


def _get_pooled_conn(host: str, port: int, timeout: float) -> http.client.HTTPConnection:
    key = (host, port)
    with _CLI_HTTP_POOL_LOCK:
        conn = _CLI_HTTP_POOL.get(key)
        if conn is not None and getattr(conn, "sock", None) is None:
            _CLI_HTTP_POOL.pop(key, None)
            conn = None
        if conn is None:
            conn = _FastHTTPConnection(host, port, timeout=timeout)
            _CLI_HTTP_POOL[key] = conn
        return conn


def _close_pooled_conn(host: str, port: int) -> None:
    key = (host, port)
    with _CLI_HTTP_POOL_LOCK:
        conn = _CLI_HTTP_POOL.pop(key, None)
        if conn is not None:
            try:
                conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass


def _daemon_port_open(timeout: float = 0.015) -> Optional[int]:
    if os.environ.get("MESH_NO_DAEMON_PROXY", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return None
    try:
        from .daemon import _pid_file

        if not _pid_file().exists() and "MESH_FORCE_DAEMON_PROBE" not in os.environ:
            return None
    except Exception:
        pass
    host = os.environ.get("MESH_HTTP_HOST", "127.0.0.1")
    try:
        port = env_int("MESH_HTTP_PORT", 7701, minimum=1, maximum=65535)
        with socket.create_connection((host, port), timeout=timeout):
            return port
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None


def _daemon_request(
    path: str,
    payload: Optional[dict[str, Any]] = None,
    method: str = "POST",
    timeout: float = 4.0,
    host: Optional[str] = None,
    port: Optional[int] = None,
) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Send an authenticated request to the running local daemon if reachable.
    - Never sends the bearer token until `verify_daemon_identity` succeeds.
    - Never silently spawns a background daemon if the socket is closed.
    - Returns `None` only when the daemon was never reached, so callers know local
      execution is safe.
    - Returns `{"_http_error": code, "detail": ..., "_timeout": bool}` on 4xx/5xx or
      mid-request timeout, and `{"_daemon_lost": True, "detail": ...}` when the request
      was sent but its response was lost. Both mean a write may already have been
      applied, so mutating callers must never silently re-execute locally.
    """
    if os.environ.get("MESH_NO_DAEMON_PROXY", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return None
    req_host = host or os.environ.get("MESH_HTTP_HOST", "127.0.0.1")
    req_port = int(port or env_int("MESH_HTTP_PORT", 7701, minimum=1, maximum=65535))
    if host is None and "MESH_HTTP_PORT" not in os.environ:
        try:
            from .daemon import _pid_file

            if not _pid_file().exists():
                return None
        except Exception:
            pass

    from .daemon import get_auth_headers, invalidate_daemon_identity_cache, verify_daemon_identity

    # Rule: the bearer token is never sent until verify_daemon_identity succeeds.
    if not verify_daemon_identity(host=req_host, port=req_port, timeout=0.5):
        return None

    data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8") if payload is not None else None
    headers = get_auth_headers()
    headers["Connection"] = "keep-alive"
    if data is not None:
        headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json"

    for attempt in range(2):
        conn = _get_pooled_conn(req_host, req_port, timeout=timeout)
        try:
            conn.request(method, path, body=data, headers=headers)
            resp = conn.getresponse()
            raw_body = resp.read().decode("utf-8")
            if 200 <= int(resp.status) < 300:
                if not raw_body.strip():
                    return {}
                try:
                    return json.loads(raw_body)
                except ValueError:
                    return {
                        "_daemon_lost": True,
                        "detail": f"Daemon returned an unreadable response for {path}.",
                    }
            elif resp.status in (400, 401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500, 502, 503, 504):
                try:
                    err_body = json.loads(raw_body)
                    detail = err_body.get("detail") if isinstance(err_body, dict) else raw_body
                except Exception:
                    detail = raw_body or f"HTTP {resp.status}"
                return {"_http_error": int(resp.status), "detail": detail}
            return {
                "_daemon_lost": True,
                "detail": f"Daemon returned unexpected HTTP {resp.status} for {path}.",
            }
        except (http.client.RemoteDisconnected, BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError) as e:
            _close_pooled_conn(req_host, req_port)
            if attempt == 0:
                continue
            invalidate_daemon_identity_cache(req_host, req_port)
            return {
                "_daemon_lost": True,
                "detail": f"Lost the daemon connection while processing {path}: {e}",
            }
        except (TimeoutError, socket.timeout) as e:
            _close_pooled_conn(req_host, req_port)
            invalidate_daemon_identity_cache(req_host, req_port)
            return {
                "_http_error": 504,
                "_timeout": True,
                "detail": f"Daemon timed out after {timeout}s while processing {path}: {e}",
            }
        except OSError as e:
            _close_pooled_conn(req_host, req_port)
            invalidate_daemon_identity_cache(req_host, req_port)
            if "timed out" in str(e).lower():
                return {
                    "_http_error": 504,
                    "_timeout": True,
                    "detail": f"Daemon timed out after {timeout}s while processing {path}: {e}",
                }
            return {
                "_daemon_lost": True,
                "detail": f"Lost the daemon connection while processing {path}: {e}",
            }
        except Exception as e:
            _close_pooled_conn(req_host, req_port)
            invalidate_daemon_identity_cache(req_host, req_port)
            return {
                "_daemon_lost": True,
                "detail": f"Lost the daemon connection while processing {path}: {e}",
            }
    return {
        "_daemon_lost": True,
        "detail": f"Daemon gave no response for {path}.",
    }


def _check_daemon_read_response(proxied: Optional[dict[str, Any]], cmd_label: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Read-only commands: on 502/503/504/timeout fall back to local execution with a quiet note;
    on other errors print and exit non-zero.
    """
    if proxied is None:
        return None
    if proxied.get("_daemon_lost"):
        detail = proxied.get("detail") or "Lost the daemon response."
        ui.note(f"Daemon unavailable ({detail}); using the local database for '{cmd_label}'.")
        return None
    if "_http_error" in proxied:
        code = int(proxied["_http_error"])
        detail = proxied.get("detail") or f"HTTP {code}"
        if code in _FALLBACK_HTTP_CODES or proxied.get("_timeout"):
            ui.note(f"Daemon unavailable ({detail}); using the local database for '{cmd_label}'.")
            return None
        ui.fail(str(detail))
    return proxied


def _check_daemon_write_response(proxied: Optional[dict[str, Any]], cmd_label: str) -> Optional[dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Mutating commands: never auto-retry locally on 502/503/504/timeout because the daemon may have
    applied the write. Tell the user the outcome is unknown and how to verify.
    """
    if proxied is None:
        return None
    if proxied.get("_daemon_lost"):
        ui.fail(
            str(proxied.get("detail") or "Lost the daemon response."),
            "The daemon may or may not have applied this change. "
            "Run `mesh inspect` to check before retrying.",
        )
    if "_http_error" in proxied:
        code = int(proxied["_http_error"])
        detail = proxied.get("detail") or f"HTTP {code}"
        if code in _FALLBACK_HTTP_CODES or proxied.get("_timeout"):
            ui.fail(
                f"{detail}",
                "The daemon may or may not have applied this change. "
                "Run `mesh inspect` to check before retrying.",
            )
        ui.fail(str(detail))
    return proxied


def _namespace_degraded_counts(namespace: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Return `(hash_384_count, needs_reembed_count)` for an existing namespace DB without creating one."""
    from .audit import ensure_secure_mesh_dir

    db_path = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve() / f"mesh_{namespace}.db"
    if not db_path.exists():
        return 0, 0

    from .store import _CACHE_LOCK, _VECTOR_CACHE

    with _CACHE_LOCK:
        entry = _VECTOR_CACHE.get((db_path, namespace))
        if entry is not None and entry.rows_by_id:
            rows = list(entry.rows_by_id.values())
            hash_cnt = sum(1 for r in rows if r.get("embedding_model") == "hash-384")
            reembed_cnt = sum(1 for r in rows if r.get("embedding_model") in ("needs-reembed", "unknown") or not r.get("embedding"))
            return hash_cnt, reembed_cnt
    try:
        from .store import MeshStore

        store = MeshStore(namespace=namespace, create=False)
        with store._connect() as conn:
            hash_cnt = int(
                conn.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories WHERE namespace = ? AND embedding_model = 'hash-384'",
                    (namespace,),
                ).fetchone()[0]
                or 0
            )
            reembed_cnt = int(
                conn.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories WHERE namespace = ? AND (embedding_model = 'needs-reembed' OR embedding_model = 'unknown' OR embedding IS NULL)",
                    (namespace,),
                ).fetchone()[0]
                or 0
            )
            return hash_cnt, reembed_cnt
    except Exception:
        return 0, 0


# --- Smart Command & Flag Typo Recovery ---
_COMMAND_ALIASES = {
    "learn": "add",
    "save": "add",
    "remember": "add",
    "search": "ask",
    "recall": "ask",
    "find": "ask",
    "edit": "update",
    "modify": "update",
    "rm": "forget",
    "del": "forget",
    "delete": "forget",
    "ls": "inspect",
    "list": "inspect",
    "show": "inspect",
    "conflict": "conflicts",
    "repl": "shell",
    "i": "shell",
    "exec": "run",
    "encrypt": "encrypt-db",
}


def _suggest_did_you_mean(unknown: str, choices: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Close matches over real commands *and* aliases, reported as canonical command names."""
    pool = list(dict.fromkeys([*choices, *_COMMAND_ALIASES.keys()]))
    found: list[str] = []
    for match in difflib.get_close_matches(unknown.lower(), pool, n=4, cutoff=0.5):
        canonical = _COMMAND_ALIASES.get(match, match)
        if canonical in choices and canonical not in found:
            found.append(canonical)
    return found[:2]


def _type_help(valid_types: Any) -> str:
    return "Memory type: " + ", ".join(sorted(valid_types))


def _parser(name: str, description: str, examples: Optional[list[tuple[str, str]]] = None) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    epilog = None
    if examples:
        width = max(len(c) for c, _ in examples) + 2
        epilog = "examples:\n" + "\n".join(f"  {c.ljust(width)}# {d}" for c, d in examples)
    return argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog=f"mesh {name}",
        description=description,
        epilog=epilog,
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    )


def _render_memory_card(index: int, mem: dict[str, Any], width: int) -> None:
    """Rich, wrapped result card used by `mesh ask`."""
    gutter = 6
    sim = mem.get("similarity")
    conf = float(mem.get("effective_confidence", mem.get("confidence", 1.0)))
    mtype = str(mem.get("memory_type", "fact"))
    short_id = str(mem.get("id", ""))[:8]

    lines = ui.wrap(str(mem.get("content", "")), width - gutter, max_lines=6)
    num = ui.style(f"{index:>2}", "brand", "bold")
    print(f"{ui.IND}{num}  {lines[0]}")
    for extra in lines[1:]:
        print(f"{ui.IND}    {extra}")

    meta: list[str] = []
    if sim is not None and float(sim) > 0:
        s = float(sim)
        meta.append(f"{ui.meter(s)} {round(s * 100):>3}%")
    meta.append(_type_text(mtype))
    meta.append(ui.style(f"conf {conf:.2f}", "muted"))
    meta.append(ui.style(short_id, "bold"))
    if mem.get("source_agent"):
        meta.append(ui.style(f"by {mem['source_agent']}", "muted"))
    if mem.get("trust_tier") and mem.get("trust_tier") != "user":
        meta.append(ui.style(f"trust:{mem['trust_tier']}", "muted"))
    age = ui.relative_time(mem.get("created_at"))
    if age:
        meta.append(ui.style(age, "muted"))
    if mem.get("local_only"):
        meta.append(ui.badge("private", "accent"))
    if mem.get("quarantined"):
        meta.append(ui.badge("quarantined", "warn"))
    if mem.get("is_stale"):
        meta.append(ui.badge("stale", "warn"))
    print(f"{ui.IND}    {_muted_join(meta)}")

    if mem.get("secret_ref") and isinstance(mem.get("secret_ref"), dict):
        sref = mem["secret_ref"]
        print(
            f"{ui.IND}    {ui.style('locator: ' + str(sref.get('uri', '')), 'warn')}"
        )

    tags = mem.get("tags") or mem.get("tags_list") or []
    if tags:
        print(f"{ui.IND}    {ui.style(' '.join('#' + str(t) for t in tags), 'muted')}")
    print()


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# setup / install-skills
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


def setup_cmd() -> None:
    parser = _parser("setup", "Start the background worker, download the embedding model and install agent skills.")
    parser.add_argument("--namespace", default=None, metavar="NAME", help="Namespace to use (default: $MESH_NAMESPACE or 'shared')")
    parser.add_argument("--no-startup", action="store_true", help="Don't register login auto-start")
    parser.add_argument("--wait", action="store_true", help="Wait for the model download to finish")
    parser.add_argument("--yes", "-y", action="store_true", help="Skip interactive y/n permission prompts")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    from .daemon import (
        DAEMON_LOG_FILE,
        DEFAULT_HOST,
        DEFAULT_IDLE_TIMEOUT,
        DEFAULT_PORT,
        install_startup,
        request_user_permission,
        start_daemon,
    )
    from .memory import _ONNX_MODEL_DIR, _has_local_onnx_pair, ensure_model_downloaded

    ns = _resolve_namespace(args.namespace)
    ui.header("Setup", f"v{VERSION}", f"namespace {ns}")
    print()

    problems = 0
    skipped_steps = 0

    # 1 ─ login auto-start
    if not args.no_startup:
        if request_user_permission("register the Mesh daemon for OS login auto-start", assume_yes=args.yes):
            info = install_startup(
                host=DEFAULT_HOST,
                port=DEFAULT_PORT,
                idle_timeout=DEFAULT_IDLE_TIMEOUT,
                start_now=False,
            )
            ui.success("Login auto-start registered", str(info.get("platform", "")))
        else:
            skipped_steps += 1
            ui.step("Login auto-start skipped")

    # 2 ─ background daemon
    if request_user_permission(
        f"start the background Mesh HTTP daemon on {DEFAULT_HOST}:{DEFAULT_PORT}",
        assume_yes=args.yes,
    ):
        with ui.spinner("Starting background daemon…"):
            res = start_daemon(host=DEFAULT_HOST, port=DEFAULT_PORT, idle_timeout=DEFAULT_IDLE_TIMEOUT, lazy=True, wait=args.wait)
        if res.get("running"):
            ui.success("Background daemon running", f"http://{DEFAULT_HOST}:{DEFAULT_PORT}  {ui.sym('dot')}  pid {res.get('pid')}")
        else:
            problems += 1
            ui.error("Background daemon failed to start", f"See the log: {_tilde(str(DAEMON_LOG_FILE))}")
    else:
        skipped_steps += 1
        ui.step("Background daemon skipped")

    # 3 ─ embedding model
    pair = _has_local_onnx_pair()
    if pair is not None:
        ui.success("Embedding model ready", _tilde(str(pair[0].parent)))
    elif request_user_permission(
        f"download the ONNX embedding model into {_tilde(str(_ONNX_MODEL_DIR))}",
        assume_yes=args.yes,
    ):
        if args.wait or args.yes:
            with ui.spinner("Downloading embedding model (this can take a minute)…"):
                pair = ensure_model_downloaded(blocking=True, timeout=120.0, assume_yes=True)
            if pair is not None:
                ui.success("Embedding model downloaded", _tilde(str(pair[0].parent)))
                _daemon_request("/warmup", payload={"namespace": ns}, method="POST")
            else:
                ui.warn(
                    "Embedding model isn't ready yet",
                    "Mesh will use its built-in fallback until the download finishes. Check with `mesh doctor`.",
                    stdout=True,
                )
        else:
            ensure_model_downloaded(blocking=False, assume_yes=True, detached=True)
            ui.info("Downloading embedding model in the background", _tilde(str(_ONNX_MODEL_DIR)))
    else:
        skipped_steps += 1
        ui.step("Embedding model download skipped", "Mesh will use its built-in fallback (lower search quality).")

    # 4 ─ agent skills
    ui.section("Agent skills")
    print()
    problems += _run_install_skills(assume_yes=args.yes, quiet_header=True)

    # Wrap-up
    print()
    ui.box(
        [
            ui.style("Try it:", "muted"),
            "",
            f"{ui.style('mesh add', 'info')} \"We deploy the API to Fly.io\"",
            f"{ui.style('mesh ask', 'info')} \"how do we deploy?\"",
            f"{ui.style('mesh doctor', 'info')}   {ui.style('verify everything works', 'muted')}",
        ],
        title="Mesh is ready" if not (problems or skipped_steps) else ("Setup finished with issues" if problems else "Setup finished — some steps skipped"),
        color="brand" if not (problems or skipped_steps) else "warn",
    )


def _run_install_skills(assume_yes: bool = False, force: bool = False, quiet_header: bool = False) -> int:
    """Install the skill into every detected agent folder. Returns the number of errors."""
