  .search-wrap { order: 3; max-width: 100%; flex-basis: 100%; }
  .metrics-inline { display: none; }
  .conflict-grid, .field-row { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .row-actions { opacity: 1; }
}
</style>
</head>

    <div class="brand">

    <div class="search-wrap">
      <input type="text" id="search-input" placeholder="Search memories (semantic + keyword)..." oninput="onSearch(this.value)">
      <span class="search-kbd">/</span>
    </div>
    <div class="metrics-inline">
      <span class="metric-pill">Memories <span class="metric-val" id="m-total">0</span></span>
      <span class="metric-pill">Stale <span class="metric-val warn" id="m-stale">0</span></span>
      <span class="metric-pill">Conflicts <span class="metric-val danger" id="m-conflicts">0</span></span>
      <span class="metric-pill">Gaps <span class="metric-val" id="m-gaps">0</span></span>
    </div>
    <button class="btn" onclick="refreshAll()" title="Refresh">&#x21bb;</button>
    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="openModal()">+ New</button>

  <div class="subnav">

      <button class="tab active" onclick="showTab('memories', this)">Memories <span class="tab-count" id="tc-memories">0</span></button>
      <button class="tab" onclick="showTab('conflicts', this)">Conflicts <span class="tab-count" id="tc-conflicts">0</span></button>
      <button class="tab" onclick="showTab('patterns', this)">Patterns</button>

      <button class="tab" onclick="showTab('namespaces', this)">Namespaces</button>
      <button class="tab" onclick="showTab('digest', this)">Digest</button>
    </div>
    <span id="shown-count" style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--subtle);font-family:var(--mono)"></span>

  <!-- Memories Panel -->
  <div class="panel active" id="panel-memories">
    <div class="toolbar">
      <button class="chip active" onclick="setType('all', this)">All</button>
      <button class="chip" onclick="setType('fact', this)">Fact</button>
      <button class="chip" onclick="setType('decision', this)">Decision</button>

      <button class="chip" onclick="setType('secret_ref', this)">Secret Ref</button>
      <button class="chip" onclick="setType('general', this)">General</button>
      <select class="sort-select" onchange="setSort(this.value)">
        <option value="accessed">Sort: Recent access</option>

    <div id="patterns-unused"></div>

      <button class="chip active" onclick="loadAudit('all', this)">All</button>
      <button class="chip" onclick="loadAudit('read', this)">Reads</button>

  <div class="modal">
    <div class="modal-head">
      <span id="modal-title">New Memory</span>
      <button class="icon-btn" onclick="closeModal()" title="Close">&#x2715;</button>

      <label>Content</label>
      <textarea id="f-content" placeholder="Specific fact, decision, or rule..."></textarea>

        <select id="f-type">
          <option value="fact">fact</option>

          <option value="secret_ref">secret_ref</option>
          <option value="general">general</option>
        </select>

      <div class="field">
        <label>Confidence (0.0 - 1.0)</label>
        <input type="number" id="f-conf" min="0" max="1" step="0.05" value="1.0">
      </div>

        <input type="number" id="f-ttl" min="1" step="1" placeholder="Permanent">
      </div>
    </div>
    <label style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted);cursor:pointer;margin-top:2px">
      <input type="checkbox" id="f-private"> Private (local_only - exclude from exports &amp; prompts)
    </label>
    <div class="modal-foot">
      <button class="btn" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" id="f-submit" onclick="saveMemory()">Save</button>
    </div>

<div id="toast"></div>

<script>
const _urlParams = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
const _csrfMeta = document.querySelector('meta[name="mesh-csrf"]');
let MESH_CSRF = (_csrfMeta && _csrfMeta.getAttribute('content')) || '';
if (_urlParams.has('code') || _urlParams.has('token')) {
  _urlParams.delete('code');
  _urlParams.delete('token');
  const cleanSearch = _urlParams.toString();
  history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname + (cleanSearch ? '?' + cleanSearch : ''));
}

let ns = _urlParams.get('ns') || '';
let memories = [];
let searchResults = null;
let otherPossibleResults = [];
let searchFilterHint = null;
let activeType = 'all';
let activeTab = 'memories';
let activeAuditFilter = 'all';
let sortBy = 'accessed';
let editId = null;
let editOrigTtl = null;
let debounceTimer = null;
let searchSeq = 0;

function authHeaders(extra = {}) {
  const h = { ...extra };
  if (MESH_CSRF && MESH_CSRF !== '__MESH_CSRF_TOKEN__') {
    h['X-Mesh-CSRF'] = MESH_CSRF;
  } else {
    h['X-Mesh-CSRF'] = '1';
  }
  return h;
}

// Delegated click handler for all dynamic row actions (prevents inline onclick XSS)
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const el = e.target.closest('[data-action]');
  if (!el) return;
  const action = el.getAttribute('data-action');
  const targetId = el.getAttribute('data-id') || '';
  if (action === 'edit') openEdit(targetId);
  else if (action === 'delete') delMemory(targetId);
  else if (action === 'approve') approveMemory(targetId);
  else if (action === 'jump-type') jumpToType(targetId);
  else if (action === 'resolve-conflict') resolveConflict(targetId);
  else if (action === 'switch-ns') switchNamespace(targetId);
  else if (action === 'export-ns') exportNs(targetId);
  else if (action === 'delete-ns') deleteNs(targetId);
});

window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
  await refreshAll();
});

let authFailureShown = false;
async function apiFetch(path, opts = {}) {
  try {
    const mergedOpts = {
      credentials: 'same-origin',
      ...opts,
      headers: authHeaders(opts.headers || {})
    };
    const res = await fetch(path, mergedOpts);
    if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) {
      if (!authFailureShown) {
        authFailureShown = true;
        notify('Authentication failed — reopen the dashboard from the terminal');
      }
      return null;
    }
    return res.ok ? await res.json() : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}

async function refreshAll() {
  const stats = await apiFetch(`/api/stats${ns ? '?namespace=' + encodeURIComponent(ns) : ''}`);
  if (stats) {
    ns = stats.namespace;
    document.getElementById('m-total').textContent = stats.total_memories;
    document.getElementById('m-stale').textContent = stats.stale_memories;
    document.getElementById('m-conflicts').textContent = stats.open_contradictions;
    document.getElementById('m-gaps').textContent = stats.memory_gaps;
    document.getElementById('tc-memories').textContent = stats.total_memories;
    document.getElementById('tc-conflicts').textContent = stats.open_contradictions;
  }
  await populateNamespaceDropdown();
  await loadMemories();
  if (activeTab === 'conflicts') await loadConflicts();
  else if (activeTab === 'patterns') await loadPatterns();
  else if (activeTab === 'audit') await loadAudit(activeAuditFilter);
  else if (activeTab === 'namespaces') await loadNamespaces();
  else if (activeTab === 'digest') await loadDigest();
}

async function populateNamespaceDropdown() {
  const list = await apiFetch('/api/namespaces') || [];
  const names = new Set(list.map(x => x.namespace));
  if (ns) names.add(ns);
  const sel = document.getElementById('ns-select');
  sel.innerHTML = Array.from(names).sort().map(n =>
    `<option value="${esc(n)}" ${n === ns ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(n)}</option>`
  ).join('');
}

function switchNamespace(newNs) {
  ns = newNs;
  clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
  searchSeq++;
  searchResults = null;
  otherPossibleResults = [];
  searchFilterHint = null;
  document.getElementById('search-input').value = '';
  refreshAll();
}

async function loadMemories() {
  const data = await apiFetch(`/api/memories?limit=200&namespace=${encodeURIComponent(ns)}`);
  memories = Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
  renderMemories();
}

function parseTags(m) {
  if (Array.isArray(m.tags_list)) return m.tags_list;
  if (Array.isArray(m.tags)) return m.tags;
  if (typeof m.tags === 'string' && m.tags.trim()) {
    try { const p = JSON.parse(m.tags); return Array.isArray(p) ? p : []; } catch { return []; }
  }
  return [];
}

function renderMemories() {
  let list = searchResults !== null ? [...searchResults] : [...memories];
  if (activeType !== 'all') {
    list = list.filter(m => (m.memory_type || 'fact') === activeType);
  }
  if (searchResults === null) {
    if (sortBy === 'accessed') {
      list.sort((a, b) => (b.accessed_at || b.created_at || '').localeCompare(a.accessed_at || a.created_at || ''));
    } else if (sortBy === 'created') {
      list.sort((a, b) => (b.created_at || '').localeCompare(a.created_at || ''));
    } else if (sortBy === 'confidence') {
      list.sort((a, b) => (b.effective_confidence ?? b.confidence ?? 1) - (a.effective_confidence ?? a.confidence ?? 1));
    }
  }

  document.getElementById('shown-count').textContent =
    searchResults !== null ? `${list.length} matches` : `${list.length} items`;

  const root = document.getElementById('memory-list');
  let html = '';

  const rescuedCount = otherPossibleResults.filter(x => String(x.reason || '').startsWith('matched_in_other_type_or_tag')).length;
  if (searchResults !== null && activeType !== 'all' && rescuedCount > 0) {
    html += `<div class="filter-rescue-banner">
      <span><strong>${rescuedCount} strong match${rescuedCount > 1 ? 'es' : ''}</strong> found in other memory types (hidden by active <code>${esc(activeType)}</code> filter).</span>
      <button class="btn" onclick="resetTypeFilter()">Show All Types</button>
    </div>`;
  } else if (searchFilterHint) {
    html += `<div class="filter-rescue-banner">
      <span>${esc(searchFilterHint)}</span>
      <button class="btn" onclick="resetTypeFilter()">Show All Types</button>
    </div>`;
  }

  if (!list.length) {
    html += `<div class="empty">No memories match the current view.</div>`;
  } else {
    html += `<div class="list">` + list.map(m => {
      const t = m.memory_type || 'fact';
      const tags = parseTags(m);
      const conf = (m.effective_confidence ?? m.confidence ?? 1.0).toFixed(2);
      const sim = m.similarity !== undefined ? `<span class="flag sim">${Math.round(m.similarity * 100)}% match</span>` : '';
      const reasonRaw = m.match_reason || '';
      const reasonClass = reasonRaw.includes('synonym') ? 'synonym' : 'reason';
      const reasonBadge = (m.similarity !== undefined && reasonRaw) ? `<span class="flag ${reasonClass}">${esc(reasonRaw)}</span>` : '';
      const stale = m.is_stale ? `<span class="flag stale">stale</span>` : '';
      const priv = m.local_only ? `<span class="flag private">private</span>` : '';
      const quar = m.quarantined ? `<span class="flag quarantined">quarantined</span>` : '';
      const trust = m.trust_tier && m.trust_tier !== 'user' ? `<span>trust:${esc(m.trust_tier)}</span>` : '';
      const approveBtn = m.quarantined ? `<button class="btn" data-action="approve" data-id="${escAttr(m.id)}">Approve</button>` : '';
      return `<div class="row">
        <div class="row-main">
          <div class="row-top">
            <span class="type-pill ${esc(t)}">${esc(t)}</span>
            <span class="row-content">${esc(m.content)}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="row-meta">
            ${sim}${reasonBadge}${stale}${priv}${quar}
            <span>conf ${conf}</span>
            <span>by ${esc(m.source_agent || 'agent')}</span>
            ${trust}
            ${m.access_count ? `<span>${m.access_count}x read</span>` : ''}
            ${m.created_at ? `<span>${relTime(m.created_at)}</span>` : ''}
            ${tags.map(tag => `<span class="tag">#${esc(tag)}</span>`).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="row-actions">
          ${approveBtn}
          <button class="icon-btn" title="Edit" data-action="edit" data-id="${escAttr(m.id)}">&#x270e;</button>
          <button class="icon-btn del" title="Delete" data-action="delete" data-id="${escAttr(m.id)}">&#x2715;</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('') + `</div>`;
  }

  const shownIds = new Set(list.map(m => m.id));
  const snippets = (searchResults !== null ? otherPossibleResults : []).filter(s => s.id && !shownIds.has(s.id));
  if (snippets.length > 0) {
    html += `<div class="other-snippets-wrap">
      <div class="section-label">Other Possible Memories in '${esc(ns)}' (Peripheral Snippets &middot; ${snippets.length})</div>
      <div class="other-snippets-grid">` +
      snippets.map(s => {
        const st = s.memory_type || 'fact';
        const stags = parseTags(s);
        const sSim = s.similarity && s.similarity > 0 ? `<span class="flag sim">${Math.round(s.similarity * 100)}%</span>` : '';
        const isRescue = String(s.reason || '').startsWith('matched_in_other_type_or_tag');
        const sReason = `<span class="flag ${isRescue ? 'rescue' : 'reason'}">${esc(s.reason || 'context')}</span>`;
        const preview = s.snippet || s.content || '';
        return `<div class="snippet-card">
          <div class="snippet-top">
            <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">
              <span class="type-pill ${esc(st)}" style="cursor:pointer" title="Filter by ${esc(st)}" data-action="jump-type" data-id="${escAttr(st)}">${esc(st)}</span>
              ${sSim}${sReason}
            </div>
            <div class="row-actions" style="opacity:0.85">
              <button class="icon-btn" title="Edit Memory" data-action="edit" data-id="${escAttr(s.id)}">&#x270e;</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="snippet-text">${esc(preview)}</div>
          <div class="row-meta">
            <span>id ${esc(String(s.id).slice(0, 8))}..</span>
            ${stags.map(tag => `<span class="tag">#${esc(tag)}</span>`).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>`;
      }).join('') +
      `</div></div>`;
  }

  root.innerHTML = html;
}

function resetTypeFilter() {
  const allBtn = document.querySelector('#panel-memories .chip');
  if (allBtn) setType('all', allBtn);
}

function jumpToType(type) {
  const chips = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#panel-memories .chip'));
  const target = chips.find(c => c.textContent.trim().toLowerCase() === type.toLowerCase());
  if (target) setType(type.toLowerCase(), target);
}

function setType(type, btn) {
  activeType = type;
  document.querySelectorAll('#panel-memories .chip').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');
  const q = document.getElementById('search-input').value.trim();
  if (q.length >= 2) {
    onSearch(q);
  } else {
    renderMemories();
  }
}

function setSort(val) {
  sortBy = val;
  renderMemories();
}

function onSearch(val) {
  clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
  const q = val.trim();
  if (!q) {
    searchSeq++;
    searchResults = null;
    otherPossibleResults = [];
    searchFilterHint = null;
    renderMemories();
    return;
  }

  const ql = q.toLowerCase();
  const matchedAll = memories.filter(m =>
    (m.content || '').toLowerCase().includes(ql) ||
    parseTags(m).some(t => t.toLowerCase().includes(ql))
  );
  searchResults = activeType === 'all'
    ? matchedAll
    : matchedAll.filter(m => (m.memory_type || 'fact') === activeType);

  const primaryIds = new Set(searchResults.map(m => m.id));
  otherPossibleResults = [
    ...matchedAll.filter(m => !primaryIds.has(m.id)).map(m => ({
      ...m,
      snippet: (m.content || '').slice(0, 120),
      reason: `matched_in_other_type_or_tag (type=${m.memory_type || 'fact'})`
    })),
    ...memories.filter(m => !primaryIds.has(m.id) && !matchedAll.includes(m) && !m.local_only).slice(0, 4).map(m => ({
      ...m,
      snippet: (m.content || '').slice(0, 120),
      reason: 'namespace_context'
    }))
  ].slice(0, 6);

  renderMemories();

  if (q.length >= 2) {
    const seq = ++searchSeq;
    const requestedNs = ns;
    debounceTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
      const typeParam = activeType !== 'all' ? `&memory_type=${encodeURIComponent(activeType)}` : '';
      const res = await apiFetch(
        `/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&n=25&include_other=true&namespace=${encodeURIComponent(requestedNs)}${typeParam}`
      );
      if (seq !== searchSeq || requestedNs !== ns) return;
      if (res && Array.isArray(res.results)) {
        searchResults = res.results;
        otherPossibleResults = Array.isArray(res.other_possible_memories) ? res.other_possible_memories : [];
        searchFilterHint = res.filter_hint || null;
        renderMemories();
      } else if (Array.isArray(res)) {
        searchResults = res;
        renderMemories();
      }
    }, 180);
  }
}

function openModal() {
  editId = null;
  editOrigTtl = null;
  document.getElementById('modal-title').textContent = 'New Memory';
  document.getElementById('f-content').value = '';
  document.getElementById('f-type').value = 'fact';
  document.getElementById('f-conf').value = '1.0';
  document.getElementById('f-tags').value = '';
  document.getElementById('f-ttl').value = '';
  document.getElementById('f-private').checked = false;
  document.getElementById('backdrop').classList.add('open');
  setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('f-content').focus(), 40);
}

function openEdit(id) {
  const pool = [
    ...(searchResults || []),
    ...(otherPossibleResults || []),
    ...memories
  ];
  const m = pool.find(x => x.id === id);
  if (!m) return;
  editId = id;
  editOrigTtl = m.ttl_days ?? null;
  document.getElementById('modal-title').textContent = 'Edit Memory';
  document.getElementById('f-content').value = m.content || m.snippet || '';
  document.getElementById('f-type').value = m.memory_type || 'fact';
  document.getElementById('f-conf').value = m.confidence ?? 1.0;
  document.getElementById('f-tags').value = parseTags(m).join(', ');
  document.getElementById('f-ttl').value = m.ttl_days || '';
  document.getElementById('f-private').checked = Boolean(m.local_only);
  document.getElementById('backdrop').classList.add('open');
}

function closeModal() {
  document.getElementById('backdrop').classList.remove('open');
  editId = null;
  editOrigTtl = null;
}

async function saveMemory() {
  const content = document.getElementById('f-content').value.trim();
  if (!content) return notify('Content is required');
  const tags = document.getElementById('f-tags').value.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const ttlRaw = document.getElementById('f-ttl').value.trim();
  const parsedTtl = ttlRaw ? parseFloat(ttlRaw) : null;
  const ttlChanged = parsedTtl !== editOrigTtl;
  const confRaw = parseFloat(document.getElementById('f-conf').value);

  const payload = {
    content,
    memory_type: document.getElementById('f-type').value,
    confidence: Number.isFinite(confRaw) ? confRaw : 1.0,
    tags,
    local_only: document.getElementById('f-private').checked,
    namespace: ns
  };

  if (editId) {
    if (ttlChanged) {
      if (parsedTtl && parsedTtl > 0) {
        payload.ttl_days = parsedTtl;
      } else if (editOrigTtl !== null) {
        payload.clear_ttl = true;
      }
    }
  } else {
    if (parsedTtl && parsedTtl > 0) {
      payload.ttl_days = parsedTtl;
    }
  }

  const url = editId ? `/api/memories/${encodeURIComponent(editId)}` : '/api/memories';
  const method = editId ? 'PUT' : 'POST';

  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method,
    credentials: 'same-origin',
    headers: authHeaders({ 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }),
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  });

  if (res.ok) {
    const wasEdit = Boolean(editId);
    closeModal();
    searchResults = null;
    otherPossibleResults = [];
    searchFilterHint = null;
    document.getElementById('search-input').value = '';
    notify(wasEdit ? 'Memory updated' : 'Memory saved');
    await refreshAll();
  } else {
    let detail = '';
    try {
      const body = await res.json();
      const reason = body && (body.detail || body.error);
      if (reason) detail = `: ${typeof reason === 'string' ? reason : JSON.stringify(reason)}`;
    } catch { /* non-JSON error body */ }
    notify(`Failed to save memory${detail}`);
  }
}

async function approveMemory(id) {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/memories/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/approve?namespace=${encodeURIComponent(ns)}`, {
    method: 'POST',
    credentials: 'same-origin',
    headers: authHeaders()
  });
  if (res.ok) {
    notify('Memory approved');
    await refreshAll();
  } else {
    notify('Could not approve memory');
  }
}

async function delMemory(id) {
  if (!confirm('Delete this memory?')) return;
  const res = await fetch(`/api/memories/${encodeURIComponent(id)}?namespace=${encodeURIComponent(ns)}`, {
    method: 'DELETE',
    credentials: 'same-origin',
    headers: authHeaders()
  });
  if (res.ok) {
    memories = memories.filter(m => m.id !== id);
    if (searchResults) searchResults = searchResults.filter(m => m.id !== id);
    if (otherPossibleResults) otherPossibleResults = otherPossibleResults.filter(m => m.id !== id);
    renderMemories();
    notify('Memory deleted');
    refreshAll();
  }
}

async function loadConflicts() {
  const list = await apiFetch(`/api/contradictions?namespace=${encodeURIComponent(ns)}`) || [];
  const el = document.getElementById('conflicts-list');
  if (!list.length) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="empty">No unresolved conflicts in '${esc(ns)}'.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = `<div class="list">` + list.map(c => `<div class="row" style="flex-direction:column">
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;width:100%;align-items:center">
      <span class="row-meta"><span class="flag stale">conflict</span> similarity ${(c.similarity || 0).toFixed(2)} &middot; ${relTime(c.detected_at)}</span>
      <button class="btn" data-action="resolve-conflict" data-id="${escAttr(c.id)}">Mark Resolved</button>
    </div>
    <div class="conflict-grid" style="width:100%">
      <div class="conflict-box old"><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--subtle);margin-bottom:3px">EXISTING</div>${esc(c.existing_content)}</div>
      <div class="conflict-box new"><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--subtle);margin-bottom:3px">NEW</div>${esc(c.new_content)}</div>
    </div>
  </div>`).join('') + `</div>`;
}

async function resolveConflict(id) {
  const res = await fetch(`/api/contradictions/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/resolve?namespace=${encodeURIComponent(ns)}`, {
    method: 'POST',
    credentials: 'same-origin',
    headers: authHeaders()
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const detail = await res.text().catch(() => '');
    notify(`Could not resolve conflict (HTTP ${res.status})${detail ? ': ' + detail : ''}`);
    return;
  }
  notify('Conflict resolved');
  loadConflicts();
  refreshAll();
}

async function loadPatterns() {
  const data = await apiFetch(`/api/patterns?namespace=${encodeURIComponent(ns)}`);
  if (!data) return;

  const tEl = document.getElementById('patterns-topics');
  tEl.innerHTML = (data.top_topics || []).length
    ? `<div class="list">` + data.top_topics.map(t => `<div class="row">
        <span class="row-content">${esc(t.query_text)}</span>
        <span class="row-meta">${t.query_count} queries &middot; avg sim ${(t.avg_similarity || 0).toFixed(2)}</span>
      </div>`).join('') + `</div>`
    : `<div class="empty">No queries logged yet.</div>`;

  const gEl = document.getElementById('patterns-gaps');
  gEl.innerHTML = (data.memory_gaps || []).length
    ? `<div class="list">` + data.memory_gaps.map(g => `<div class="row">
        <span class="row-content">${esc(g.query_text)}</span>
        <span class="row-meta"><span class="flag stale">gap</span> asked ${g.query_count}x &middot; avg sim ${(g.avg_similarity || 0).toFixed(2)}</span>
      </div>`).join('') + `</div>`
    : `<div class="empty">No memory gaps detected.</div>`;

  const uEl = document.getElementById('patterns-unused');
  uEl.innerHTML = (data.unused_memories || []).length
    ? `<div class="list">` + data.unused_memories.map(m => `<div class="row">
        <span class="row-content">${esc(m.content)}</span>
        <button class="icon-btn del" title="Delete" data-action="delete" data-id="${escAttr(m.id)}">&#x2715;</button>
      </div>`).join('') + `</div>`
    : `<div class="empty">No unused memories.</div>`;
}

async function loadAudit(filter = 'all', btn = null) {
  activeAuditFilter = filter;
  if (btn) {
    document.querySelectorAll('#panel-audit .chip').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
    btn.classList.add('active');
  }
  const list = await apiFetch(`/api/audit?namespace=${encodeURIComponent(ns)}&limit=100&action=${encodeURIComponent(filter)}`) || [];
  const el = document.getElementById('audit-list');
  if (!list.length) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="empty">No audit activity recorded yet.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = `<div class="list">` + list.map(e => `<div class="row">
    <div class="row-main">
      <div class="row-top">
        <span class="type-pill">${esc(e.action)}</span>
        <span class="row-content">${esc(e.action === 'recall' ? e.query : e.content)}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="row-meta">
        <span>by ${esc(e.agent_id || 'agent')}</span>
        <span>${relTime(e.timestamp)}</span>
        ${e.top_result ? `<span>&rarr; "${esc(e.top_result.slice(0, 70))}"</span>` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`).join('') + `</div>`;
}

async function loadNamespaces() {
  const list = await apiFetch('/api/namespaces') || [];
  const el = document.getElementById('namespaces-list');
  if (!list.length) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="empty">No namespaces found.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  el.innerHTML = `<div class="list">` + list.map(n => `<div class="row" style="align-items:center">
    <div class="row-main">
      <div class="row-top">
        <span class="row-content" style="font-weight:600;cursor:pointer" data-action="switch-ns" data-id="${escAttr(n.namespace)}">${esc(n.namespace)}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="row-meta">
        <span>${n.memory_count} memories</span>
        <span>${n.local_only_count} private</span>
        <span>updated ${relTime(n.last_updated)}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:6px">
      <button class="btn" data-action="export-ns" data-id="${escAttr(n.namespace)}">Export</button>
      <button class="btn btn-danger" data-action="delete-ns" data-id="${escAttr(n.namespace)}">Delete</button>
    </div>
  </div>`).join('') + `</div>`;
}

async function exportNs(targetNs) {
  const data = await apiFetch(`/api/export/${encodeURIComponent(targetNs)}?include_embeddings=false`);
  if (!data) return;
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = `mesh_${targetNs}.json`;
  a.click();
  notify(`Exported ${data.memory_count} memories`);
}

async function deleteNs(targetNs) {
  if (!confirm(`Delete namespace '${targetNs}' and all its memories?`)) return;
  const res = await fetch(`/api/namespaces/${encodeURIComponent(targetNs)}`, {
    method: 'DELETE',
    credentials: 'same-origin',
    headers: authHeaders()
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const detail = await res.text().catch(() => '');
    notify(`Could not delete ${targetNs} (HTTP ${res.status})${detail ? ': ' + detail : ''}`);
    return;
  }
  if (targetNs === ns) {
    ns = '';
    searchResults = null;
    otherPossibleResults = [];
    searchFilterHint = null;
  }
  notify(`Deleted ${targetNs}`);
  await loadNamespaces();
  await refreshAll();
}

async function loadDigest() {
  const d = await apiFetch(`/api/digest?namespace=${encodeURIComponent(ns)}&hours=24`);
  const el = document.getElementById('digest-list');
  if (!d) return;
  if (!d.new_count && !d.contradiction_count && !d.stale_count) {
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="empty">All clear &mdash; no new activity or stale memories in the last 24h.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  const items = (d.new_memories || []).map(m => `<div class="row">
    <div class="row-main">
      <div class="row-top"><span class="type-pill ${esc(m.memory_type)}">${esc(m.memory_type)}</span><span class="row-content">${esc(m.content)}</span></div>
      <div class="row-meta"><span>by ${esc(m.source_agent)}</span><span>${relTime(m.created_at)}</span></div>
    </div>
  </div>`).join('');
  el.innerHTML = `<div class="section-label">Last 24h: ${d.new_count} new &middot; ${d.total_recalls} recalls &middot; ${d.contradiction_count} conflicts</div>` +
    (items ? `<div class="list">${items}</div>` : '');
}

function showTab(name, btn) {
  activeTab = name;
  document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('panel-' + name).classList.add('active');
  btn.classList.add('active');
  if (name === 'conflicts') loadConflicts();
  if (name === 'patterns') loadPatterns();
  if (name === 'audit') loadAudit(activeAuditFilter);
  if (name === 'namespaces') loadNamespaces();
  if (name === 'digest') loadDigest();
}

function esc(s) {
  return String(s ?? '')
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}

function escAttr(s) {
  return esc(s).replace(/\\/g, '\\\\');
}

function relTime(s) {
  if (!s || s === 'never') return 'never';
  const d = new Date(s);
  if (isNaN(d)) return String(s).slice(0, 10);
  const sec = (Date.now() - d.getTime()) / 1000;
  if (sec < 60) return 'just now';
  if (sec < 3600) return Math.floor(sec / 60) + 'm ago';
  if (sec < 86400) return Math.floor(sec / 3600) + 'h ago';
  if (sec < 604800) return Math.floor(sec / 86400) + 'd ago';
  return d.toLocaleDateString();
}

function notify(msg) {
  const t = document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.style.display = 'block';
  clearTimeout(t._timer);
  t._timer = setTimeout(() => { t.style.display = 'none'; }, 2400);
}

document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  const activeTag = document.activeElement ? document.activeElement.tagName : '';
  if (e.key === '/' && activeTag !== 'INPUT' && activeTag !== 'TEXTAREA' && activeTag !== 'SELECT') {
    e.preventDefault();
    document.getElementById('search-input').focus();
  } else if (e.key === 'n' && !e.metaKey && !e.ctrlKey && activeTag !== 'INPUT' && activeTag !== 'TEXTAREA' && activeTag !== 'SELECT') {
    e.preventDefault();
    openModal();
  } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
    closeModal();
  }
});
</script>
</body>
</html>"""


@api.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
def dashboard(request: Request) -> HTMLResponse:
    """
    Serve the Dashboard UI (Finding S9).
    Never embeds `~/.mesh/auth_token` into HTML responses or cookies.
    If the caller presents a valid `?code=` one-time launch nonce, header token, or existing
    session cookie, sets an `HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict` opaque session cookie (`mesh_dash_session`)
    and injects the paired `X-Mesh-CSRF` token into the HTML `<meta name="mesh-csrf">` tag.
    """
    expected_token = get_or_create_auth_token()
    header_tok = _extract_header_token(request)
    cookie_val = (request.cookies.get(_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME) or "").strip()
    existing_csrf = _validate_dashboard_session(cookie_val) if cookie_val else None

    code = (request.query_params.get("code") or "").strip()
    nonce_ok = bool(code and _consume_launch_nonce(code))
    header_ok = bool(header_tok and _tokens_match(header_tok, expected_token))

    has_auth_override = verify_dashboard_auth in api.dependency_overrides
    is_authenticated = has_auth_override or nonce_ok or header_ok or (existing_csrf is not None)

    new_session_id: Optional[str] = None
    csrf_token = existing_csrf or ""
    if is_authenticated and existing_csrf is None:
        new_session_id, csrf_token = _create_dashboard_session()

    rendered_html = DASHBOARD_HTML.replace("__MESH_CSRF_TOKEN__", csrf_token)
    response = HTMLResponse(content=rendered_html, status_code=200)
    if new_session_id is not None:
        response.set_cookie(
            key=_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME,
            value=new_session_id,
            httponly=True,
            samesite="strict",
            path="/",
        )
    return response


def run(
    namespace: Optional[str] = None,
    host: Optional[str] = None,
    port: Optional[int] = None,
    no_browser: Optional[bool] = None,
    yes: Optional[bool] = None,
) -> None:
    """Start the Mesh dashboard at http://localhost:<port>[cite: 2]"""
    global _mesh, _default_namespace, _ACTIVE_BIND_HOST, _ACTIVE_BIND_PORT
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Start the Mesh web dashboard.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--namespace",
        default=os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE", "shared"),
        help="Mesh namespace to inspect (default: shared)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--host",
        default="127.0.0.1",
        help="Host interface to bind (default: 127.0.0.1)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--port",
        type=int,
        default=7433,
        help="Port to run on (default: 7433)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-browser",
        action="store_true",
        help="Don't auto-open the browser",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--yes",
        "-y",
        action="store_true",
        help="Skip interactive y/n permission prompts",
    )
    args, _ = parser.parse_known_args()

    resolved_ns = namespace if namespace is not None else args.namespace
    resolved_host = (host if host is not None else args.host).strip()
    resolved_port = int(port if port is not None else args.port)
    resolved_no_browser = bool(no_browser if no_browser is not None else args.no_browser)
    resolved_yes = bool(yes if yes is not None else args.yes)

    clean_ns = validate_namespace(resolved_ns)
    _default_namespace = clean_ns
    _ACTIVE_BIND_HOST = resolved_host.lower()
    _ACTIVE_BIND_PORT = resolved_port

    env_allowed = os.environ.get("MESH_ALLOWED_HOSTS", "").strip()
    if _ACTIVE_BIND_HOST not in ("127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1", "[::1]"):
        if not env_allowed:
            err_msg = (
                f"Refusing to start dashboard on non-loopback bind '{resolved_host}' without MESH_ALLOWED_HOSTS specified.\n"
                f"Set MESH_ALLOWED_HOSTS to comma-separated allowed hostnames/IPs to confirm.\n"
            )
            sys.stderr.write(err_msg)
            raise ValueError(err_msg)
        else:
            sys.stderr.write(
                f"[WARNING] Binding Mesh dashboard to '{resolved_host}' with MESH_ALLOWED_HOSTS={env_allowed}. "
                "Traffic is unencrypted plain HTTP protected only by bearer-token authentication.\n"
            )

    get_or_create_auth_token()
    db_file = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve() / f"mesh_{clean_ns}.db"
    if db_file.exists():
        _mesh = Mesh(namespace=clean_ns, agent_id="dashboard", create=False, trusted_local=True)

    from .memory import _has_local_onnx_pair

    if _has_local_onnx_pair() is None:
        if request_user_permission(
            "download the ~90MB ONNX embedding model and start the background warmup worker for the Dashboard",
            assume_yes=resolved_yes,
        ):
            threading.Thread(target=warmup_model, args=(clean_ns, False, True), daemon=True).start()
    else:
        threading.Thread(target=warmup_model, args=(clean_ns, False, False), daemon=True).start()

    nonce = secrets.token_urlsafe(16)
    _register_launch_nonce(nonce)
    launch_url = f"http://localhost:{resolved_port}/?ns={clean_ns}&code={nonce}"
    display_url = (
        launch_url
        if (hasattr(sys.stdout, "isatty") and sys.stdout.isatty())
        else f"http://localhost:{resolved_port}/?ns={clean_ns}"
    )

    from . import ui

    ui.header("Dashboard", f"namespace {clean_ns}")
    print()
    ui.kv([("Open", ui.style(display_url, "info")), ("Browser", "opening automatically" if not resolved_no_browser else "not opened (--no-browser)")])
    print()
    ui.hint("Press Ctrl+C to stop", stream=sys.stdout)
    print()

    if not resolved_no_browser:
        def open_browser() -> None:
            time.sleep(1.0)
            webbrowser.open(launch_url)

        threading.Thread(target=open_browser, daemon=True).start()

    uvicorn.run(api, host=resolved_host, port=resolved_port, log_level="error")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()

r"""
mesh/http_server.py — High-speed HTTP REST API & Background Daemon Server for Mesh.

Runs on 127.0.0.1:7701 by default. Separate from the dashboard (port 7433).

Addresses Findings:
  - S1: Supports `secret_ref` locator metadata and `vault_secrets` on `/learn` and `/update`
  - S6: Enforces server-side `MESH_MCP_INCLUDE_PRIVATE=0` lock (cannot be overridden by request
        `include_private=true`), blocks unauthorized `local_only` downgrades, and enforces per-agent scopes
  - S8: Supports `trust_tier`, `quarantined`, `include_quarantined`, and `/approve` endpoint
  - S9: Supports live-reloaded auth tokens and optional Unix domain socket (`MESH_UNIX_SOCKET`)
  - S11: Enforces `guard_ingest` and `guard_tags` on `/learn` and `/update` (including `content=None`)
  - N4: Enforces request body size limits (`_MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES`) across all endpoints including `/mcp`,
        and prevents `agent_id` rotation from evicting other namespaces in `_MESH_REGISTRY`
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import hmac
import json

import os

import sqlite3

from collections import OrderedDict
from pathlib import Path

    _get_audit_db_path,

    get_audit_log,
    reject_symlink,
    validate_namespace,
)

_LOCAL_ORIGIN_REGEX = r"^https?://(127\.0\.0\.1|localhost|\[::1\])(:\d+)?$"

_MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES = 262144

# Whitelisted context keys accepted by `/mcp` (Issue H4).
# Security-sensitive keys (`enforce_agent_ownership`, auth/token/permission/privacy/sync flags)
# are never trusted from the request body and are derived strictly from server configuration.
_ALLOWED_MCP_CONTEXT_KEYS = {"workspace_root"}


def _is_private_locked_off() -> bool:
    """
    Finding S6: Return True when `MESH_MCP_INCLUDE_PRIVATE` is explicitly set to a falsy
    value (`0`, `false`, `no`) or `MESH_BLOCK_PRIVATE_OVERRIDE=1` is set on the server.
    When True, callers cannot override `include_private` to `True` via request parameters.
    """
    if env_bool("MESH_BLOCK_PRIVATE_OVERRIDE", False):
        return True
    raw = os.environ.get("MESH_MCP_INCLUDE_PRIVATE")
    if raw is not None and raw.strip().lower() in ("0", "false", "no"):
        return True
    return False


def _resolve_http_include_private(requested: Optional[bool]) -> bool:
    """
    Resolve `include_private` for HTTP routes (Finding S6):
      - If the server has locked off private access (`MESH_MCP_INCLUDE_PRIVATE=0`), always returns `False`.
      - If `requested is None`, defaults to `env_bool("MESH_MCP_INCLUDE_PRIVATE", False)`.
      - Otherwise returns `bool(requested)`.
    """
    if _is_private_locked_off():
        return False
    if requested is None:
        return env_bool("MESH_MCP_INCLUDE_PRIVATE", False)
    return bool(requested)


def _tokens_match(provided: str, expected: str) -> bool:
    if not provided or not expected:
        return False
    try:
        return hmac.compare_digest(
            provided.encode("utf-8", errors="surrogatepass"),
            expected.encode("utf-8", errors="surrogatepass"),
        )
    except Exception:
        return False


def _extract_hostname(host_header: str) -> str:
    h = (host_header or "").strip().lower()
    if not h:
        return ""
    if h.startswith("["):
        end = h.find("]")
        return h[: end + 1] if end != -1 else h
    return h.split(":")[0]


def verify_request_auth(request: Request) -> None:
    """
    Verify the local Mesh authentication token on protected endpoints using `hmac.compare_digest`[cite: 2].
    Can be overridden in unit tests via `app.dependency_overrides[verify_request_auth] = lambda: None`[cite: 2].
    """
    expected_token = get_or_create_auth_token()
    auth_header = request.headers.get("authorization", "")
    provided_token = ""
    if auth_header.lower().startswith("bearer "):
        provided_token = auth_header[7:].strip()
    if not provided_token:
        provided_token = request.headers.get("x-mesh-token", "").strip()

    if not provided_token or not _tokens_match(provided_token, expected_token):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=401,
            detail="Unauthorized: missing or invalid Mesh authentication token.",
        )


def _raise_http_error(exc: BaseException, endpoint: str) -> NoReturn:
    """
    Centralized exception-to-HTTPException mapper for HTTP routes (Issue H5)[cite: 2]:
      - HTTPException -> re-raised unchanged
      - NamespaceLimitExceededError -> 429
      - NamespaceNotFoundError / FileNotFoundError / missing object ValueError -> 404
      - Authorization / scope / read-only ValueError -> 403
      - Conflict / lock timeout / busy database / already exists -> 409
      - Other ValueError (validation / malformed input) -> 400
      - Unexpected Exception -> logged server-side and returned as generic 500
    """
    if isinstance(exc, HTTPException):
        raise exc
    if isinstance(exc, NamespaceLimitExceededError):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, (NamespaceNotFoundError, FileNotFoundError)):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, TimeoutError):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
            detail="Conflict: namespace is currently locked by another operation.",
        ) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, ValueError):
        msg = str(exc)
        msg_low = msg.lower()
        if "not found" in msg_low or "does not exist" in msg_low:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=msg) from exc
        if "already exists" in msg_low:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=msg) from exc
        if "not authorized" in msg_low or "read-only" in msg_low:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=msg) from exc
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=msg) from exc
    if isinstance(exc, (RuntimeError, sqlite3.OperationalError, OSError)):
        msg_low = str(exc).lower()
        if any(
            marker in msg_low
            for marker in ("locked", "busy", "checkpoint", "another process", "hold", "open")
        ):
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=409,
                detail="Conflict: another process holds the namespace database open.",
            ) from exc
    logger.exception("Unhandled error in HTTP endpoint '%s': %s", endpoint, exc)
    raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Internal server error.") from exc


app = FastAPI(
    title="Mesh HTTP API",
    description="Local HTTP interface for the Mesh shared agent memory layer.",
    version=VERSION,
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=[
        "http://127.0.0.1:7433",
        "http://localhost:7433",
        "http://127.0.0.1:7701",
        "http://localhost:7701",
    ],
    allow_origin_regex=_LOCAL_ORIGIN_REGEX,
    allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"],
    allow_headers=["Content-Type", "Authorization", "X-Mesh-Token"],
)


@app.middleware("http")
async def _security_middleware(request: Request, call_next: Any) -> Any:
    """
    1. Validate `Host` header to block DNS rebinding attacks[cite: 2].
    2. Validate `Origin` header (if present) to block cross-site requests from arbitrary websites[cite: 2].
    3. Enforce `Content-Length` bound (`_MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES`) before reading body (Finding N4).
    4. Verify `~/.mesh/auth_token` on all endpoints except `/health`, `/identity`, and `OPTIONS` preflights[cite: 2].
    """
    configured_host = os.environ.get("MESH_HTTP_HOST", _ACTIVE_BIND_HOST).strip().lower()
    allowed_hosts = set(_ALLOWED_HOSTS)
    if configured_host not in ("0.0.0.0", "::", "[::]"):  # nosec B104
        allowed_hosts.add(configured_host)
    if _ACTIVE_BIND_HOST not in ("0.0.0.0", "::", "[::]"):  # nosec B104
        allowed_hosts.add(_ACTIVE_BIND_HOST)

    custom_hosts: set[str] = set()
    env_allowed = os.environ.get("MESH_ALLOWED_HOSTS", "").strip()
    if env_allowed:
        for item in env_allowed.split(","):
            ci = _extract_hostname(item.strip().lower())
            if ci:
                allowed_hosts.add(ci)
                custom_hosts.add(ci)

    has_auth_override = verify_request_auth in app.dependency_overrides
    if has_auth_override:
        allowed_hosts.add("testserver")

    req_host = _extract_hostname(request.headers.get("host", ""))
    if req_host and req_host not in allowed_hosts:
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=403,
            content={"detail": f"Forbidden Host header '{req_host}' (DNS rebinding protection)."},
        )

    origin = request.headers.get("origin")
    if origin:
        parsed_origin = urlparse(origin)
        origin_host = (parsed_origin.hostname or "").lower()
        is_valid_local = bool(re.match(_LOCAL_ORIGIN_REGEX, origin))
        is_valid_custom = origin_host in custom_hosts and parsed_origin.scheme in ("http", "https")
        if origin_host not in allowed_hosts or not (is_valid_local or is_valid_custom):
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=403,
                content={"detail": "Forbidden cross-origin request."},
            )

    # Finding N4: Reject oversized Content-Length upfront across all routes
    content_len_hdr = request.headers.get("content-length")
    if content_len_hdr and request.url.path != "/identity":
        try:
            if int(content_len_hdr) > _MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES:
                return JSONResponse(
                    status_code=413,
                    content={"detail": f"Request body exceeds maximum allowed size ({_MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES} bytes)."},
                )
        except ValueError:
            return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"detail": "Invalid Content-Length header."})

    if request.method == "OPTIONS" or request.url.path in ("/health", "/identity") or has_auth_override:
        return await call_next(request)

    try:
        verify_request_auth(request)
    except HTTPException as exc:
        return JSONResponse(status_code=exc.status_code, content={"detail": exc.detail})
    return await call_next(request)


_MESH_LOCK = threading.RLock()
_MESH_REGISTRY: OrderedDict[tuple[str, str], Mesh] = OrderedDict()
_MESH_REGISTRY_MAX = 64


def _get_registry_max() -> int:
    if os.environ.get("MESH_MAX_OPEN_NAMESPACES"):
        return env_int("MESH_MAX_OPEN_NAMESPACES", _MESH_REGISTRY_MAX, minimum=2)
    return max(2, int(_MESH_REGISTRY_MAX))


def evict_http_mesh_cache(namespace: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
    """Evict cached `Mesh` instances for `namespace` (or all namespaces if None)[cite: 2]."""
    with _MESH_LOCK:
        if namespace is None:
            _MESH_REGISTRY.clear()
            return
        stale_keys = [k for k in _MESH_REGISTRY if k[0] == namespace]
        for k in stale_keys:
            _MESH_REGISTRY.pop(k, None)


def _is_cached_mesh_valid(mesh: Mesh, expected_ns: str) -> bool:
    try:
        return (
            mesh.namespace == expected_ns
            and mesh.store.namespace == expected_ns
            and mesh.store.db_path.exists()
            and mesh.store.db_path.stat().st_size > 0
        )
    except OSError:
        return False


def get_mesh(
    namespace: Optional[str] = None,
    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,
    create: bool = True,
) -> Mesh:
    """
    Return a cached `Mesh` instance for the given namespace and agent_id.
    - Finding N4: Prevents `agent_id` rotation within a single namespace from evicting
      other active namespaces from `_MESH_REGISTRY` and `store._VECTOR_CACHE`.
      If `ns` already has an entry in `_MESH_REGISTRY` when capacity is reached, evicts
      the oldest `(ns, old_aid)` entry for the *same* namespace first without dropping
      the namespace's underlying SQLite connection or vector cache.
    """
    raw_ns = (namespace or os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE", "shared")).strip() or "shared"
    ns = validate_namespace(raw_ns)
    aid = (agent_id or os.environ.get("MESH_AGENT_ID", "http-client")).strip() or "http-client"
    key = (ns, aid)

    stale_to_clean: list[tuple[str, Any]] = []
    with _MESH_LOCK:
        mesh = _MESH_REGISTRY.get(key)
        if mesh is not None:
            if not _is_cached_mesh_valid(mesh, ns):
                _MESH_REGISTRY.pop(key, None)
                stale_to_clean.append((ns, mesh.store.db_path))
                mesh = None
            else:
                if create and not mesh.store.create:
                    mesh.store.create = True
                _MESH_REGISTRY.move_to_end(key)
                return mesh

    for ev_ns, ev_path in stale_to_clean:
        _evict_namespace_caches(ev_ns, ev_path)

    if not create:
        db_file = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve() / f"mesh_{ns}.db"
        if not db_file.exists():
            raise NamespaceNotFoundError(f"Namespace '{ns}' does not exist.")

    evicted_namespaces: list[tuple[str, Any]] = []
    with _MESH_LOCK:
        mesh = _MESH_REGISTRY.get(key)
        if mesh is not None and _is_cached_mesh_valid(mesh, ns):
            if create and not mesh.store.create:
                mesh.store.create = True
            _MESH_REGISTRY.move_to_end(key)
            return mesh

        mesh = Mesh(namespace=ns, agent_id=aid, create=create)
        if mesh.store.db_path.exists() or create:
            max_reg = _get_registry_max()
            # Finding N4: Cap per-namespace agent_id entries so rotating agent_ids on one
            # namespace never evicts other namespaces from the registry.
            same_ns_keys = [cand_k for cand_k in _MESH_REGISTRY if cand_k[0] == ns and cand_k != key]
            per_ns_cap = max(1, max_reg // 2)
            while len(same_ns_keys) >= per_ns_cap:
                old_same_key = same_ns_keys.pop(0)
                _MESH_REGISTRY.pop(old_same_key, None)

            while len(_MESH_REGISTRY) >= max_reg:
                victim_key: Optional[tuple[str, str]] = None
                # If this namespace already has an entry in the registry, evict its own older
                # agent_id entry first so other namespaces are not thrash-evicted (Finding N4).
                for cand_key in _MESH_REGISTRY:
                    if cand_key[0] == ns and cand_key != key:
                        victim_key = cand_key
                        break
                if victim_key is None:
                    for cand_key in _MESH_REGISTRY:
                        if cand_key[0] != ns:
                            victim_key = cand_key
                            break
                if victim_key is None:
                    break
                victim_mesh = _MESH_REGISTRY.pop(victim_key, None)
                victim_ns = victim_key[0]
                if (
                    victim_mesh is not None
                    and victim_ns != ns
                    and not any(k[0] == victim_ns for k in _MESH_REGISTRY)
                ):
                    evicted_namespaces.append((victim_ns, victim_mesh.store.db_path))
            _MESH_REGISTRY[key] = mesh
            _MESH_REGISTRY.move_to_end(key)

    for ev_ns, ev_path in evicted_namespaces:
        _evict_namespace_caches(ev_ns, ev_path)
    return mesh


# --- Request Models ---


class LearnRequest(BaseModel):
    content: str = Field(..., max_length=65536)
    memory_type: Optional[str] = Field(default="fact", max_length=64)
    confidence: Optional[float] = 1.0
    tags: Optional[list[str]] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=64)
    local_only: Optional[bool] = False
    ttl_days: Optional[float] = None
    namespace: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=64)
    agent_id: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=128)
    secret_ref: Optional[dict[str, Any] | str] = None
    vault_secrets: Optional[bool] = False
    quarantined: Optional[bool] = False

    @field_validator("tags")
    @classmethod
    def _validate_tags(cls, v: Optional[list[str]]) -> Optional[list[str]]:
        if v is not None:
            for item in v:
                if len(str(item)) > 128:
                    raise ValueError("Individual tag length cannot exceed 128 characters.")
        return v


class RecallRequest(BaseModel):
    query: str = Field(..., max_length=16384)
    count: Optional[int] = Field(default=5, ge=1, le=200)
    n: Optional[int] = Field(default=None, ge=1, le=200)
    memory_type: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=64)
    tags: Optional[list[str]] = Field(default=None, max_length=64)
    source_agent: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=128)
    min_similarity: Optional[float] = 0.0
    min_confidence: Optional[float] = 0.0
    include_stale: Optional[bool] = True
    include_private: Optional[bool] = None
    include_quarantined: Optional[bool] = False
    include_other_possible: Optional[bool] = True
    max_other_possible: Optional[int] = Field(default=5, ge=0, le=50)
    namespace: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=64)
    agent_id: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=128)

    @field_validator("tags")
    @classmethod
    def _validate_tags(cls, v: Optional[list[str]]) -> Optional[list[str]]:
        if v is not None:
            for item in v:
                if len(str(item)) > 128:
                    raise ValueError("Individual tag length cannot exceed 128 characters.")
        return v

    namespace: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, max_length=64)

    quarantined: Optional[bool] = None

    @field_validator("tags")

# --- Endpoints (Synchronous `def` so FastAPI runs them in a worker threadpool) ---


@app.get("/health")
def health() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Unauthenticated health check returning only status and version (no PID or engine details)[cite: 2]."""
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "version": VERSION,
    }


@app.api_route("/identity", methods=["GET", "POST"])
async def identity(request: Request, nonce: Optional[str] = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Challenge-response authentication verification for the local Mesh daemon[cite: 2].
    Allows clients to cryptographically verify daemon identity before transmitting auth tokens[cite: 2].
    """
    raw_nonce: Optional[str] = None
    if nonce is not None:
        raw_nonce = nonce.strip()
    elif "nonce" in request.query_params:
        raw_nonce = (request.query_params.get("nonce") or "").strip()

    if raw_nonce is None and request.method == "POST":
        content_len_hdr = request.headers.get("content-length")
        if content_len_hdr:
            try:
                if int(content_len_hdr) > _MAX_IDENTITY_BODY_BYTES:
                    raise HTTPException(
                        status_code=400,
                        detail=f"Identity request body exceeds {_MAX_IDENTITY_BODY_BYTES} bytes.",
                    )
            except ValueError:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid Content-Length header.")
        raw_bytes = await request.body()
        if len(raw_bytes) > _MAX_IDENTITY_BODY_BYTES:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=400,
                detail=f"Identity request body exceeds {_MAX_IDENTITY_BODY_BYTES} bytes.",
            )
        if raw_bytes:
            try:
                body = json.loads(raw_bytes.decode("utf-8"))
            except Exception:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Malformed JSON body on /identity.")
            if isinstance(body, dict) and "nonce" in body:
                val = body.get("nonce")
                if not isinstance(val, str):
                    raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Challenge nonce must be a string.")
                raw_nonce = val.strip()

    if raw_nonce is None and "x-mesh-nonce" in request.headers:
        raw_nonce = request.headers.get("x-mesh-nonce", "").strip()

    if not raw_nonce:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Missing challenge nonce.")

    if len(raw_nonce) > 128 or not _VALID_NONCE_RE.fullmatch(raw_nonce):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Invalid challenge nonce: must be 1-128 characters matching ^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$.",
        )

    try:
        nonce_bytes = bytes.fromhex(raw_nonce)
    except ValueError:
        nonce_bytes = raw_nonce.encode("utf-8")

    token = get_or_create_auth_token()
    proof = hmac.new(token.encode("utf-8"), nonce_bytes, hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "proof": proof,
        "pid": os.getpid(),
        "version": VERSION,
    }


@app.get("/engine", dependencies=[Depends(verify_request_auth)])
def engine_status() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return current embedding engine and idle-unload status[cite: 2]."""
    return {"status": "ok", "engine": get_engine_status()}


@app.post("/warmup", dependencies=[Depends(verify_request_auth)])
def warmup_endpoint(req: WarmupRequest) -> Any:
    """
    Warm the embedding model and heal pending embeddings across requested namespaces[cite: 2].
    """
    target_namespaces: list[str] = []
    if req.namespaces:
        target_namespaces.extend(str(n).strip() for n in req.namespaces if str(n).strip())
    if req.namespace and req.namespace.strip():
        if req.namespace.strip() not in target_namespaces:
            target_namespaces.append(req.namespace.strip())
    if not target_namespaces:
        default_ns = (os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE") or "shared").strip() or "shared"
        target_namespaces.append(default_ns)

    if len(target_namespaces) == 1 and not req.namespaces:
        try:
            validate_namespace(target_namespaces[0])
        except ValueError as e:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))

    per_namespace: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    errors: list[dict[str, str]] = []
    succeeded = 0
    failed = 0

    for raw_ns in target_namespaces:
        try:
            clean_ns = validate_namespace(raw_ns)
            res = warmup_model(namespace=clean_ns, blocking=bool(req.blocking), assume_yes=False)
            if isinstance(res, dict) and res.get("status") == "error":
                failed += 1
                err_msg = str(res.get("error") or f"Warmup failed for namespace '{clean_ns}'.")
                per_namespace.append({"namespace": clean_ns, "status": "error", "error": err_msg})
                errors.append({"namespace": clean_ns, "error": err_msg})
            else:
                succeeded += 1
                ns_exists = bool(res.get("namespace_exists")) if isinstance(res, dict) else False
                per_namespace.append(
                    {"namespace": clean_ns, "status": "ok", "namespace_exists": ns_exists}
                )
        except Exception as exc:
            failed += 1
            logger.exception("Warmup failed for namespace '%s': %s", raw_ns, exc)
            err_summary = str(exc) if isinstance(exc, ValueError) else f"Failed warming namespace '{raw_ns}'."
            per_namespace.append({"namespace": raw_ns, "status": "error", "error": err_summary})
            errors.append({"namespace": raw_ns, "error": err_summary})

    total = len(target_namespaces)
    if failed == 0:
        overall_status = "ok"
        http_code = 200
    elif succeeded > 0:
        overall_status = "partial"
        http_code = 207
    else:
        overall_status = "error"
        http_code = 500

    payload = {
        "status": overall_status,
        "total": total,
        "succeeded": succeeded,
        "failed": failed,
        "namespaces": per_namespace,
        "errors": errors,
        "engine": get_engine_status(),
    }
    return JSONResponse(status_code=http_code, content=payload)


@app.post("/unload", dependencies=[Depends(verify_request_auth)])
def unload_engine() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Explicitly unload the neural embedding model from RAM to free memory immediately[cite: 2]."""
    unloaded = unload_model()
    return {"status": "ok", "unloaded": unloaded, "engine": get_engine_status()}


def _sanitize_mcp_context(raw_context: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Whitelist allowed `/mcp` context keys (Issue H4)[cite: 2].
    Drops all security-sensitive keys (`enforce_agent_ownership`, auth/token/permission/privacy/sync flags)
    so ownership enforcement and permissions are derived strictly from server configuration[cite: 2].
    """
    if not isinstance(raw_context, dict):
        return {}
    sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for k, v in raw_context.items():
        if k in _ALLOWED_MCP_CONTEXT_KEYS and isinstance(v, str) and len(v) <= 1024:
            sanitized[k] = v
    return sanitized


def _resolve_mcp_agent_id(request: Request, caller_agent_id: Optional[str]) -> str:
    """
    Bind `agent_id` to an authenticated identity if configured (`request.state.agent_id`
    or `MESH_AUTHENTICATED_AGENT_ID`); otherwise treat `caller_agent_id` as an unverified label (Issue H4)[cite: 2].
    """
    state_agent = getattr(request.state, "agent_id", None) or getattr(
        request.state, "authenticated_agent_id", None
    )
    if isinstance(state_agent, str) and state_agent.strip():
        return re.sub(r"[\x00-\x1f\x7f]+", "", state_agent).strip()[:64] or "mcp-agent"
    env_bound = (os.environ.get("MESH_AUTHENTICATED_AGENT_ID") or "").strip()
    if env_bound:
        return re.sub(r"[\x00-\x1f\x7f]+", "", env_bound).strip()[:64] or "mcp-agent"
    raw = (
        caller_agent_id
        if (caller_agent_id is not None and str(caller_agent_id).strip())
        else (os.environ.get("MESH_AGENT_ID") or "mcp-agent")
    )
    return re.sub(r"[\x00-\x1f\x7f]+", "", str(raw)).strip()[:64] or "mcp-agent"


@app.post("/mcp", dependencies=[Depends(verify_request_auth)])
async def mcp_proxy(request: Request) -> Any:
    """
    Execute an MCP tool request in the shared daemon process (Issues H4, H6, N4)[cite: 2]:
      - Enforces `_MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES` before parsing JSON (Finding N4)
      - Protocol/parse errors or unknown tools -> JSON-RPC `error` object
      - Tool execution failures -> MCP result with `isError: true` and explanatory `content` block
      - Whitelists `context` keys and derives `enforce_agent_ownership` from server configuration
    """
    from .mcp_server import _MCP_TOOLS, _execute_tool_sync

    try:
        raw_body = await request.body()
        if not raw_body:
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=400,
                content={
                    "jsonrpc": "2.0",
                    "id": None,
                    "error": {"code": -32700, "message": "Parse error: empty request body."},
                    "detail": "Parse error: empty request body.",
                },
            )
        if len(raw_body) > _MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES:
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=413,
                content={
                    "jsonrpc": "2.0",
                    "id": None,
                    "error": {
                        "code": -32600,
                        "message": f"Request body exceeds maximum allowed size ({_MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES} bytes).",
                    },
                    "detail": f"Request body exceeds maximum allowed size ({_MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES} bytes).",
                },
            )
        try:
            body_data = json.loads(raw_body.decode("utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=400,
                content={
                    "jsonrpc": "2.0",
                    "id": None,
                    "error": {"code": -32700, "message": "Parse error: invalid JSON."},
                    "detail": "Parse error: invalid JSON.",
                },
            )
        if not isinstance(body_data, dict):
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=400,
                content={
                    "jsonrpc": "2.0",
                    "id": None,
                    "error": {"code": -32600, "message": "Invalid Request: expected JSON object."},
                    "detail": "Invalid Request: expected JSON object.",
                },
            )

        req_id = body_data.get("id")
        raw_params = body_data.get("params")
        params: dict[str, Any] = raw_params if isinstance(raw_params, dict) else {}
        tool_name = str(body_data.get("name") or params.get("name") or "").strip()
        arguments = body_data.get("arguments") if "arguments" in body_data else params.get("arguments", {})
        default_ns = body_data.get("default_namespace") or params.get("default_namespace")
        raw_agent_id = body_data.get("agent_id") or params.get("agent_id")
        raw_context = body_data.get("context") if "context" in body_data else params.get("context", {})

        if not tool_name:
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=400,
                content={
                    "jsonrpc": "2.0",
                    "id": req_id,
                    "error": {"code": -32600, "message": "Invalid Request: missing tool 'name'."},
                    "detail": "Invalid Request: missing tool 'name'.",
                },
            )
        if not isinstance(arguments, dict):
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=400,
                content={
                    "jsonrpc": "2.0",
                    "id": req_id,
                    "error": {"code": -32602, "message": "Invalid params: 'arguments' must be an object."},
                    "detail": "Invalid params: 'arguments' must be an object.",
                },
            )

        known_tools = {t.name for t in _MCP_TOOLS}
        if tool_name not in known_tools:
            err_payload = {"error": f"Unknown tool: {tool_name}", "status": "error"}
            text_block = {"type": "text", "text": json.dumps(err_payload, indent=2)}
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=200,
                content={
                    "jsonrpc": "2.0",
                    "id": req_id,
                    "status": "error",
                    "isError": True,
                    "error": {"code": -32601, "message": f"Unknown tool: {tool_name}"},
                    "content": [text_block],
                    "result": {"isError": True, "content": [text_block]},
                    "payload": err_payload,
                },
            )

        if default_ns:
            validate_namespace(str(default_ns))
        if arguments.get("namespace"):
            validate_namespace(str(arguments["namespace"]))

        safe_context = _sanitize_mcp_context(raw_context)
        bound_agent_id = _resolve_mcp_agent_id(request, raw_agent_id)

        payload = _execute_tool_sync(
            name=tool_name,
            arguments=arguments,
            default_namespace=default_ns,
            agent_id=bound_agent_id,
            allow_daemon_proxy=False,
            context=safe_context,
        )

        is_tool_error = False
        if isinstance(payload, dict):
            if payload.get("status") in ("rejected", "error") or (
                payload.get("error") is not None and payload.get("status") != "synchronized"
            ):
                is_tool_error = True

        text_block = {"type": "text", "text": json.dumps(payload, indent=2)}
        return {
            "jsonrpc": "2.0",
            "id": req_id,
            "status": "error" if is_tool_error else "ok",
            "isError": is_tool_error,
            "content": [text_block],
            "result": {
                "isError": is_tool_error,
                "content": [text_block],
            },
            "payload": payload,
        }
    except ValueError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))
    except Exception as e:
        _raise_http_error(e, "/mcp")


@app.post("/learn", dependencies=[Depends(verify_request_auth)])
def learn(req: LearnRequest) -> Any:
    """Store a memory. Runs `guard_ingest` & `guard_tags` and returns the memory ID and any contradiction warnings."""
    try:
        from .guards import guard_ingest, guard_tags

        raw_ns = (req.namespace or os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE", "shared")).strip() or "shared"
        ns = validate_namespace(raw_ns)
        guard = guard_ingest(req.content, local_only=bool(req.local_only))
        if guard.rejected:
            reason_str = guard.reason or "Memory content rejected by ingestion guard."
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=400,
                content={
                    "status": "rejected",
                    "error": reason_str,
                    "detail": reason_str,
                },
            )
        try:
            clean_tags, tags_priv, tag_warnings = guard_tags(
                req.tags or [],
                local_only=bool(guard.local_only),
            )
        except ValueError as tag_err:
            reason_str = str(tag_err)
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=400,
                content={
                    "status": "rejected",
                    "error": reason_str,
                    "detail": reason_str,
                },
            )

        final_local = bool(guard.local_only or tags_priv)
        try:
            mesh = get_mesh(ns, req.agent_id, create=True)
            result = mesh.learn(
                content=guard.content,
                memory_type=req.memory_type or "fact",
                confidence=req.confidence if req.confidence is not None else 1.0,
                tags=clean_tags,
                local_only=final_local,
                ttl_days=req.ttl_days,
                secret_ref=req.secret_ref,
                vault_secrets=bool(req.vault_secrets),
                quarantined=bool(req.quarantined),
                trust_tier="agent",
            )
        except (NamespaceNotFoundError, sqlite3.OperationalError):
            evict_http_mesh_cache(ns)
            _evict_namespace_caches(ns)
            mesh = get_mesh(ns, req.agent_id, create=True)
            result = mesh.learn(
                content=guard.content,
                memory_type=req.memory_type or "fact",
                confidence=req.confidence if req.confidence is not None else 1.0,
                tags=clean_tags,
                local_only=final_local,
                ttl_days=req.ttl_days,
                secret_ref=req.secret_ref,
                vault_secrets=bool(req.vault_secrets),
                quarantined=bool(req.quarantined),
                trust_tier="agent",
            )
        all_warnings = tuple([*guard.warnings, *tag_warnings])
        if all_warnings:
            result["warnings"] = all_warnings
        return {"status": "ok", "result": result}
    except Exception as e:
        _raise_http_error(e, "/learn")


@app.post("/recall", dependencies=[Depends(verify_request_auth)])
def recall(req: RecallRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Retrieve memories relevant to a query using 5-stage hybrid search + peripheral snippets."""
    try:
        raw_ns = (req.namespace or os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE", "shared")).strip() or "shared"
        ns = validate_namespace(raw_ns)
        try:
            mesh = get_mesh(ns, req.agent_id, create=False)
        except NamespaceNotFoundError:
            return {
                "status": "ok",
                "namespace": ns,
                "namespace_exists": False,
                "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(ns),
                "results": [],
                "count": 0,
                "total_in_namespace": 0,
                "other_possible_memories": [],
                "filter_hint": None,
            }
        limit_n = req.n if req.n is not None else (req.count or 5)
        max_other = (req.max_other_possible or 5) if req.include_other_possible is not False else 0
        inc_priv = _resolve_http_include_private(req.include_private)
        bundle = mesh.recall_with_context(
            query=req.query,
            n=limit_n,
            min_similarity=req.min_similarity or 0.0,
            min_confidence=req.min_confidence or 0.0,
            include_stale=True if req.include_stale is None else req.include_stale,
            memory_type=req.memory_type,
            tags=req.tags,
            source_agent=req.source_agent,
            max_other_possible=max_other,
            include_private=inc_priv,
            include_quarantined=bool(req.include_quarantined),
        )
        return {
            "status": "ok",
            "namespace": ns,
            "namespace_exists": True,
            "results": bundle["results"],
            "count": bundle["count"],
            "total_in_namespace": bundle["total_in_namespace"],
            "other_possible_memories": bundle["other_possible_memories"],
            "filter_hint": bundle["filter_hint"],
        }
    except Exception as e:
        _raise_http_error(e, "/recall")


@app.post("/update", dependencies=[Depends(verify_request_auth)])
def update(req: UpdateRequest) -> Any:
    """Update an existing memory in-place by ID (supports clearing TTL via `clear_ttl=True`)."""
    try:
        from .guards import guard_ingest, guard_tags

        content = req.content
        local_only = req.local_only
        warnings: list[str] = []
        if content is not None:
            guard = guard_ingest(content, local_only=bool(req.local_only))
            if guard.rejected:
                reason_str = guard.reason or "Memory content rejected by ingestion guard."
                return JSONResponse(
                    status_code=400,
                    content={"status": "rejected", "error": reason_str, "detail": reason_str},
                )
            content = guard.content
            if guard.local_only:
                local_only = True
            warnings.extend(guard.warnings)

        clean_tags = req.tags
        if req.tags is not None:
            try:
                clean_tags, tags_priv, tag_warns = guard_tags(req.tags, local_only=bool(local_only))
            except ValueError as tag_err:
                reason_str = str(tag_err)
                return JSONResponse(
                    status_code=400,
                    content={"status": "rejected", "error": reason_str, "detail": reason_str},
                )
            if tags_priv:
                local_only = True
            warnings.extend(tag_warns)

        mesh = get_mesh(req.namespace, req.agent_id, create=False)
        result = mesh.update(
            memory_id=req.memory_id,
            content=content,
            memory_type=req.memory_type,
            confidence=req.confidence,
            tags=clean_tags,
            ttl_days=req.ttl_days,
            clear_ttl=bool(req.clear_ttl),
            local_only=local_only,
            secret_ref=req.secret_ref,
            quarantined=req.quarantined,
        )
        payload: dict[str, Any] = {"status": "ok", "result": result}
        if warnings:
            payload["warnings"] = tuple(warnings)
        return payload
    except Exception as e:
        _raise_http_error(e, "/update")

    """Approve a quarantined memory by ID (Finding S8)."""

        return {"status": "ok", "result": result}

@app.post("/forget", dependencies=[Depends(verify_request_auth)])
def forget(req: ForgetRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Delete a memory by ID[cite: 2]."""
    try:
        try:
            mesh = get_mesh(req.namespace, req.agent_id, create=False)
        except NamespaceNotFoundError:
            return {"status": "not_found", "deleted": None}
        deleted = mesh.forget(req.memory_id)
        return {
            "status": "ok" if deleted else "not_found",
            "deleted": req.memory_id if deleted else None,
        }
    except Exception as e:
        _raise_http_error(e, "/forget")


@app.delete("/memories/{memory_id}", dependencies=[Depends(verify_request_auth)])
def delete_memory_rest(
    memory_id: str,
    namespace: Optional[str] = None,
    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """RESTful DELETE endpoint returning 404 when the namespace or memory does not exist (Issue H5)[cite: 2]."""
    try:
        raw_ns = (namespace or os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE", "shared")).strip() or "shared"
        ns = validate_namespace(raw_ns)
        mesh = get_mesh(ns, agent_id, create=False)
        deleted = mesh.forget(memory_id)
        if not deleted:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=404,
                detail=f"Memory ID '{memory_id}' not found in namespace '{ns}'.",
            )
        return {"status": "deleted", "deleted": memory_id, "namespace": ns}
    except Exception as e:
        _raise_http_error(e, "DELETE /memories/{memory_id}")


@app.get("/inspect", dependencies=[Depends(verify_request_auth)])
def inspect(
    namespace: Optional[str] = None,
    limit: int = 50,
    memory_type: Optional[str] = None,
    tag: Optional[str] = None,
    include_private: Optional[bool] = None,
    include_quarantined: bool = True,
    quarantined_only: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """List memories in the namespace (excludes `local_only` private memories by default)[cite: 2]."""
    try:
        if limit < 1 or limit > 1000:
            raise ValueError("limit must be between 1 and 1000.")
        inc_priv = _resolve_http_include_private(include_private)
        raw_ns = (namespace or os.environ.get("MESH_NAMESPACE", "shared")).strip() or "shared"
        ns = validate_namespace(raw_ns)
        try:
            mesh = get_mesh(ns, create=False)
        except NamespaceNotFoundError:
            return {
                "status": "ok",
                "namespace": ns,
                "namespace_exists": False,
                "did_you_mean": suggest_did_you_mean_namespaces(ns),
                "memories": [],
                "count": 0,
            }
        result = mesh.inspect(
            limit=limit,
            memory_type=memory_type,
            tag=tag,
            include_private=inc_priv,
            include_quarantined=include_quarantined,
            quarantined_only=quarantined_only,
        )
        return {
            "status": "ok",
            "namespace": ns,
            "namespace_exists": True,
            "memories": result,
            "count": len(result),
        }
    except Exception as e:
        _raise_http_error(e, "/inspect")
