"""
mesh/io.py — High-speed export and import for Mesh memory stores.

Addresses Findings:
  - S1: Preserves and validates `secret_ref` locator metadata across export and import
  - S5: Supports authenticated encrypted exports (`encrypt=True`, `passphrase=...`) and
        automatic decryption on `import_namespace()` via `mesh.crypto`
  - S8: Excludes `quarantined=True` memories from exports by default (`include_quarantined=False`)
        and preserves `trust_tier` / `quarantined` metadata on import
  - S11: Applies both `guard_ingest()` (on content) and `guard_tags()` (on tags) to every
         imported memory
  - N6: Rejects symlinked `output_path` and `input_path` via `reject_symlink()` and `O_NOFOLLOW`
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import os
import re
import sqlite3
import sys
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

from .audit import (
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir,
    reject_symlink,
    secure_file_permissions,
    validate_namespace,
)
from .crypto import decrypt_export_payload, encrypt_export_payload
from .guards import guard_ingest, guard_tags
from .memory import (
    MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH,
    VALID_MEMORY_TYPES,
    _PENDING_REEMBED_LOCK,
    _PENDING_REEMBED_QUEUE,
    _embed_batch_with_tag,
    _hash_embed_384,
    _parse_tags,
)
from .passive import _normalize_memory_type
from .secrets import normalize_secret_ref
from .store import (
    EMBEDDING_DIM,
    MeshStore,
    _normalize_trust_tier,
    _parse_iso_utc,
    flush_all_telemetry,
    validate_memory_id,
)
from .version import VERSION, is_version_compatible

_KNOWN_FILE_EMBEDDING_MODELS = {"onnx-minilm-l6-v2", "hash-384"}
_VALID_MERGE_STRATEGIES = {"skip_existing", "overwrite"}
_EXPORT_FILE_CACHE: dict[tuple[str, int, int], Any] = {}


def _is_valid_file_vector(raw_emb: Any, file_model_tag: str) -> bool:
    """
    Validate a 384-dim vector from an export file (Phase 4.4):
      1. `file_model_tag` must be in `_KNOWN_FILE_EMBEDDING_MODELS` (`onnx-minilm-l6-v2` or `hash-384`).
      2. Must be a 384-element list of finite numbers.
      3. Must not be all zeros, and its L2 norm must be within `[0.5, 1.5]`.
      4. If `file_model_tag == "onnx-minilm-l6-v2"`, reject vectors carrying the `hash-384`
         trailing-zero watermark (`raw_emb[368:]` all zero).
    """
    if file_model_tag not in _KNOWN_FILE_EMBEDDING_MODELS:
        return False
    if not isinstance(raw_emb, list) or len(raw_emb) != EMBEDDING_DIM:
        return False
    sq_sum = 0.0
    non_zero = 0
    for x in raw_emb:
        if not isinstance(x, (int, float)) or isinstance(x, bool) or not math.isfinite(float(x)):
            return False
        fx = float(x)
        if abs(fx) > 1e-12:
            non_zero += 1
        sq_sum += fx * fx
    if non_zero == 0:
        return False
    norm = math.sqrt(sq_sum)
    if norm < 0.5 or norm > 1.5:
        return False
    if file_model_tag == "onnx-minilm-l6-v2":
        tail_zeros = sum(1 for x in raw_emb[368:] if abs(float(x)) <= 1e-12)
        if tail_zeros == 16:
            return False
    return True


def _load_existing_id_privacy_map(namespace: str) -> dict[str, bool]:
    """
    Read `{memory_id: is_local_only}` from an existing namespace DB without creating a new DB file.

    Fails *closed*: a genuine read failure (locked/corrupt DB) raises instead of returning
    `{}`, because an empty map would disable both the private-overwrite guard and the
    `skip_existing` check. Only an absent DB/table/column is treated as "nothing exists".
    """
    db_path = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve() / f"mesh_{namespace}.db"
    if not db_path.exists():
        return {}
    reject_symlink(db_path)
    try:
        from .store import _CACHE_LOCK, _VECTOR_CACHE

        with _CACHE_LOCK:
            cached = _VECTOR_CACHE.get((db_path, namespace))
            if cached is not None and cached.rows_by_id:
                return {mid: bool(rm.get("local_only")) for mid, rm in cached.rows_by_id.items()}
    except Exception:
        pass
    out: dict[str, bool] = {}
    try:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)
        try:
            columns = {str(row[1]) for row in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(memories)")}
            if not columns:
                return {}
            if "local_only" in columns:
                rows = conn.execute(
                    "SELECT id, local_only FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?",
                    (namespace,),
                ).fetchall()
            else:
                # Pre-privacy schema: `local_only` did not exist, so no row can be private.
                rows = [
                    (mid, 0)
                    for (mid,) in conn.execute(
                        "SELECT id FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?",
                        (namespace,),
                    ).fetchall()
                ]
            for mid, lo in rows:
                out[str(mid)] = bool(lo)
        finally:
            conn.close()
    except Exception as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Could not read existing namespace '{namespace}' before import; "
            f"refusing to merge without the private-memory guard ({exc})."
        ) from exc
    return out


def export_namespace(
    namespace: str = "shared",
    output_path: Optional[str] = None,
    include_embeddings: bool = True,
    encrypt: bool = False,
    passphrase: Optional[str] = None,
    include_quarantined: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Export all non-private (and by default non-quarantined) memories from an existing
    namespace to a JSON file, optionally encrypting the export payload (Findings S5, S8, N6).
    Raises `NamespaceNotFoundError` (a `ValueError` subclass) if the namespace does not exist.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    flush_all_telemetry()
    store = MeshStore(namespace=clean_ns, create=False)
    total_count = store.count()
    raw_exportable = store.get_exportable_memories(
        include_embeddings=include_embeddings,
        include_quarantined=include_quarantined,
    )

    exportable: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for mem in raw_exportable:
        if mem.get("local_only"):
            continue
        if not include_quarantined and mem.get("quarantined"):
            continue
        mid = mem["id"]
        entry: dict[str, Any] = {
            "id": mid,
            "content": mem["content"],
            "memory_type": mem.get("memory_type", "general"),
            "confidence": float(mem.get("confidence", 1.0)),
            "source_agent": mem.get("source_agent", "unknown"),
            "tags": _parse_tags(mem.get("tags", "[]")),
            "local_only": False,
            "trust_tier": _normalize_trust_tier(mem.get("trust_tier"), default="user"),
            "quarantined": bool(mem.get("quarantined", False)),
            "created_at": mem.get("created_at", ""),
            "ttl_days": mem.get("ttl_days"),
            "decay_start_at": mem.get("decay_start_at"),
            "access_count": max(0, int(mem.get("access_count") or 0)),
            "embedding_model": mem.get("embedding_model", "onnx-minilm-l6-v2"),
        }
        if mem.get("secret_ref"):
            entry["secret_ref"] = mem["secret_ref"]
        if include_embeddings:
            entry["embedding"] = mem.get("embedding")
        exportable.append(entry)

    exportable_work_items, exportable_work_events, total_work_items, skipped_private_work_items = (
        store.get_exportable_work_items(include_private=False)
    )

    skipped_count = max(0, total_count - len(exportable))
    export_data: dict[str, Any] = {
        "mesh_version": VERSION,
        "exported_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "namespace": clean_ns,
        "memory_count": len(exportable),
        "skipped_local_only": skipped_count,
        "memories": exportable,
        "work_items_count": len(exportable_work_items),
        "skipped_local_only_work_items": skipped_private_work_items,
        "work_items": exportable_work_items,
        "work_events": exportable_work_events,
    }

    payload_to_write = (
        encrypt_export_payload(export_data, passphrase=passphrase)
        if (encrypt or (passphrase is not None and passphrase.strip()))
        else export_data
    )

    if output_path:
        path = Path(output_path)
        # Finding N6: Refuse to write to a symlinked output file or symlinked parent directory
        reject_symlink(path)
        if path.parent.exists():
            reject_symlink(path.parent)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        content_str = json.dumps(payload_to_write, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        if os.name == "nt":
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(content_str)
        else:
            flags = os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC
            if hasattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW"):
                flags |= os.O_NOFOLLOW
            fd = os.open(str(path), flags, 0o600)
            with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(content_str)
        secure_file_permissions(path)
        if hasattr(sys.stderr, "isatty") and sys.stderr.isatty():
            from . import ui

            ui.success(
                f"Exported {ui.plural(len(exportable), 'memory', 'memories')}",
                ui.short_path(path),
                stream=sys.stderr,
            )
            if skipped_count > 0:
                ui.note(f"{ui.plural(skipped_count, 'private memory', 'private memories')} left out — private memories are never exported.")

    return payload_to_write


def import_namespace(
    input_path: str,
    target_namespace: Optional[str] = None,
    merge_strategy: str = "skip_existing",
    dry_run: bool = False,
    passphrase: Optional[str] = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Import memories from a JSON export file (plaintext or encrypted) into a validated namespace.

    Args:
        input_path: Path to the JSON export file.
        target_namespace: Override the namespace from the export file.
        merge_strategy: "skip_existing" or "overwrite" (raises ValueError otherwise).
        dry_run: If True, compute real preview stats (`would_import`, `would_skip`, `would_error`)
                 using the exact same validation without creating or writing to any database.
        passphrase: Optional passphrase to decrypt a `mesh_encrypted_export` file.

    Returns:
        Dict with import stats: `imported`, `skipped`, `errors`, `flagged`, `namespace`
        (plus `would_import`, `would_skip`, `would_error` on `dry_run=True`).
    """
    clean_strategy = (merge_strategy or "").strip().lower()
    if clean_strategy not in _VALID_MERGE_STRATEGIES:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Invalid merge_strategy '{merge_strategy}'. Must be one of {sorted(_VALID_MERGE_STRATEGIES)}."
        )

    path = Path(input_path).resolve()
    reject_symlink(path)
    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Export file not found: {input_path}")

    st = path.stat()
    cache_key = (str(path), int(st.st_mtime_ns), int(st.st_size))
    cached_payload = _EXPORT_FILE_CACHE.get(cache_key)
    if cached_payload is not None:
        data = cached_payload
    else:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            raw_data = json.load(f)

        if isinstance(raw_data, dict) and raw_data.get("mesh_encrypted_export"):
            data = decrypt_export_payload(raw_data, passphrase=passphrase)
        else:
            data = raw_data
        if len(_EXPORT_FILE_CACHE) >= 8:
            _EXPORT_FILE_CACHE.clear()
        _EXPORT_FILE_CACHE[cache_key] = data

    if not isinstance(data, dict) or "memories" not in data or not isinstance(data["memories"], list):
        raise ValueError("Invalid Mesh export file format.")

    file_version = data.get("mesh_version")
    if not is_version_compatible(file_version, VERSION):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Export file version '{file_version}' is incompatible with Mesh v{VERSION}."
        )

    raw_ns = target_namespace or data.get("namespace", "shared")
    namespace = validate_namespace(raw_ns)
    memories = data.get("memories", [])

    flush_all_telemetry()
    existing_privacy_map = _load_existing_id_privacy_map(namespace)
    seen_ids: set[str] = set(existing_privacy_map.keys())
    file_seen_ids: set[str] = set()

    stats: dict[str, Any] = {
        "imported": 0,
        "skipped": 0,
        "errors": 0,
        "flagged": 0,
        "namespace": namespace,
    }

    to_import: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], Optional[list[float]], Optional[str]]] = []
    texts_to_embed: list[str] = []
    embed_owner_indices: list[int] = []

    for mem in memories:
        if not isinstance(mem, dict):
            stats["errors"] += 1
            continue
        try:
            raw_id_early = str(mem.get("id") or "").strip()
            if raw_id_early:
                try:
                    cand_id_early = validate_memory_id(raw_id_early)
                    if cand_id_early in file_seen_ids:
                        stats["skipped"] += 1
                        continue
                    if clean_strategy == "skip_existing" and cand_id_early in seen_ids:
                        file_seen_ids.add(cand_id_early)
                        stats["skipped"] += 1
                        continue
                except ValueError:
                    pass

            raw_content = str(mem.get("content") or "").strip()
            if not raw_content or len(raw_content) > MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH:
                stats["errors"] += 1
                continue

            orig_local_only = bool(mem.get("local_only", False))
            guard_res = guard_ingest(raw_content, local_only=orig_local_only)
            if guard_res.rejected:
                stats["errors"] += 1
                continue

            # Finding S11: Validate tags through `guard_tags` on import
            raw_tags = _parse_tags(mem.get("tags", []))
            try:
                clean_tags, tags_priv, _ = guard_tags(raw_tags, local_only=guard_res.local_only)
            except ValueError:
                stats["errors"] += 1
                continue

            final_local = bool(guard_res.local_only or tags_priv)
            if final_local and not orig_local_only:
                stats["flagged"] += 1

            content = guard_res.content
            mem_copy = dict(mem)
            mem_copy["local_only"] = final_local
            mem_copy["content"] = content
            mem_copy["tags"] = clean_tags

            raw_id = str(mem_copy.get("id") or "").strip()
            mem_id: Optional[str] = None
            if raw_id:
                try:
                    mem_id = validate_memory_id(raw_id)
                except ValueError:
                    mem_id = None
            if mem_id is None:
                mem_id = str(uuid.uuid5(uuid.NAMESPACE_URL, f"mesh:{namespace}:{content}"))

            if mem_id in file_seen_ids:
                stats["skipped"] += 1
                continue
            file_seen_ids.add(mem_id)

            if clean_strategy == "skip_existing":
                if mem_id in seen_ids:
                    stats["skipped"] += 1
                    continue
                seen_ids.add(mem_id)
            elif clean_strategy == "overwrite":
                if existing_privacy_map.get(mem_id, False):
                    stats["skipped"] += 1
                    continue
                seen_ids.add(mem_id)

            file_model_tag = str(mem_copy.get("embedding_model") or "").strip().lower()
            raw_emb = mem_copy.get("embedding")
            valid_vec: Optional[list[float]] = None
            valid_tag: Optional[str] = None
            if raw_emb is not None and _is_valid_file_vector(raw_emb, file_model_tag):
                valid_vec = [float(x) for x in raw_emb]
                valid_tag = file_model_tag

            idx = len(to_import)
            to_import.append((mem_id, mem_copy, valid_vec, valid_tag))
            if valid_vec is None and not dry_run:
                embed_owner_indices.append(idx)
                texts_to_embed.append(content)
        except Exception as e:
            stats["errors"] += 1
            from . import ui

            ui.warn(f"Couldn't read one memory in the file: {e}")

    file_work_items = data.get("work_items") or []
    file_work_events = data.get("work_events") or []

    if dry_run:
        stats["would_import"] = len(to_import)
        stats["would_skip"] = stats["skipped"]
        stats["would_error"] = stats["errors"]
        stats["would_import_work_items"] = len(file_work_items)
        from . import ui

        ui.info(
            f"Dry run: would import {ui.plural(stats['would_import'], 'memory', 'memories')} into '{namespace}'",
            f"{stats['would_skip']} would be skipped  {ui.sym('dot')}  {stats['would_error']} errors  {ui.sym('dot')}  strategy: {clean_strategy}\nNothing was written. Re-run without --dry-run to apply.",
            stream=sys.stderr,
        )
        return stats

    store = MeshStore(namespace=namespace, create=True)

    computed_embeddings: dict[int, tuple[list[float], str]] = {}
    if texts_to_embed:
        try:
            vecs, actual_batch_tag = _embed_batch_with_tag(texts_to_embed)
            for item_idx, vec in zip(embed_owner_indices, vecs):
                if len(vec) == EMBEDDING_DIM and all(math.isfinite(x) for x in vec):
                    computed_embeddings[item_idx] = (vec, actual_batch_tag)
        except Exception as e:
            from . import ui

            ui.warn(f"Couldn't embed the imported memories ({e})", "They'll be imported with hash-384 fallback embeddings and fixed up later.")

        for item_idx in embed_owner_indices:
            if item_idx not in computed_embeddings:
                fallback_text = str(to_import[item_idx][1]["content"])
                computed_embeddings[item_idx] = (
                    _hash_embed_384(fallback_text).tolist(),
                    "hash-384",
                )

    now_iso = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    batch_items: list[tuple[str, str, list[float], dict[str, Any], bool]] = []
    hash_queued_items: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    for idx, (mem_id, mem, pre_vec, pre_tag) in enumerate(to_import):
        try:
            content = str(mem["content"]).strip()
            embedding: Optional[list[float]] = pre_vec
            emb_tag: Optional[str] = pre_tag
            if embedding is None or emb_tag is None:
                comp = computed_embeddings.get(idx)
                if comp is not None:
                    embedding, emb_tag = comp
            if not embedding or len(embedding) != EMBEDDING_DIM or not emb_tag:
                raise ValueError("Missing or invalid 384-dim embedding vector")

            raw_type = str(mem.get("memory_type", "general")).strip().lower()
            mem_type = (
                raw_type
                if raw_type in VALID_MEMORY_TYPES
                else _normalize_memory_type(raw_type)
            )
            tags = _parse_tags(mem.get("tags", []))

            raw_conf = mem.get("confidence", 1.0)
            try:
                conf_val = float(raw_conf)
                if not math.isfinite(conf_val):
                    conf_val = 1.0
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                conf_val = 1.0
            confidence = max(0.0, min(1.0, conf_val))

            raw_access = mem.get("access_count", 0)
            try:
                access_val = int(float(raw_access))
            except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError):
                access_val = 0
            access_count = max(0, access_val)

            raw_agent = str(mem.get("source_agent", "mesh-import") or "mesh-import")
            clean_agent = re.sub(r"[\x00-\x1f\x7f]+", "", raw_agent).strip()[:64] or "mesh-import"

            raw_created = mem.get("created_at")
            parsed_created = _parse_iso_utc(raw_created) if raw_created else None
            created_at = parsed_created.isoformat() if parsed_created else now_iso

            ttl_days = mem.get("ttl_days")
            if ttl_days is not None:
                try:
                    ttl_days = float(ttl_days)
                    if not math.isfinite(ttl_days) or ttl_days <= 0:
                        ttl_days = None
                except (ValueError, TypeError):
                    ttl_days = None

            raw_decay = mem.get("decay_start_at")
            parsed_decay = _parse_iso_utc(raw_decay) if raw_decay else None
            decay_start_at = (
                parsed_decay.isoformat()
                if parsed_decay
                else (created_at if ttl_days is not None else None)
            )

            trust_tier = _normalize_trust_tier(mem.get("trust_tier"), default="user")
            quarantined_bool = bool(mem.get("quarantined", False))
            norm_ref = normalize_secret_ref(mem.get("secret_ref"), default_namespace=namespace)

            metadata = {
                "memory_type": mem_type,
                "confidence": confidence,
                "source_agent": clean_agent,
                "tags": tags,
                "created_at": created_at,
                "updated_at": now_iso,
                "accessed_at": now_iso,
                "access_count": access_count,
                "ttl_days": ttl_days,
                "decay_start_at": decay_start_at,
                "embedding_model": emb_tag,
                "trust_tier": trust_tier,
                "quarantined": quarantined_bool,
                "secret_ref": norm_ref,
            }
            batch_items.append(
                (mem_id, content, embedding, metadata, bool(mem.get("local_only", False)))
            )
            if emb_tag == "hash-384":
                hash_queued_items.append((mem_id, content))
        except Exception as e:
            stats["errors"] += 1
            from . import ui

            if mem.get("local_only"):
                label = "a private memory"
            else:
                label = f'"{ui.truncate(str(mem.get("content", "")), 50)}"'
            ui.warn(f"Couldn't import {label}: {e}")

    if batch_items:
        try:
            store.upsert_batch(batch_items)
            stats["imported"] += len(batch_items)
        except Exception:
            for mem_id, content, embedding, metadata, local_only in batch_items:
                try:
                    store.upsert(
                        memory_id=mem_id,
                        content=content,
                        embedding=embedding,
                        metadata=metadata,
                        local_only=local_only,
                        embedding_model=str(metadata["embedding_model"]) if metadata.get("embedding_model") is not None else None,
                    )
                    stats["imported"] += 1
                except Exception as e:
                    stats["errors"] += 1
                    from . import ui

                    ui.warn(f"Couldn't import a memory: {e}")

    if hash_queued_items:
        with _PENDING_REEMBED_LOCK:
            for mid, text in hash_queued_items:
                _PENDING_REEMBED_QUEUE.put(namespace, mid, text)

    if file_work_items:
        if clean_strategy == "skip_existing" and not to_import:
            existing_wis = store.list_work_items(include_private=True, limit=10000)
            existing_wi_ids = {w["id"] for w in existing_wis}
            file_wi_ids = {w.get("id") for w in file_work_items if w.get("id")}
            if file_wi_ids and file_wi_ids.issubset(existing_wi_ids):
                stats["imported_work_items"] = 0
                stats["skipped_work_items"] = len(file_work_items)
                stats["imported_work_events"] = 0
            else:
                w_stats = store.import_work_items(file_work_items, file_work_events, merge_strategy=clean_strategy)
                stats["imported_work_items"] = w_stats["imported_items"]
                stats["skipped_work_items"] = w_stats["skipped_items"]
                stats["imported_work_events"] = w_stats["imported_events"]
                stats["errors"] += w_stats["errors"]
        else:
            w_stats = store.import_work_items(file_work_items, file_work_events, merge_strategy=clean_strategy)
            stats["imported_work_items"] = w_stats["imported_items"]
            stats["skipped_work_items"] = w_stats["skipped_items"]
            stats["imported_work_events"] = w_stats["imported_events"]
            stats["errors"] += w_stats["errors"]

    if hasattr(sys.stderr, "isatty") and sys.stderr.isatty():
        from . import ui

        _summary = f"{stats['skipped']} skipped (already present)  {ui.sym('dot')}  {stats['errors']} errors"
        if stats["errors"]:
            ui.warn(f"Imported {ui.plural(stats['imported'], 'memory', 'memories')} with problems", _summary)
        else:
            ui.success(f"Imported {ui.plural(stats['imported'], 'memory', 'memories')}", _summary, stream=sys.stderr)
    return stats
=== END FILE: mesh/io.py ===
=== FILE: mesh/namespaces.py (LINES: 700) ===
"""
mesh/namespaces.py — Namespace listing, deletion, renaming, and statistics.

Security & Integrity:
  - Validates all namespace identifiers against path traversal via `validate_namespace()`
  - Rejects symlinked `.db`, `-wal`, and `-shm` files across listing, stats, rename, and deletion (Finding N6)
  - Flushes buffered telemetry (`flush_all_telemetry`) before reading stats, deleting, or renaming
  - Uses `_compute_row_effective_confidence()` from `store.py` for a single unified staleness definition
  - Evicts all in-memory vector, connection, lock, audit, context, HTTP, MCP, and Dashboard caches on delete/rename
  - Cleans up advisory `.mesh_<ns>.lock` files and enforces atomic rollback on failed `rename_namespace()`
  - Enforces 0600 POSIX file permissions and Windows owner ACLs on renamed SQLite databases
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
import gc
import logging
import shutil
import sqlite3
import sys
import time
from contextlib import ExitStack
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Optional

from .audit import (
    _get_audit_db_path,
    ensure_secure_mesh_dir,
    evict_audit_cache,
    get_mesh_dir,
    reject_symlink,
    secure_file_permissions,
    validate_namespace,
)
from .store import (
    MeshStore,
    NamespaceNotFoundError,
    _CACHE_LOCK,
    _CONNECTION_POOL,
    _INITIALIZED_DBS,
    _TRANSIENT_EMBEDDINGS,
    _VECTOR_CACHE,
    _compute_row_effective_confidence,
    _evict_vector_cache_for_db_path,
    _utc_now,
    close_store_connection,
    evict_namespace_write_lock,
    flush_all_telemetry,
    namespace_write_lock,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _get_all_db_paths() -> list[Path]:
    """
    Return paths to all valid, non-symlinked namespace SQLite databases in ~/.mesh/.
    Finding N6: Rejects symlinks and verifies resolved parent confinement inside `mesh_dir`.
    """
    mesh_dir = get_mesh_dir()
    if not mesh_dir.exists() or mesh_dir.is_symlink():
        return []
    resolved_base = mesh_dir.resolve()
    results: list[Path] = []
    for p in mesh_dir.glob("mesh_*.db"):
        if p.name.endswith(("-wal", "-shm")):
            continue
        try:
            reject_symlink(p)
            if not p.is_file():
                continue
            if p.resolve().parent != resolved_base:
                continue
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            continue
        name = p.stem
        ns = name[5:] if name.startswith("mesh_") else name
        try:
            validate_namespace(ns)
            results.append(p)
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return results


def _namespace_from_db_path(path: Path) -> str:
    """Extract namespace name from db filename: mesh_shared.db -> shared"""
    name = path.stem
    return name[5:] if name.startswith("mesh_") else name


def _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir: Path, namespace: str) -> Path:
    """
    Resolve `mesh_<namespace>.db` inside `base_dir`, refusing symlinks on the DB or sidecars (Finding N6).
    """
    candidate = base_dir / f"mesh_{namespace}.db"
    reject_symlink(candidate)
    resolved = candidate.resolve()
    if resolved.parent != base_dir:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Refusing to operate on '{resolved}': it resolves outside the Mesh data directory."
        )
    return resolved


def _distinct_memory_namespaces(db_path: Path) -> list[str]:
    """Return the distinct `namespace` values stored in a database (empty on error)."""
    try:
        reject_symlink(db_path)
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)
        try:
            rows = conn.execute("SELECT DISTINCT namespace FROM memories").fetchall()
        finally:
            conn.close()
        return [str(r[0]) for r in rows if r[0] is not None]
    except (sqlite3.Error, ValueError):
        return []


def _reject_namespace_collision(db_path: Path, clean_ns: str, action: str) -> None:
    """
    Refuse a destructive operation when the database's rows do not belong to `clean_ns`.
    """
    names = _distinct_memory_namespaces(db_path)
    if not names:
        return
    variants = sorted(
        {
            n
            for n in names
            if n != clean_ns and n.lower() == clean_ns.lower()
        }
    )
    if variants or clean_ns not in names:
        found = variants[0] if variants else names[0]
        raise ValueError(
            f"Cannot {action} namespace '{clean_ns}': '{db_path.name}' contains rows for "
            f"{found!r}. This filesystem is case-insensitive, so the two names share one "
            f"database. Use the exact casing ({found!r}) or migrate both casings first."
        )


def _checkpoint_truncate_or_raise(conn: sqlite3.Connection, db_path: Path) -> None:
    """Checkpoint the WAL and fail loudly if another process prevents truncation."""
    try:
        row = conn.execute("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)").fetchone()
    except sqlite3.OperationalError as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Could not checkpoint '{db_path}' ({exc}). Stop the Mesh daemon and retry."
        ) from exc
    if row and int(row[0]) != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Could not checkpoint '{db_path}' because another process is using it. "
            "Stop the Mesh daemon and other agents, then retry."
        )


def _evict_namespace_caches(namespace: str, db_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
    """
    Clear all in-memory caches and close pooled SQLite connections for `namespace`
    across store, audit, context_builder, http_server, mcp_server, and dashboard.
    Only touches already-imported server modules in `sys.modules` to preserve lazy loading.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    if db_path is None:
        base_dir = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve()
        db_path = _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir, clean_ns)
    resolved_db = db_path.resolve()

    evict_namespace_write_lock(clean_ns)

    with _CACHE_LOCK:
        _evict_vector_cache_for_db_path(resolved_db)
        _evict_vector_cache_for_db_path(db_path)
        _INITIALIZED_DBS.pop(resolved_db, None)
        _INITIALIZED_DBS.pop(db_path, None)
        close_store_connection(resolved_db)
        close_store_connection(db_path)
        stale_keys = [k for k in _TRANSIENT_EMBEDDINGS if k[0] == clean_ns]
        for k in stale_keys:
            _TRANSIENT_EMBEDDINGS.pop(k, None)

    evict_audit_cache(clean_ns)

    ctx_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.context_builder")
    if ctx_mod is not None and hasattr(ctx_mod, "evict_context_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            ctx_mod.evict_context_mesh_cache(clean_ns)
        except Exception:
            pass

    http_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.http_server")
    if http_mod is not None and hasattr(http_mod, "evict_http_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            http_mod.evict_http_mesh_cache(clean_ns)
        except Exception:
            pass

    mcp_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.mcp_server")
    if mcp_mod is not None and hasattr(mcp_mod, "evict_mcp_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            mcp_mod.evict_mcp_mesh_cache(clean_ns)
        except Exception:
            pass

    dash_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.dashboard")
    if dash_mod is not None and hasattr(dash_mod, "evict_dashboard_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            dash_mod.evict_dashboard_mesh_cache(clean_ns)
        except Exception:
            pass

    cli_mod = sys.modules.get("mesh.cli")
    if cli_mod is not None and hasattr(cli_mod, "evict_cli_mesh_cache"):
        try:
            cli_mod.evict_cli_mesh_cache(clean_ns)
        except Exception:
            pass


def evict_lru_namespace(exclude_namespace: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Evict the least-recently-used namespace (other than `exclude_namespace`) across
    store, vector, audit, context_builder, HTTP, MCP, and dashboard caches.
    """
    excl = validate_namespace(exclude_namespace) if exclude_namespace else None
    victim_ns: Optional[str] = None
    victim_db: Optional[Path] = None
    with _CACHE_LOCK:
        for db_path, ns in _VECTOR_CACHE.keys():
            if ns != excl:
                victim_ns = ns
                victim_db = db_path
                break
        if victim_ns is None:
            for db_path in _CONNECTION_POOL.keys():
                cand_ns = _namespace_from_db_path(db_path)
                if cand_ns != excl:
                    victim_ns = cand_ns
                    victim_db = db_path
                    break
    if victim_ns is not None:
        _evict_namespace_caches(victim_ns, victim_db)
    return victim_ns


def _remove_sqlite_files(db_path: Path, strict: bool = False) -> bool:
    """Delete a SQLite database file along with any WAL or SHM sidecar files (rejecting symlinks, Finding N6)."""
    reject_symlink(db_path)
    all_removed = True
    for suffix in ("", "-wal", "-shm"):
        candidate = Path(f"{db_path}{suffix}")
        for attempt in range(5):
            if not candidate.exists() and not candidate.is_symlink():
                break
            try:
                candidate.unlink()
                break
            except OSError:
                if attempt == 0:
                    gc.collect()
                time.sleep(0.02 * (attempt + 1))
        if candidate.exists():
            all_removed = False
            if strict:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"Failed to delete SQLite file '{candidate}'. Another process may still hold the file open."
                )
    return all_removed


def list_namespaces() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    List all validated namespaces with their memory counts and last updated time.
    - Never silently drops a namespace whose DB file errors; includes it with `"error": "<message>"`.
    - Computes `last_updated` taking edits and accesses into account (`MAX(created_at, updated_at, accessed_at)`).
    """
    flush_all_telemetry()
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for db_path in _get_all_db_paths():
        namespace = _namespace_from_db_path(db_path)
        try:
            clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        cached_entry = None
        try:
            with _CACHE_LOCK:
                c = _VECTOR_CACHE.get((db_path, clean_ns))
                if c is not None and c.rows_by_id:
                    cached_entry = c
        except Exception:
            pass

        if cached_entry is not None:
            rows = cached_entry.rows_by_id
            total = len(rows)
            local_only = sum(1 for r in rows.values() if r.get("local_only"))
            last_ts = max(
                (
                    str(r.get("updated_at") or r.get("accessed_at") or r.get("created_at") or "")
                    for r in rows.values()
                ),
                default="never",
            )
            last_updated = last_ts if last_ts else "never"
            results.append(
                {
                    "namespace": clean_ns,
                    "memory_count": total,
                    "local_only_count": local_only,
                    "last_updated": last_updated,
                    "updated_at": last_updated,
                }
            )
            continue

        try:
            reject_symlink(db_path)
            store = MeshStore(namespace=clean_ns, create=False)
            with store._connect() as conn:
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                cols = {r[1] for r in cursor.execute("PRAGMA table_info(memories)").fetchall()}
                if not cols:
                    raise sqlite3.DatabaseError(f"Database '{db_path.name}' is missing 'memories' table")
                cursor.execute("SELECT DISTINCT namespace FROM memories")
                row_names = [str(r[0]) for r in cursor.fetchall() if r[0] is not None]
                cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM memories")
                total = int(cursor.fetchone()["count"] or 0)
                cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM memories WHERE local_only = 1")
                local_only = int(cursor.fetchone()["count"] or 0)
                if "updated_at" in cols:
                    cursor.execute(
                        """
                        SELECT MAX(
                            CASE
                                WHEN COALESCE(updated_at, '') >= COALESCE(created_at, '')
                                     AND COALESCE(updated_at, '') >= COALESCE(accessed_at, '')
                                THEN updated_at
                                WHEN COALESCE(accessed_at, '') >= COALESCE(created_at, '')
                                THEN accessed_at
                                ELSE created_at
                            END
                        ) as last
                        FROM memories
                        """,
                    )
                else:
                    cursor.execute(
                        """
                        SELECT MAX(
                            CASE
                                WHEN COALESCE(accessed_at, '') >= COALESCE(created_at, '')
                                THEN accessed_at
                                ELSE created_at
                            END
                        ) as last
                        FROM memories
                        """,
                    )
                row = cursor.fetchone()
                last_updated = row["last"] if row and row["last"] else "never"
                cursor.close()
            entry: dict[str, Any] = {
                "namespace": clean_ns,
                "memory_count": total,
                "local_only_count": local_only,
                "last_updated": last_updated,
                "updated_at": last_updated,
            }
            variants = sorted({n for n in row_names if n != clean_ns})
            if variants:
                entry["namespace_variants"] = variants
            results.append(entry)
        except Exception as exc:
            results.append(
                {
                    "namespace": clean_ns,
                    "memory_count": 0,
                    "local_only_count": 0,
                    "last_updated": "error",
                    "updated_at": "error",
                    "error": str(exc),
                }
            )
    results.sort(key=lambda x: x["namespace"])
    return results


def delete_namespace(namespace: str) -> int:
    """
    Delete a validated namespace and ALL its memories (plus its isolated audit trail DB and `.lock` file).
    Raises `NamespaceNotFoundError` (a `ValueError` subclass) if the namespace does not exist.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    flush_all_telemetry()
    base_dir = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve()
    db_path = _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir, clean_ns)
    audit_db_path = _get_audit_db_path(clean_ns)

    if not db_path.exists() and not audit_db_path.exists():
        evict_namespace_write_lock(clean_ns)
        raise NamespaceNotFoundError(f"Namespace '{namespace}' does not exist")

    count = 0
    _evict_namespace_caches(clean_ns, db_path)

    if db_path.exists():
        reject_symlink(db_path)
        _reject_namespace_collision(db_path, clean_ns, "delete")
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)
        try:
            try:
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,))
                row = cursor.fetchone()
                count = int(row[0]) if row else 0
                cursor.close()
            except sqlite3.Error as exc:
                logger.warning(
                    "Could not read memory count before deleting namespace '%s': %s",
                    clean_ns,
                    exc,
                )
                count = 0
            _checkpoint_truncate_or_raise(conn, db_path)
        finally:
            conn.close()

        _evict_namespace_caches(clean_ns, db_path)

    if audit_db_path.exists():
        reject_symlink(audit_db_path)
        conn_audit = sqlite3.connect(audit_db_path, timeout=5.0)
        try:
            _checkpoint_truncate_or_raise(conn_audit, audit_db_path)
        finally:
            conn_audit.close()
        evict_audit_cache(clean_ns)

    if audit_db_path.exists():
        _remove_sqlite_files(audit_db_path, strict=True)
    if db_path.exists():
        _remove_sqlite_files(db_path, strict=True)

    evict_namespace_write_lock(clean_ns)
    return count


def rename_namespace(old_name: str, new_name: str) -> int:
    """
    Rename a namespace safely with full validation, symlink rejection, cache eviction,
    and atomic rollback on failure.
    """
    clean_old = validate_namespace(old_name)
    clean_new = validate_namespace(new_name)
    if clean_old == clean_new:
        raise ValueError(f"Old and new namespace names are identical ('{clean_old}').")

    flush_all_telemetry()
    base_dir = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve()
    old_db = _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir, clean_old)
    new_db = _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir, clean_new)
    old_audit = _get_audit_db_path(clean_old)
    new_audit = _get_audit_db_path(clean_new)

    if not old_db.exists():
        raise NamespaceNotFoundError(f"Namespace '{old_name}' does not exist")
    if new_db.exists() or new_audit.exists():
        raise ValueError(f"Namespace '{new_name}' already exists. Delete it first.")
    _reject_namespace_collision(old_db, clean_old, "rename")

    _evict_namespace_caches(clean_old, old_db)
    _evict_namespace_caches(clean_new, new_db)

    count = 0
    lock_names = sorted({clean_old, clean_new})
    with ExitStack() as stack:
        for lock_ns in lock_names:
            stack.enter_context(namespace_write_lock(lock_ns, timeout=30.0))

        conn_old = sqlite3.connect(old_db, timeout=5.0)
        try:
            _checkpoint_truncate_or_raise(conn_old, old_db)
        finally:
            conn_old.close()

        try:
            reject_symlink(old_db)
            reject_symlink(new_db)
            shutil.copy2(old_db, new_db)
            secure_file_permissions(new_db)

            conn_new = sqlite3.connect(new_db, timeout=5.0)
            try:
                conn_new.execute("UPDATE memories SET namespace = ? WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_new, clean_old))
                conn_new.execute("UPDATE queries SET namespace = ? WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_new, clean_old))
                conn_new.execute(
                    "UPDATE contradictions SET namespace = ? WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_new, clean_old)
                )
                tables = {
                    r[0]
                    for r in conn_new.execute(
                        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"
                    ).fetchall()
                }
                if "mesh_meta" in tables:
                    conn_new.execute(
                        "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO mesh_meta (key, value) VALUES ('content_version', COALESCE((SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) + 1 FROM mesh_meta WHERE key = 'content_version'), '1'))"
                    )
                conn_new.commit()
                cur = conn_new.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_new,)
                )
                row = cur.fetchone()
                count = int(row[0]) if row else 0
                cur.close()
            finally:
                conn_new.close()
            secure_file_permissions(new_db)

            if old_audit.exists():
                reject_symlink(old_audit)
                reject_symlink(new_audit)
                conn_audit_old = sqlite3.connect(old_audit, timeout=5.0)
                try:
                    _checkpoint_truncate_or_raise(conn_audit_old, old_audit)
                finally:
                    conn_audit_old.close()

                shutil.copy2(old_audit, new_audit)
                secure_file_permissions(new_audit)
                conn_audit_new = sqlite3.connect(new_audit, timeout=5.0)
                try:
                    conn_audit_new.execute(
                        "UPDATE audit_log SET namespace = ? WHERE namespace = ?",
                        (clean_new, clean_old),
                    )
                    conn_audit_new.commit()
                finally:
                    conn_audit_new.close()
                secure_file_permissions(new_audit)
        except Exception:
            close_store_connection(new_db)
            _remove_sqlite_files(new_db)
            if new_audit.exists():
                _remove_sqlite_files(new_audit)
            raise

        removed = _remove_sqlite_files(old_db, strict=True)
        if old_audit.exists():
            removed = _remove_sqlite_files(old_audit, strict=True) and removed
        if not removed:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Renamed data to '{clean_new}', but could not fully remove the old namespace files. "
                "Close the Mesh daemon and retry."
            )

    evict_namespace_write_lock(clean_old)
    evict_namespace_write_lock(clean_new)
    return count


def namespace_stats(namespace: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Return detailed statistics for a validated namespace using the unified
    `_compute_row_effective_confidence()` staleness definition.
    Never creates a `.db` file if the namespace does not exist.
    """
    clean_ns = validate_namespace(namespace)
    flush_all_telemetry()
    base_dir = ensure_secure_mesh_dir().resolve()
    db_path = _safe_mesh_db_path(base_dir, clean_ns)
    if not db_path.exists():
        raise NamespaceNotFoundError(f"Namespace '{namespace}' does not exist")

    now = _utc_now()
    cached_entry = None
    try:
        with _CACHE_LOCK:
            c = _VECTOR_CACHE.get((db_path, clean_ns))
            if c is not None and c.rows_by_id:
                cached_entry = c
    except Exception:
        pass

    if cached_entry is not None:
        rows = list(cached_entry.rows_by_id.values())
        total = len(rows)
        local_only = sum(1 for r in rows if r.get("local_only"))
        cached_type_breakdown = Counter(str(r.get("memory_type", "fact")) for r in rows)
        avg_conf = (
            round(sum(float(r.get("confidence", 1.0)) for r in rows) / total, 4)
            if total > 0
            else 0.0
        )
        total_recalls = sum(int(r.get("access_count") or 0) for r in rows)
        created_times = [str(r.get("created_at") or "") for r in rows if r.get("created_at")]
        oldest = min(created_times) if created_times else "none"
        newest = max(created_times) if created_times else "none"
        stale = 0
        for r in rows:
            _, is_stale = _compute_row_effective_confidence(r, now=now)
            if is_stale:
                stale += 1

        total_searches = 0
        try:
            store = MeshStore(namespace=clean_ns, create=False)
            with store._connect() as conn_q:
                row_q = conn_q.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM queries WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,)).fetchone()
                total_searches = int(row_q[0] or 0) if row_q else 0
        except Exception:
            total_searches = 0

        return {
            "memory_count": total,
            "local_only_count": local_only,
            "exportable_count": total - local_only,
            "type_breakdown": dict(cached_type_breakdown),
            "avg_confidence": avg_conf,
            "total_recalls": total_recalls,
            "total_searches": total_searches,
            "oldest_memory": oldest,
            "newest_memory": newest,
            "stale_count": stale,
        }

    store = MeshStore(namespace=clean_ns, create=False)
    with store._connect() as conn:
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,))
        total = int(cursor.fetchone()["c"] or 0)

        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM memories WHERE namespace = ? AND local_only = 1",
            (clean_ns,),
        )
        local_only = int(cursor.fetchone()["c"] or 0)

        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT memory_type, COUNT(*) as c FROM memories WHERE namespace = ? GROUP BY memory_type",
            (clean_ns,),
        )
        type_rows = cursor.fetchall()
        type_breakdown = {r["memory_type"]: int(r["c"]) for r in type_rows}

        cursor.execute("SELECT AVG(confidence) as avg FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,))
        avg_conf_row = cursor.fetchone()
        avg_conf = float(avg_conf_row["avg"] or 0.0)

        cursor.execute(
            "SELECT SUM(access_count) as total FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,)
        )
        recall_row = cursor.fetchone()
        total_recalls = int(recall_row["total"] or 0)

        total_searches = 0
        try:
            cursor.execute(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM queries WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,)
            )
            total_searches = int(cursor.fetchone()["total"] or 0)
        except sqlite3.OperationalError:
            total_searches = 0

        cursor.execute("SELECT MIN(created_at) as oldest FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,))
        oldest = cursor.fetchone()["oldest"] or "none"

        cursor.execute("SELECT MAX(created_at) as newest FROM memories WHERE namespace = ?", (clean_ns,))
        newest = cursor.fetchone()["newest"] or "none"

        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT created_at, decay_start_at, ttl_days, confidence
            FROM memories
            WHERE namespace = ?
            """,
            (clean_ns,),
        )
        stale = 0
        for r in cursor.fetchall():
            _, is_stale = _compute_row_effective_confidence(dict(r), now=now)
            if is_stale:
                stale += 1

        cursor.close()

    return {
        "memory_count": total,
        "local_only_count": local_only,
        "exportable_count": total - local_only,
        "type_breakdown": type_breakdown,
        "avg_confidence": avg_conf,
        "total_recalls": total_recalls,
        "total_searches": total_searches,
        "oldest_memory": oldest,
        "newest_memory": newest,
        "stale_count": stale,
    }

=== FILE: mesh/version.py (LINES: 170) ===
"""
mesh/version.py — Package version metadata, PEP 440 version comparison, and opt-in update checker.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import sys
import threading
from typing import Optional

VERSION = "0.8.2"

_PEP440_RE = re.compile(
    r"^\s*v?(?P<release>\d+(?:\.\d+)*)"
    r"(?:[-._]?(?P<pre_type>a|alpha|b|beta|c|rc|pre|preview)[-._]?(?P<pre_num>\d+)?)?"
    r"(?:[-._]?(?P<post_type>post|rev|r)[-._]?(?P<post_num>\d+)?)?"
    r"(?:[-._]?(?P<dev_type>dev)[-._]?(?P<dev_num>\d+)?)?"
    r"(?:\+[A-Za-z0-9._-]+)?\s*$",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)

_PRE_TYPE_RANK = {
    "a": -3,
    "alpha": -3,
    "b": -2,
    "beta": -2,
    "c": -1,
    "rc": -1,
    "pre": -1,
    "preview": -1,
}

_UPDATE_CHECK_DONE = False
_UPDATE_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def parse_version_tuple(version_str: Optional[str]) -> tuple[int, ...]:
    """
    Parse a version string into a PEP 440-aware comparable integer tuple:
      `(major, minor, patch, pre_rank, pre_num, post_rank, post_num, dev_rank, dev_num)`

    Sorting order guarantees:
      `0.6.2.dev1 < 0.6.2a1 < 0.6.2b1 < 0.6.2rc1 < 0.6.2 < 0.6.2.post1 < 0.6.3.dev1 < 0.6.3 < 0.7.0 < 1.0.0`
    The first three elements are always `(major, minor, patch)`.
    """
    if not version_str or not isinstance(version_str, str):
        return (0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)

    m = _PEP440_RE.match(version_str)
    if not m:
        nums = [int(x) for x in re.findall(r"\d+", version_str)[:3]]
        while len(nums) < 3:
            nums.append(0)
        return (nums[0], nums[1], nums[2], 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)

    rel_parts = [int(x) for x in m.group("release").split(".")[:3]]
    while len(rel_parts) < 3:
        rel_parts.append(0)
    major, minor, patch = rel_parts[0], rel_parts[1], rel_parts[2]

    pre_type = (m.group("pre_type") or "").lower()
    pre_num_raw = m.group("pre_num")
    post_num_raw = m.group("post_num")
    # Detect the *matched optional group*, not the presence of letters anywhere
    # in the string. `re.search("r", ...)` also matched "rc", "pre", "+arch"
    # and local-version labels, silently turning releases into post-releases.
    has_post = m.group("post_type") is not None
    has_dev = m.group("dev_type") is not None
    dev_num_raw = m.group("dev_num")

    if pre_type:
        pre_rank = _PRE_TYPE_RANK.get(pre_type, -1)
        pre_num = int(pre_num_raw) if pre_num_raw is not None else 0
    elif has_dev and not has_post:
        # Pure `.devN` release sorts before alpha (`-3`)
        pre_rank = -4
        pre_num = 0
    else:
        pre_rank = 0
        pre_num = 0

    if has_post:
        post_rank = 1
        post_num = int(post_num_raw) if post_num_raw is not None else 0
    else:
        post_rank = 0
        post_num = 0

    if has_dev:
        dev_rank = -1
        dev_num = int(dev_num_raw) if dev_num_raw is not None else 0
    else:
        dev_rank = 0
        dev_num = 0

    return (major, minor, patch, pre_rank, pre_num, post_rank, post_num, dev_rank, dev_num)


def is_version_compatible(file_version: Optional[str], current_version: str = VERSION) -> bool:
    """
    Verify export schema compatibility using the release segment `(major, minor, patch)`.
    - Missing/empty file_version is rejected.
    - Major versions must match (`0.x` vs `0.y`, or `1.x` vs `1.y`).
    - During `0.x`, file minor version cannot exceed current minor version (e.g. `0.7.0` file on `0.6.3` fails).
    """
    if not file_version or not isinstance(file_version, str) or not file_version.strip():
        return False
    if not re.search(r"\d", file_version):
        return False
    f_maj, f_min, _ = parse_version_tuple(file_version)[:3]
    c_maj, c_min, _ = parse_version_tuple(current_version)[:3]
    if f_maj != c_maj:
        return False
    if c_maj == 0 and f_min > c_min:
        return False
    return True


def check_for_updates(force: bool = False, blocking: bool = False) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Strictly opt-in PyPI update check (`force=True` or `MESH_CHECK_UPDATES=1`).
    Never contacts PyPI when `MESH_NO_UPDATE_CHECK=1` or by default.
    """
    global _UPDATE_CHECK_DONE
    if os.environ.get("MESH_NO_UPDATE_CHECK", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes"):
        return None
    opt_in = force or os.environ.get("MESH_CHECK_UPDATES", "").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes")
    if not opt_in:
        return None
    with _UPDATE_LOCK:
        if _UPDATE_CHECK_DONE and not force:
            return None
        _UPDATE_CHECK_DONE = True

    def _fetch() -> Optional[str]:
        try:
            import urllib.request
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                "https://pypi.org/pypi/mesh-context-layer/json",
                headers={"Accept": "application/json", "User-Agent": f"mesh-context-layer/{VERSION}"},
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=2.0) as resp:
                if resp.status == 200:
                    data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                    latest = str(data.get("info", {}).get("version", "")).strip()
                    if latest and parse_version_tuple(latest) > parse_version_tuple(VERSION):
                        try:
                            from . import ui

                            ui.hint(
                                f"Update available: v{VERSION} → v{latest}. "
                                "Run `pip install -U mesh-context-layer`.",
                                stream=sys.stderr,
                            )
                        except Exception:
                            sys.stderr.write(
                                f"[Mesh] Update available: v{VERSION} -> v{latest} "
                                "(run `pip install -U mesh-context-layer`)\n"
                            )
                        return latest
        except Exception:
            pass
        return None

    if blocking:
        return _fetch()
    threading.Thread(target=_fetch, name="mesh-update-check", daemon=True).start()
    return None

=== FILE: scripts/check_docs.py (LINES: 444) ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
scripts/check_docs.py — Automated verification of Mesh documentation consistency.

Validates:
1. Version stamps in docs match package version (or known historical release notes).
2. Internal markdown relative links and anchors resolve.
3. CLI commands, environment variables, MCP tools, and HTTP routes in the code
   are present in DOCS.md.
4. CLI and MCP tool names mentioned in the docs exist in the code.
5. The README rules block matches its source-of-truth file byte-for-byte.
6. Field-test sections state the tested version.
7. docs/field-test/PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json matches the published files.

Exit code: 0 if valid, 1 if any check fails.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import re
import sys

# Ensure repository root is in sys.path
REPO_ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT))

from mesh.mcp_server import _MCP_TOOLS
from mesh.version import VERSION

CORE_DOCS = [
    REPO_ROOT / "README.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "DOCS.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "ARCHITECTURE.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "CHANGELOG.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "SECURITY.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "THREAT_MODEL.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "CONTRIBUTING.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "AGENTS.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "examples" / "README.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "docs" / "field-test" / "README.md",
    REPO_ROOT / "docs" / "AGENT_QA.md",
]

DOCS_FILE = REPO_ROOT / "DOCS.md"
RULES_FILE = REPO_ROOT / "mesh" / "skills" / "mesh-context-layer" / "rules_block.md"
PUBLIC_MANIFEST = REPO_ROOT / "docs" / "field-test" / "PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json"

# Variables read by Mesh or its documented integrations. Extracted from mesh/*.py
# at check time; this seed list covers names constructed dynamically or read by
# third-party clients.
EXTRA_ENV_VARS = {
    "NO_COLOR",
    "FORCE_COLOR",
    "XDG_CONFIG_HOME",
    "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY",
    "OPENAI_API_KEY",
    "GROQ_API_KEY",
}

# CLI command names registered in mesh.cli.
REGISTERED_CLI_COMMANDS = {
    "add",
    "ask",
    "inspect",
    "update",
    "forget",
    "approve",
    "conflicts",
    "patterns",
    "why",
    "status",
    "namespaces",
    "doctor",
    "brief",
    "task",
    "tasks",
    "log",
    "install",
    "uninstall",
    "setup",
    "shell",
    "i",
    "run",
    "export",
    "import",
    "encrypt-db",
    "server",
    "server-http",
    "http",
    "daemon",
    "context",
    "completion",
    "capture",
    "digest",
    "install-skills",
    "audit",
    "version",
    "help",
}


def get_mcp_tool_names() -> set[str]:
    return {tool.name for tool in _MCP_TOOLS}


def check_version_stamps(errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """Verify primary architecture and developer guide reference current version."""
    version_docs = [
        REPO_ROOT / "ARCHITECTURE.md",
        REPO_ROOT / "DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md",
    ]
    expected_stamp = f"v{VERSION}"
    for doc in version_docs:
        if not doc.exists():
            errors.append(f"Missing documentation file: {doc.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)}")
            continue
        content = doc.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if expected_stamp not in content and VERSION not in content:
            errors.append(
                f"[{doc.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)}] Missing current version stamp '{expected_stamp}' or '{VERSION}'."
            )
        else:
            print(f"  [OK] {doc.name} includes current version stamp ({VERSION}).")


def _github_anchor(heading: str) -> str:
    """Approximate GitHub's heading slug for in-repo anchors."""
    text = heading.strip().lower()
    text = re.sub(r"`|\*|_|~|\[|\]|\(|\)|<|>|!|\"|'|:|;|,|\.|\?|/|\\|\||&|=|\+", "", text)
    text = re.sub(r"[^\w\s-]", "", text, flags=re.UNICODE)
    return re.sub(r"\s+", "-", text).strip("-")


def _markdown_anchors(path: pathlib.Path) -> set[str]:
    content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    anchors: set[str] = set()
    in_fence = False
    for line in content.splitlines():
        if line.lstrip().startswith("```"):
            in_fence = not in_fence
            continue
        if in_fence:
            continue
        match = re.match(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)$", line)
        if match:
            anchors.add(_github_anchor(match.group(2)))
    return anchors


def check_markdown_links(errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """Verify relative file links and anchors in core docs resolve."""
    link_pattern = re.compile(r"\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)]+)\)")

    docs_to_check = list(CORE_DOCS)
    releases_dir = REPO_ROOT / "docs" / "releases"
    if releases_dir.exists():
        docs_to_check.extend(releases_dir.glob("*.md"))

    for doc in docs_to_check:
        if not doc.exists():
            continue
        content = doc.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        rel_doc = doc.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)

        for match in link_pattern.finditer(content):
            target = match.group(2).strip()
            # Skip external URLs, mailto, images, and file:// links.
            if target.startswith(("http://", "https://", "mailto:", "file://")):
                continue
            # Split the fragment and query before resolving the path.
            file_part, _, anchor = target.partition("#")
            file_part = file_part.split("?", 1)[0]
            if not file_part:
                target_path = doc
            else:
                target_path = (doc.parent / file_part).resolve()
                if not target_path.exists():
                    errors.append(f"[{rel_doc}] Broken relative link '{target}' -> target '{file_part}' not found.")
                    continue
            if anchor and target_path.suffix.lower() == ".md" and target_path.is_file():
                if anchor not in _markdown_anchors(target_path):
                    errors.append(f"[{rel_doc}] Broken anchor '{target}' -> no heading '{anchor}' in '{file_part or rel_doc}'.")


def _code_blob() -> str:
    return "\n".join(
        p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
        for p in (REPO_ROOT / "mesh").rglob("*.py")
    )


def check_code_surface_in_docs(errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """Every CLI command, env var, MCP tool, and HTTP route must appear in DOCS.md."""
    if not DOCS_FILE.exists():
        errors.append("Missing DOCS.md.")
        return
    docs = DOCS_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    blob = _code_blob()

    for command in sorted(REGISTERED_CLI_COMMANDS):
        pattern = re.compile(r"mesh\s+" + re.escape(command) + r"(?![\w-])")
        if not pattern.search(docs):
            errors.append(f"[DOCS.md] CLI command 'mesh {command}' is registered in code but not documented.")

    env_vars = set(re.findall(r"\bMESH_[A-Z0-9_]+\b", blob)) | EXTRA_ENV_VARS
    for name in sorted(env_vars):
        if name not in docs:
            errors.append(f"[DOCS.md] Environment variable '{name}' is read by code but not documented.")

    for tool in sorted(get_mcp_tool_names()):
        if tool not in docs:
            errors.append(f"[DOCS.md] MCP tool '{tool}' exists in code but is not documented.")

    http_source = (REPO_ROOT / "mesh" / "http_server.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    routes = set(re.findall(r"@app\.(?:get|post|put|delete|patch)\(\s*['\"]([^'\"]+)['\"]", http_source))
    for route in sorted(routes):
        if route not in docs:
            errors.append(f"[DOCS.md] HTTP route '{route}' exists in code but is not documented.")


def check_rules_block(errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """README's embedded rules block must equal the source file."""
    readme = (REPO_ROOT / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    marker = "### Rules block (verbatim source of truth)"
    start = readme.find(marker)
    if start == -1:
        errors.append("[README.md] Missing 'Rules block (verbatim source of truth)' section.")
        return
    fence_start = readme.find("```", start)
    if fence_start == -1:
        errors.append("[README.md] Rules block section has no fenced code block.")
        return
    fence_start = readme.find("\n", fence_start) + 1
    fence_end = readme.find("```", fence_start)
    if fence_end == -1:
        errors.append("[README.md] Rules block fenced code block is not closed.")
        return
    embedded = readme[fence_start:fence_end].strip()
    if not RULES_FILE.exists():
        errors.append("Missing rules_block.md source file.")
        return
    source = RULES_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    if embedded != source:
        errors.append(
            "[README.md] Embedded rules block differs from mesh/skills/mesh-context-layer/rules_block.md."
        )


def _sections(content: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """Split markdown into (heading, body) sections."""
    sections: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    heading = ""
    body: list[str] = []
    in_fence = False
    for line in content.splitlines():
        if line.lstrip().startswith("```"):
            in_fence = not in_fence
        if not in_fence and re.match(r"^#{1,6}\s+", line):
            sections.append((heading, "\n".join(body)))
            heading = line
            body = []
        else:
            body.append(line)
    sections.append((heading, "\n".join(body)))
    return sections


def check_field_test_version_labels(errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """Sections quoting field-test numbers must state the tested version."""
    readme = (REPO_ROOT / "README.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    number_pattern = re.compile(r"\b\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*(?:seconds?|s)\b|\b\d+\s*(?:out of|/)\s*\d+\b")
    for heading, body in _sections(readme):
        lower = (heading + "\n" + body).lower()
        if "field test" not in lower and "probe" not in lower:
            continue
        # Field tests were run on 0.8.1b2; accept either current VERSION or the historical 0.8.1b2 label
        has_version = VERSION in (heading + "\n" + body) or "0.8.1b2" in (heading + "\n" + body)
        if number_pattern.search(body) and not has_version:
            errors.append(
                f"[README.md] Field-test section '{heading.strip()[:60]}' quotes numbers without a verified tested version label."
            )


def check_public_manifest(errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """Verify the published field-test subset against its checksum manifest."""
    if not PUBLIC_MANIFEST.exists():
        errors.append("[docs/field-test] Missing PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json.")
        return
    try:
        manifest = json.loads(PUBLIC_MANIFEST.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        errors.append(f"[docs/field-test/PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json] Invalid JSON: {exc}")
        return
    root = PUBLIC_MANIFEST.parent
    listed: set[str] = set()
    for entry in manifest.get("files", []):
        rel = entry.get("path", "")
        listed.add(rel)
        target = root / rel
        if not target.is_file():
            errors.append(f"[docs/field-test/PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json] Listed file is missing: '{rel}'.")
            continue
        data = target.read_bytes()
        actual_size = len(data)
        actual_sha = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        data_lf = data.replace(b"\r\n", b"\n")
        data_crlf = data_lf.replace(b"\n", b"\r\n")
        valid_sizes = {actual_size, len(data_lf), len(data_crlf)}
        valid_shas = {
            actual_sha,
            hashlib.sha256(data_lf).hexdigest(),
            hashlib.sha256(data_crlf).hexdigest(),
        }

        expected_size = entry.get("size_bytes")
        expected_sha = entry.get("sha256")

        if expected_size not in valid_sizes:
            errors.append(f"[docs/field-test/PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json] Size mismatch for '{rel}'.")
        if expected_sha not in valid_shas:
            errors.append(f"[docs/field-test/PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json] SHA-256 mismatch for '{rel}'.")

    for path in root.rglob("*"):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        if any(part in ("__pycache__", ".DS_Store") for part in path.parts) or path.suffix == ".pyc":
            continue
        rel = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        if rel in ("MANIFEST.json", "PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json"):
            continue
        if rel not in listed:
            errors.append(f"[docs/field-test/PUBLIC_MANIFEST.json] Published file is not listed: '{rel}'.")


def check_cli_references(errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """Verify CLI subcommand mentions in docs match real commands."""
    cmd_pattern = re.compile(r"`mesh\s+([a-z][a-z0-9_-]*)(?:\s+[^`]+)?`")
    ignored_subcommands = {"--help", "--version", "-y", "--yes", "--dir", "--apply"}

    for doc in [
        REPO_ROOT / "README.md",
        REPO_ROOT / "DOCS.md",
        REPO_ROOT / "DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md",
        REPO_ROOT / "AGENTS.md",
        REPO_ROOT / "CONTRIBUTING.md",
    ]:
        if not doc.exists():
            continue
        content = doc.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        rel_doc = doc.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)

        for match in cmd_pattern.finditer(content):
            subcmd = match.group(1).lower()
            if subcmd.startswith("-") or subcmd in ignored_subcommands:
                continue
            if subcmd not in REGISTERED_CLI_COMMANDS:
                errors.append(f"[{rel_doc}] Documented CLI command 'mesh {subcmd}' is not in registered CLI commands.")


def check_mcp_tool_references(errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """Verify MCP tool mentions in docs match real MCP tools."""
    mcp_tools = get_mcp_tool_names()
    # Match `mesh_<name>` or `mesh_<name>(...)` or `mesh_<name>` tool
    tool_ref_pattern = re.compile(r"`(mesh_[a-z0-9_]+)(?:\([^`]*\))?`(?:\s+tool)?")
    ignored = {
        "mesh_context_layer",
        "mesh_mcp_command",
        "mesh_home",
        "mesh_namespace",
        "mesh_shared",
        "mesh_dash_session",
        "mesh_locked_namespace",
        "mesh_work",
        "mesh_instance",
        "mesh_default_namespace",
        "mesh_benchmark",
        "mesh_db",
    }

    for doc in CORE_DOCS:
        if not doc.exists():
            continue
        content = doc.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        rel_doc = doc.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)

        for match in tool_ref_pattern.finditer(content):
            tool_name = match.group(1)
            # Skip file references like `mesh_foo.py` or `mesh_foo.db`
            if any(ext in match.group(0) for ext in [".py", ".db", ".json", ".txt"]):
                continue
            if tool_name in ignored or tool_name in mcp_tools:
                continue
            # If followed by 'tool' or has parens, it was intended as a tool
            if "tool" in match.group(0).lower() or "(" in match.group(0):
                errors.append(f"[{rel_doc}] Unknown MCP tool referenced: '{tool_name}'.")


def check_no_fernet_references(errors: list[str]) -> None:
    """Verify that stale 'Fernet' references do not appear in user-facing documentation."""
    for doc in CORE_DOCS:
        if not doc.exists():
            continue
        content = doc.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        rel_doc = doc.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)
        for line_no, line in enumerate(content.splitlines(), start=1):
            if re.search(r"\bfernet\b", line, re.IGNORECASE) and "check_no_fernet" not in line:
                errors.append(
                    f"[{rel_doc}:{line_no}] Stale 'Fernet' reference found. "
                    "Mesh uses application-level AES-256-GCM envelope (with stdlib BLAKE2b/HMAC Encrypt-then-MAC fallback)."
                )


def main() -> int:
    print(f"Checking Mesh Documentation Consistency (Package version: v{VERSION})...")
    errors: list[str] = []

    check_version_stamps(errors)
    check_markdown_links(errors)
    check_cli_references(errors)
    check_mcp_tool_references(errors)
    check_code_surface_in_docs(errors)
    check_rules_block(errors)
    check_field_test_version_labels(errors)
    check_public_manifest(errors)
    check_no_fernet_references(errors)

    if errors:
        print(f"\n[FAIL] Documentation verification found {len(errors)} error(s):")
        for err in errors:
            print(f"::error::{err}")
            print(f"  - {err}")
        return 1

    print("\n[OK] All documentation consistency checks passed successfully!")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
=== END FILE: scripts/check_docs.py ===
